	< script > (function(root, factory) {
		'use strict';
		var api = factory();
		if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
		else root.ChloeVitals = api;
	}(typeof self !== 'undefined' ? self : this, function() {
		'use strict';
		var DEFAULT_CAP = 240;

		function createVitals(opts) {
			opts = opts || {};
			var host = opts.host || (typeof self !== 'undefined' ? self : global);
			var nowFn = opts.now || function() {
				return Date.now();
			};
			var cap = isFinite(opts.capacity) ? Math.max(2, Math.floor(opts.capacity)) : DEFAULT_CAP;
			var patched = false,
				orig = null,
				startedAt = 0;
			var liveI = {},
				liveT = {},
				liveR = {},
				nI = 0,
				nT = 0,
				nR = 0;
			var errors = 0,
				rejections = 0;

			function enable() {
				if (patched) return false;
				orig = {
					si: host.setInterval,
					ci: host.clearInterval,
					st: host.setTimeout,
					ct: host.clearTimeout,
					raf: host.requestAnimationFrame,
					caf: host.cancelAnimationFrame
				};
				host.setInterval = function() {
					var id = orig.si.apply(host, arguments);
					if (!liveI[id]) {
						liveI[id] = 1;
						nI++;
					}
					return id;
				};
				host.clearInterval = function(id) {
					if (liveI[id]) {
						delete liveI[id];
						nI--;
					}
					return orig.ci.call(host, id);
				};
				host.setTimeout = function(fn) {
					var id, args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
					var wrapped = typeof fn === 'function' ? function() {
						if (liveT[id]) {
							delete liveT[id];
							nT--;
						}
						return fn.apply(this, arguments);
					} : fn;
					id = orig.st.apply(host, [wrapped].concat(args));
					if (!liveT[id]) {
						liveT[id] = 1;
						nT++;
					}
					return id;
				};
				host.clearTimeout = function(id) {
					if (liveT[id]) {
						delete liveT[id];
						nT--;
					}
					return orig.ct.call(host, id);
				};
				if (orig.raf) {
					host.requestAnimationFrame = function(fn) {
						var id;
						var wrapped = typeof fn === 'function' ? function() {
							if (liveR[id]) {
								delete liveR[id];
								nR--;
							}
							return fn.apply(this, arguments);
						} : fn;
						id = orig.raf.call(host, wrapped);
						if (!liveR[id]) {
							liveR[id] = 1;
							nR++;
						}
						return id;
					};
					host.cancelAnimationFrame = function(id) {
						if (liveR[id]) {
							delete liveR[id];
							nR--;
						}
						return orig.caf.call(host, id);
					};
				}
				startedAt = nowFn();
				patched = true;
				return true;
			}

			function disable() {
				if (!patched) return false;
				host.setInterval = orig.si;
				host.clearInterval = orig.ci;
				host.setTimeout = orig.st;
				host.clearTimeout = orig.ct;
				if (orig.raf) {
					host.requestAnimationFrame = orig.raf;
					host.cancelAnimationFrame = orig.caf;
				}
				liveI = {};
				liveT = {};
				liveR = {};
				nI = nT = nR = 0;
				patched = false;
				orig = null;
				return true;
			}

			function census() {
				var s = {
					live_intervals: nI,
					live_timeouts: nT,
					live_rafs: nR,
					errors: errors,
					rejections: rejections,
					uptime_s: patched ? Math.round((nowFn() - startedAt) / 1000) : 0
				};
				try {
					if (host.document) s.dom_nodes = host.document.getElementsByTagName('*').length;
				} catch (e) {}
				try {
					var m = host.performance && host.performance.memory;
					if (m && isFinite(m.usedJSHeapSize)) s.heap_mb = Math.round(m.usedJSHeapSize / 1048576);
				} catch (e) {}
				return s;
			}

			function noteError() {
				errors++;
			}

			function noteRejection() {
				rejections++;
			}
			var ring = [];

			function sample(extra) {
				var row = {
						t: nowFn()
					},
					c = census(),
					k;
				for (k in c)
					if (c.hasOwnProperty(k)) row[k] = c[k];
				if (extra)
					for (k in extra)
						if (extra.hasOwnProperty(k) && typeof extra[k] === 'number' && isFinite(extra[k])) row[k] = extra[k];
				ring.push(row);
				while (ring.length > cap) ring.shift();
				return row;
			}

			function series() {
				return ring.slice();
			}

			function fieldsOf() {
				var seen = {},
					out = [],
					i, k;
				for (i = 0; i < ring.length; i++)
					for (k in ring[i])
						if (k !== 't' && typeof ring[i][k] === 'number' && !seen[k]) {
							seen[k] = 1;
							out.push(k);
						}
				return out.sort();
			}

			function suspects(o) {
				o = o || {};
				var minSamples = o.minSamples || 8,
					minDelta = o.minDelta || 1,
					minGrowthPct = o.minGrowthPct || 0;
				var skipFirst = o.skipFirst || 0,
					ignore = o.ignore || ['uptime_s'];
				var out = [],
					names = fieldsOf(),
					i, j, f, pts, everFell, first, last, delta, pct;
				for (i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
					f = names[i];
					if (ignore.indexOf(f) >= 0) continue;
					pts = [];
					for (j = 0; j < ring.length; j++)
						if (typeof ring[j][f] === 'number') pts.push(ring[j]);
					pts = pts.slice(skipFirst);
					if (pts.length < minSamples) continue;
					everFell = false;
					for (j = 1; j < pts.length; j++)
						if (pts[j][f] < pts[j - 1][f]) {
							everFell = true;
							break;
						}
					if (everFell) continue;
					first = pts[0][f];
					last = pts[pts.length - 1][f];
					delta = last - first;
					if (delta < minDelta) continue;
					pct = first > 0 ? (delta / first) * 100 : null;
					if (minGrowthPct > 0 && pct !== null && pct < minGrowthPct) continue;
					out.push({
						field: f,
						samples: pts.length,
						first: first,
						last: last,
						delta: delta,
						growthPct: pct === null ? null : Math.round(pct * 10) / 10,
						spanMs: pts[pts.length - 1].t - pts[0].t,
						monotonic: true
					});
				}
				out.sort(function(a, b) {
					return b.delta - a.delta;
				});
				return out;
			}
			var ledger = {};

			function record(r) {
				r = r || {};
				var who = String(r.source || '').trim();
				if (!who) return null;
				if (!ledger[who]) ledger[who] = {
					source: who,
					calls: 0,
					errors: 0,
					ms: 0,
					maxMs: 0,
					chars: 0,
					lastAt: 0
				};
				var e = ledger[who],
					ms = (isFinite(r.ms) && r.ms > 0) ? r.ms : 0;
				e.calls++;
				if (r.ok === false) e.errors++;
				e.ms += ms;
				if (ms > e.maxMs) e.maxMs = ms;
				e.chars += (isFinite(r.chars) && r.chars > 0) ? r.chars : 0;
				e.lastAt = nowFn();
				return e;
			}

			function usage() {
				var out = [],
					k, e;
				for (k in ledger)
					if (ledger.hasOwnProperty(k)) {
						e = ledger[k];
						out.push({
							source: e.source,
							calls: e.calls,
							errors: e.errors,
							ms: e.ms,
							maxMs: e.maxMs,
							chars: e.chars,
							lastAt: e.lastAt,
							avgMs: e.calls ? Math.round(e.ms / e.calls) : 0,
							errorRate: e.calls ? Math.round((e.errors / e.calls) * 1000) / 10 : 0
						});
					}
				out.sort(function(a, b) {
					return b.calls - a.calls;
				});
				return out;
			}

			function exportReport(o) {
				o = o || {};
				return {
					kind: 'chloe-vitals',
					at: nowFn(),
					enabled: patched,
					capacity: cap,
					samples: ring.length,
					census: census(),
					usage: usage(),
					suspects: suspects({
						skipFirst: o.skipFirst == null ? 2 : o.skipFirst,
						minGrowthPct: o.minGrowthPct == null ? 5 : o.minGrowthPct
					}),
					series: series()
				};
			}
			return {
				enable: enable,
				disable: disable,
				isPatched: function() {
					return patched;
				},
				census: census,
				noteError: noteError,
				noteRejection: noteRejection,
				sample: sample,
				series: series,
				fields: fieldsOf,
				suspects: suspects,
				record: record,
				usage: usage,
				exportReport: exportReport,
				capacity: function() {
					return cap;
				}
			};
		}
		return {
			createVitals: createVitals
		};
	}));
(function() {
	try {
		if (String(localStorage.getItem("chloe-vitals") || "0") !== "1") return;
		var v = window.ChloeVitals && window.ChloeVitals.createVitals ? window.ChloeVitals.createVitals({}) : null;
		if (!v) return;
		v.enable();
		window.chloeVitals = v;
		addEventListener("error", function() {
			try {
				v.noteError();
			} catch (e) {}
		});
		addEventListener("unhandledrejection", function() {
			try {
				v.noteRejection();
			} catch (e) {}
		});
		setInterval(function() {
			try {
				v.sample();
			} catch (e) {}
		}, 30000);
	} catch (e) {}
})();
(function() {
	if (!window.weld) window.weld = {};
})();
(function() {
	'use strict';
	if (!window.weld) window.weld = {};
	if (window.weld.markdown) return;

	function escapeHtml(s) {
		return String(s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
	}
	var SAFE_URL_RE = /^(?:https?:|mailto:|tel:|\/|#|\.\/|\.\.\/|[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+\.)/;
	var SAFE_IMG_URL_RE = /^(?:https?:|data:image\/(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp|svg\+xml);|\/|\.\/|\.\.\/)/;

	function safeUrl(url, forImg) {
		url = String(url).trim();
		var re = forImg ? SAFE_IMG_URL_RE : SAFE_URL_RE;
		return re.test(url) ? url : '#';
	}

	function applyFormatting(text) {
		text = text.replace(/\*\*([^*\n]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
		text = text.replace(/__([^_\n]+)__/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
		text = text.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*(?!\*)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
		text = text.replace(/(^|[^_])_([^_\n]+)_(?!_)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
		text = text.replace(/~~([^~\n]+)~~/g, '<del>$1</del>');
		return text;
	}

	function parseInline(text) {
		var slots = [];

		function slot(html) {
			slots.push(html);
			return '\u0001S' + (slots.length - 1) + '\u0001';
		}
		text = text.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, function(_, code) {
			return slot('<code>' + escapeHtml(code) + '</code>');
		});
		text = text.replace(/!\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)\s]+)(?:\s+"([^"]*)")?\)/g, function(_, alt, url, title) {
			var t = title ? ' title="' + escapeHtml(title) + '"' : '';
			return slot('<img src="' + safeUrl(url, true) + '" alt="' + escapeHtml(alt) + '"' + t + '>');
		});
		text = text.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)\s]+)(?:\s+"([^"]*)")?\)/g, function(_, label, url, title) {
			var t = title ? ' title="' + escapeHtml(title) + '"' : '';
			var inner = applyFormatting(escapeHtml(label));
			return slot('<a href="' + safeUrl(url) + '"' + t + ' target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + inner + '</a>');
		});
		text = text.replace(/\b(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+[^\s<.,;:?!])/g, function(url) {
			return slot('<a href="' + safeUrl(url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + escapeHtml(url) + '</a>');
		});
		text = escapeHtml(text);
		text = applyFormatting(text);
		text = text.replace(/\u0001S(\d+)\u0001/g, function(_, idx) {
			return slots[parseInt(idx, 10)];
		});
		return text;
	}

	function render(input, opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		if (typeof input !== 'string') input = String(input == null ? '' : input);
		input = input.replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n');
		var lines = input.split('\n');
		var out = [];
		var i = 0;
		while (i < lines.length) {
			var line = lines[i];
			var fence = line.match(/^(```|~~~)\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_-]*)\s*$/);
			if (fence) {
				var marker = fence[1];
				var lang = fence[2] || '';
				i++;
				var code = [];
				while (i < lines.length && lines[i].indexOf(marker) !== 0) {
					code.push(lines[i]);
					i++;
				}
				i++;
				var langAttr = lang ? ' class="language-' + escapeHtml(lang) + '"' : '';
				out.push('<pre><code' + langAttr + '>' + escapeHtml(code.join('\n')) + '</code></pre>');
				continue;
			}
			var hdr = line.match(/^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$/);
			if (hdr) {
				var n = hdr[1].length;
				out.push('<h' + n + '>' + parseInline(hdr[2]) + '</h' + n + '>');
				i++;
				continue;
			}
			if (/^\s*([-*_])\s*\1\s*\1[\s\1]*$/.test(line)) {
				out.push('<hr>');
				i++;
				continue;
			}
			if (line.charAt(0) === '>') {
				var quote = [];
				while (i < lines.length && lines[i].charAt(0) === '>') {
					quote.push(lines[i].replace(/^>\s?/, ''));
					i++;
				}
				out.push('<blockquote>' + render(quote.join('\n')) + '</blockquote>');
				continue;
			}
			if (/^\s*[-*+]\s+/.test(line)) {
				var items = [];
				while (i < lines.length && /^\s*[-*+]\s+/.test(lines[i])) {
					items.push(lines[i].replace(/^\s*[-*+]\s+/, ''));
					i++;
				}
				out.push('<ul>' + items.map(function(it) {
					return '<li>' + parseInline(it) + '</li>';
				}).join('') + '</ul>');
				continue;
			}
			if (/^\s*\d+\.\s+/.test(line)) {
				var oitems = [];
				while (i < lines.length && /^\s*\d+\.\s+/.test(lines[i])) {
					oitems.push(lines[i].replace(/^\s*\d+\.\s+/, ''));
					i++;
				}
				out.push('<ol>' + oitems.map(function(it) {
					return '<li>' + parseInline(it) + '</li>';
				}).join('') + '</ol>');
				continue;
			}
			if (line.trim() === '') {
				i++;
				continue;
			}
			var para = [line];
			i++;
			while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() !== '' && !/^(#{1,6}\s|```|~~~|>|\s*[-*+]\s|\s*\d+\.\s)/.test(lines[i])) {
				para.push(lines[i]);
				i++;
			}
			out.push('<p>' + parseInline(para.join(' ')) + '</p>');
		}
		return out.join('\n');
	}

	function renderInto(el, input, opts) {
		if (typeof el === 'string') el = document.querySelector(el);
		if (!el) throw new Error('weld.markdown.renderInto: element not found');
		el.innerHTML = render(input, opts);
		return el;
	}
	var ALLOWED_TAGS = {
		p: 1,
		br: 1,
		hr: 1,
		h1: 1,
		h2: 1,
		h3: 1,
		h4: 1,
		h5: 1,
		h6: 1,
		strong: 1,
		em: 1,
		del: 1,
		b: 1,
		i: 1,
		u: 1,
		s: 1,
		ul: 1,
		ol: 1,
		li: 1,
		blockquote: 1,
		pre: 1,
		code: 1,
		a: 1,
		img: 1,
		table: 1,
		thead: 1,
		tbody: 1,
		tr: 1,
		th: 1,
		td: 1,
		div: 1,
		span: 1
	};
	var ALLOWED_ATTRS = {
		a: {
			href: 1,
			title: 1,
			target: 1,
			rel: 1
		},
		img: {
			src: 1,
			alt: 1,
			title: 1,
			width: 1,
			height: 1
		},
		'*': {
			'class': 1
		}
	};

	function sanitize(html) {
		var tmp = document.createElement('template');
		tmp.innerHTML = String(html);
		walk(tmp.content);
		return tmp.innerHTML;
	}

	function walk(node) {
		var children = Array.prototype.slice.call(node.childNodes);
		for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i++) {
			var child = children[i];
			if (child.nodeType === 1) {
				var tag = child.tagName.toLowerCase();
				if (!ALLOWED_TAGS[tag]) {
					var text = document.createTextNode(child.textContent);
					child.parentNode.replaceChild(text, child);
					continue;
				}
				var attrs = Array.prototype.slice.call(child.attributes);
				for (var j = 0; j < attrs.length; j++) {
					var attr = attrs[j];
					var tagAllowed = ALLOWED_ATTRS[tag] && ALLOWED_ATTRS[tag][attr.name];
					var globalAllowed = ALLOWED_ATTRS['*'][attr.name];
					if (!tagAllowed && !globalAllowed) {
						child.removeAttribute(attr.name);
						continue;
					}
					if (attr.name === 'href') child.setAttribute('href', safeUrl(attr.value));
					if (attr.name === 'src') {
						var isImg = tag === 'img';
						child.setAttribute('src', safeUrl(attr.value, isImg));
					}
				}
				walk(child);
			}
		}
	}
	window.weld.markdown = {
		render: render,
		renderInto: renderInto,
		sanitize: sanitize,
		parseInline: parseInline,
		escapeHtml: escapeHtml,
		safeUrl: safeUrl,
		version: '1.0.0'
	};
})();
(function() {
	'use strict';
	if (!window.weld) window.weld = {};
	if (window.weld.banlist) return window.weld.banlist;
	var RATINGS = ['g', 'pg', 'pg13', 'r', 'x'];

	function normalize(s) {
		return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase();
	}

	function compile(phrase) {
		var p = normalize(phrase).trim();
		if (!p) return null;
		if (p.indexOf('*') === -1) {
			return {
				raw: p,
				test: function(text) {
					return text.indexOf(p) !== -1;
				}
			};
		}
		var esc = p.replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\*/g, '.*');
		var re = new RegExp(esc);
		return {
			raw: p,
			test: function(text) {
				return re.test(text);
			}
		};
	}

	function compileList(arr) {
		var out = [];
		(arr || []).forEach(function(p) {
			var c = compile(p);
			if (c) out.push(c);
		});
		return out;
	}

	function ratingRank(r) {
		var i = RATINGS.indexOf(normalize(r));
		return i === -1 ? RATINGS.length : i;
	}

	function create(config) {
		config = config || {};
		var users = {};
		(config.users || []).forEach(function(u) {
			users[normalize(u)] = true;
		});
		var phrases = compileList(config.phrases);
		var negPhrases = compileList(config.negativePhrases);
		var ceiling = config.rating != null ? normalize(config.rating) : 'x';

		function checkUser(name) {
			var ok = !users[normalize(name)];
			return {
				ok: ok,
				reason: ok ? null : 'banned user'
			};
		}

		function matchIn(text, list) {
			var t = normalize(text);
			for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++)
				if (list[i].test(t)) return list[i].raw;
			return null;
		}

		function check(text) {
			var m = matchIn(text, phrases);
			return {
				ok: !m,
				matched: m,
				reason: m ? ('banned phrase: ' + m) : null
			};
		}

		function checkPrompt(prompt, negative) {
			var reasons = [];
			var m1 = matchIn(prompt, phrases);
			if (m1) reasons.push('banned phrase: ' + m1);
			if (negative != null) {
				var m2 = matchIn(negative, negPhrases);
				if (m2) reasons.push('banned negative phrase: ' + m2);
			}
			return {
				ok: reasons.length === 0,
				reasons: reasons
			};
		}

		function allowedRating(itemRating) {
			return ratingRank(itemRating) <= ratingRank(ceiling);
		}

		function filter(items, getText) {
			getText = getText || function(x) {
				return x && (x.text || x.prompt || x);
			};
			return (items || []).filter(function(it) {
				if (it && it.user != null && !checkUser(it.user).ok) return false;
				if (it && it.rating != null && !allowedRating(it.rating)) return false;
				return check(getText(it)).ok;
			});
		}
		return {
			checkUser: checkUser,
			check: check,
			checkPrompt: checkPrompt,
			allowedRating: allowedRating,
			filter: filter,
			banUser: function(u) {
				users[normalize(u)] = true;
			},
			unbanUser: function(u) {
				delete users[normalize(u)];
			},
			addPhrase: function(p) {
				var c = compile(p);
				if (c) phrases.push(c);
			},
			addNegativePhrase: function(p) {
				var c = compile(p);
				if (c) negPhrases.push(c);
			},
			setRating: function(r) {
				ceiling = normalize(r);
			},
			rating: function() {
				return ceiling;
			},
			users: function() {
				return Object.keys(users);
			}
		};
	}
	window.weld.banlist = {
		create: create,
		ratings: RATINGS.slice(),
		version: '1.0.0'
	};
	return window.weld.banlist;
})();
(function() {
	'use strict';
	if (!window.weld) window.weld = {};
	if (window.weld.gallery) return;
	var DB_STORE = 'blobs';

	function uid() {
		return 'img_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
	}

	function createGallery(opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		var dbName = opts.dbName || 'weld-gallery';
		var maxItems = opts.maxItems || 200;
		var manifest = [];
		var dbInstance = null;
		var dbPromise = null;
		var container = null;
		var lightboxInstance = null;
		var sortMode = opts.sortMode || 'newest';
		var favOnly = false;
		var changeListeners = [];

		function openDb() {
			if (dbInstance) return Promise.resolve(dbInstance);
			if (dbPromise) return dbPromise;
			dbPromise = new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
				var req = indexedDB.open(dbName, 1);
				req.onupgradeneeded = function(e) {
					var db = e.target.result;
					if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(DB_STORE)) {
						db.createObjectStore(DB_STORE);
					}
					if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('meta')) {
						db.createObjectStore('meta');
					}
				};
				req.onsuccess = function(e) {
					dbInstance = e.target.result;
					resolve(dbInstance);
				};
				req.onerror = function(e) {
					dbPromise = null;
					reject(e.target.error);
				};
			});
			return dbPromise;
		}

		function idbOp(store, mode, fn) {
			return openDb().then(function(db) {
				return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
					var tx = db.transaction(store, mode);
					var s = tx.objectStore(store);
					var req = fn(s);
					if (req && req.onsuccess !== undefined) {
						req.onsuccess = function() {
							resolve(req.result);
						};
						req.onerror = function() {
							reject(req.error);
						};
					} else {
						tx.oncomplete = function() {
							resolve();
						};
						tx.onerror = function() {
							reject(tx.error);
						};
					}
				});
			});
		}

		function saveManifest() {
			return idbOp('meta', 'readwrite', function(s) {
				return s.put(manifest, 'manifest');
			});
		}

		function loadManifest() {
			return idbOp('meta', 'readonly', function(s) {
				return s.get('manifest');
			}).then(function(m) {
				manifest = m || [];
			});
		}

		function emit() {
			for (var i = 0; i < changeListeners.length; i++) {
				try {
					changeListeners[i](manifest);
				} catch (e) {}
			}
		}

		function store(blob, meta) {
			if (!(blob instanceof Blob)) {
				return Promise.reject(new Error('gallery.store: expected a Blob'));
			}
			meta = meta || {};
			var id = uid();
			var entry = {
				id: id,
				prompt: meta.prompt || '',
				seed: meta.seed != null ? meta.seed : null,
				resolution: meta.resolution || null,
				createdAt: Date.now(),
				favorited: false,
				metadata: meta.metadata || null
			};
			return idbOp(DB_STORE, 'readwrite', function(s) {
				return s.put(blob, id);
			}).then(function() {
				while (manifest.length >= maxItems) {
					var evictIdx = -1;
					for (var i = 0; i < manifest.length; i++) {
						if (!manifest[i].favorited) {
							evictIdx = i;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (evictIdx === -1) evictIdx = 0;
					var removed = manifest.splice(evictIdx, 1)[0];
					idbOp(DB_STORE, 'readwrite', function(s) {
						return s.delete(removed.id);
					}).catch(function() {});
				}
				manifest.push(entry);
				return saveManifest();
			}).then(function() {
				emit();
				return id;
			});
		}

		function remove(id) {
			manifest = manifest.filter(function(e) {
				return e.id !== id;
			});
			return Promise.all([
				idbOp(DB_STORE, 'readwrite', function(s) {
					return s.delete(id);
				}).catch(function() {}),
				saveManifest()
			]).then(function() {
				emit();
				return true;
			});
		}

		function removeMany(ids) {
			var idSet = {};
			for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) idSet[ids[i]] = true;
			manifest = manifest.filter(function(e) {
				return !idSet[e.id];
			});
			var chain = Promise.resolve();
			ids.forEach(function(id) {
				chain = chain.then(function() {
					return idbOp(DB_STORE, 'readwrite', function(s) {
						return s.delete(id);
					}).catch(function() {});
				});
			});
			return chain.then(saveManifest).then(function() {
				emit();
			});
		}

		function clear() {
			var ids = manifest.map(function(e) {
				return e.id;
			});
			manifest = [];
			return removeMany(ids).then(saveManifest).then(function() {
				emit();
			});
		}

		function toggleFavorite(id) {
			var entry = manifest.find(function(e) {
				return e.id === id;
			});
			if (!entry) return Promise.resolve(null);
			entry.favorited = !entry.favorited;
			return saveManifest().then(function() {
				emit();
				return entry.favorited;
			});
		}

		function getBlob(id) {
			return idbOp(DB_STORE, 'readonly', function(s) {
				return s.get(id);
			});
		}

		function getBlobUrl(id) {
			return getBlob(id).then(function(blob) {
				return blob ? URL.createObjectURL(blob) : null;
			});
		}

		function getView() {
			var view = manifest.slice();
			if (favOnly) view = view.filter(function(e) {
				return e.favorited;
			});
			switch (sortMode) {
				case 'oldest':
					view.sort(function(a, b) {
						return (a.createdAt || 0) - (b.createdAt || 0);
					});
					break;
				case 'favorites':
					view.sort(function(a, b) {
						if (a.favorited !== b.favorited) return a.favorited ? -1 : 1;
						return (b.createdAt || 0) - (a.createdAt || 0);
					});
					break;
				default:
					view.sort(function(a, b) {
						return (b.createdAt || 0) - (a.createdAt || 0);
					});
			}
			return view;
		}

		function setSort(mode) {
			sortMode = mode;
			emit();
		}

		function setFavOnly(v) {
			favOnly = !!v;
			emit();
		}

		function render(targetEl) {
			container = typeof targetEl === 'string' ? document.querySelector(targetEl) : targetEl;
			if (!container) return Promise.reject(new Error('gallery.render: container not found'));
			return loadManifest().then(function() {
				return renderView();
			});
		}

		function renderView() {
			if (!container) return Promise.resolve();
			var view = getView();
			container.innerHTML = '';
			if (view.length === 0) {
				container.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--weld-muted,#7d8590);font:13px var(--weld-mono,monospace);padding:40px;text-align:center;">No images yet.</div>';
				return Promise.resolve();
			}
			var promises = [];
			view.forEach(function(entry) {
				var cell = document.createElement('div');
				cell.className = 'w-gallery-item' + (entry.favorited ? ' is-fav' : '');
				cell.dataset.id = entry.id;
				cell.innerHTML = '<div class="w-gallery-loading"></div>';
				container.appendChild(cell);
				var p = getBlobUrl(entry.id).then(function(url) {
					if (!url) {
						cell.innerHTML = '<div class="w-gallery-empty">missing</div>';
						return;
					}
					var img = document.createElement('img');
					img.src = url;
					img.alt = entry.prompt || '';
					img.addEventListener('click', function() {
						openLightbox(entry.id);
					});
					cell.innerHTML = '';
					cell.appendChild(img);
					if (entry.seed != null) {
						var tag = document.createElement('div');
						tag.className = 'w-gallery-seed';
						tag.textContent = 'seed ' + entry.seed;
						cell.appendChild(tag);
					}
				});
				promises.push(p);
			});
			return Promise.all(promises);
		}

		function openLightbox(startId) {
			if (!window.weld || !window.weld.ui || !window.weld.ui.lightbox) return;
			if (!lightboxInstance) lightboxInstance = window.weld.ui.lightbox();
			var view = getView();
			var items = [];
			var startIdx = 0;
			view.forEach(function(entry, i) {
				items.push({
					src: '',
					id: entry.id,
					meta: entry.prompt || '',
					alt: entry.prompt || ''
				});
				if (entry.id === startId) startIdx = i;
			});
			var chain = Promise.resolve();
			items.forEach(function(item, idx) {
				chain = chain.then(function() {
					return getBlobUrl(item.id).then(function(url) {
						items[idx].src = url || '';
					});
				});
			});
			chain.then(function() {
				lightboxInstance.open(items, {
					index: startIdx
				});
			});
		}

		function downloadImage(id, filename) {
			return getBlob(id).then(function(blob) {
				if (!blob) return false;
				var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
				var a = document.createElement('a');
				a.href = url;
				a.download = filename || (id + '.jpg');
				document.body.appendChild(a);
				a.click();
				document.body.removeChild(a);
				setTimeout(function() {
					URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
				}, 1000);
				return true;
			});
		}

		function init() {
			return loadManifest();
		}

		function onChange(fn) {
			changeListeners.push(fn);
			return function() {
				var idx = changeListeners.indexOf(fn);
				if (idx >= 0) changeListeners.splice(idx, 1);
			};
		}
		return {
			init: init,
			store: store,
			remove: remove,
			removeMany: removeMany,
			clear: clear,
			toggleFavorite: toggleFavorite,
			getBlob: getBlob,
			getBlobUrl: getBlobUrl,
			getView: getView,
			getManifest: function() {
				return manifest.slice();
			},
			setSort: setSort,
			setFavOnly: setFavOnly,
			render: render,
			renderView: renderView,
			downloadImage: downloadImage,
			onChange: onChange,
			count: function() {
				return manifest.length;
			},
			maxItems: function() {
				return maxItems;
			}
		};
	}

	function injectGalleryCss() {
		if (window.__weldGalleryCss) return;
		window.__weldGalleryCss = true;
		var s = document.createElement('style');
		s.textContent = '.w-gallery{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(160px,1fr));gap:10px;}' + '.w-gallery-item{aspect-ratio:1;background:var(--weld-faint,#21262d);' + 'border:1px solid var(--weld-border,rgba(255,255,255,0.10));border-radius:6px;' + 'overflow:hidden;position:relative;cursor:pointer;}' + '.w-gallery-item img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;' + 'opacity:0.9;transition:opacity 0.15s,transform 0.2s;}' + '.w-gallery-item:hover img{opacity:1;transform:scale(1.03);}' + '.w-gallery-item.is-fav{border-color:rgba(210,153,34,0.4);}' + '.w-gallery-seed{position:absolute;bottom:4px;right:4px;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);' + 'color:rgba(255,255,255,0.6);font:10px var(--weld-mono,monospace);' + 'padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;opacity:0;transition:opacity 0.15s;}' + '.w-gallery-item:hover .w-gallery-seed{opacity:0.85;}' + '.w-gallery-loading{width:100%;height:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;' + 'justify-content:center;color:var(--weld-muted,#7d8590);font-size:12px;}' + '.w-gallery-loading::after{content:"loading";font:11px var(--weld-mono,monospace);}' + '.w-gallery-empty{width:100%;height:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;' + 'justify-content:center;color:var(--weld-muted,#7d8590);font:11px var(--weld-mono,monospace);}';
		document.head.appendChild(s);
	}
	window.weld.gallery = {
		create: function(opts) {
			injectGalleryCss();
			return createGallery(opts);
		},
		version: '1.0.0'
	};
})();
(function() {
	'use strict';
	if (!window.weld) window.weld = {};
	if (window.weld.toast) return;

	function injectCss() {
		if (window.__weldToastCss) return;
		window.__weldToastCss = true;
		var s = document.createElement('style');
		s.textContent = '.weld-toast-host{position:fixed;z-index:9999;display:flex;flex-direction:column;' + 'gap:8px;pointer-events:none;font:14px system-ui,sans-serif;}' + '.weld-toast-host.top-right{top:20px;right:20px;align-items:flex-end;}' + '.weld-toast-host.top-left{top:20px;left:20px;align-items:flex-start;}' + '.weld-toast-host.top-center{top:20px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);align-items:center;}' + '.weld-toast-host.bottom-right{bottom:20px;right:20px;align-items:flex-end;}' + '.weld-toast-host.bottom-left{bottom:20px;left:20px;align-items:flex-start;}' + '.weld-toast-host.bottom-center{bottom:20px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);align-items:center;}' + '.weld-toast{pointer-events:auto;min-width:240px;max-width:360px;' + 'padding:12px 16px;background:var(--weld-surface,#161b22);' + 'border:1px solid var(--weld-border,rgba(255,255,255,0.10));border-radius:6px;' + 'box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);color:var(--weld-ink,#e6edf3);' + 'display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;line-height:1.4;' + 'transform:translateX(20px);opacity:0;transition:transform 220ms,opacity 220ms;}' + '.weld-toast.shown{transform:translateX(0);opacity:1;}' + '.weld-toast .weld-toast-icon{flex:0 0 18px;height:18px;border-radius:50%;margin-top:1px;' + 'display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font:600 11px monospace;color:#fff;}' + '.weld-toast.info  .weld-toast-icon{background:#4493f8;}' + '.weld-toast.success .weld-toast-icon{background:#3fb950;}' + '.weld-toast.warn  .weld-toast-icon{background:#d29922;}' + '.weld-toast.error .weld-toast-icon{background:#f85149;}' + '.weld-toast .weld-toast-msg{flex:1;font-size:13px;word-wrap:break-word;}' + '.weld-toast .weld-toast-close{cursor:pointer;color:var(--weld-muted,#7d8590);' + 'font-size:16px;line-height:1;background:none;border:none;padding:0;margin-left:6px;}' + '.weld-toast .weld-toast-close:hover{color:var(--weld-ink,#e6edf3);}' + '.weld-toast-backdrop{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5);z-index:9998;' + 'display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font:14px system-ui,sans-serif;' + 'opacity:0;transition:opacity 180ms;}' + '.weld-toast-backdrop.shown{opacity:1;}' + '.weld-toast-modal{background:var(--weld-surface,#161b22);' + 'border:1px solid var(--weld-border,rgba(255,255,255,0.10));border-radius:8px;' + 'padding:24px;min-width:320px;max-width:480px;' + 'color:var(--weld-ink,#e6edf3);box-shadow:0 16px 64px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);' + 'transform:scale(0.96);transition:transform 180ms;}' + '.weld-toast-backdrop.shown .weld-toast-modal{transform:scale(1);}' + '.weld-toast-modal h4{font-size:15px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:10px;}' + '.weld-toast-modal p{font-size:13px;color:var(--weld-ink,#e6edf3);margin-bottom:14px;line-height:1.5;}' + '.weld-toast-modal input,.weld-toast-modal textarea{width:100%;padding:8px 10px;' + 'background:var(--weld-faint,#21262d);' + 'border:1px solid var(--weld-border,rgba(255,255,255,0.10));border-radius:4px;' + 'color:var(--weld-ink,#e6edf3);font:13px system-ui,sans-serif;margin-bottom:14px;}' + '.weld-toast-modal textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:80px;font-family:inherit;}' + '.weld-toast-actions{display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end;}' + '.weld-toast-btn{padding:6px 14px;border-radius:4px;font:600 13px system-ui,sans-serif;' + 'cursor:pointer;border:1px solid transparent;}' + '.weld-toast-btn.primary{background:#4493f8;color:#fff;}' + '.weld-toast-btn.primary:hover{background:#3a85e0;}' + '.weld-toast-btn.danger{background:#f85149;color:#fff;}' + '.weld-toast-btn.danger:hover{background:#e0463f;}' + '.weld-toast-btn.ghost{background:transparent;color:var(--weld-ink,#e6edf3);' + 'border-color:var(--weld-border,rgba(255,255,255,0.10));}' + '.weld-toast-btn.ghost:hover{background:var(--weld-faint,#21262d);}';
		document.head.appendChild(s);
	}
	var config = {
		position: 'top-right',
		duration: 4000,
		max: 5
	};
	var hosts = {};
	var activeToasts = [];

	function getHost(position) {
		if (hosts[position]) return hosts[position];
		var h = document.createElement('div');
		h.className = 'weld-toast-host ' + position;
		document.body.appendChild(h);
		hosts[position] = h;
		return h;
	}

	function configure(opts) {
		if (!opts) return;
		if (opts.position) config.position = opts.position;
		if (opts.duration != null) config.duration = opts.duration;
		if (opts.max != null) config.max = opts.max;
	}
	var ICONS = {
		info: 'i',
		success: '\u2713',
		warn: '!',
		error: 'x'
	};

	function show(message, opts) {
		injectCss();
		opts = opts || {};
		var type = opts.type || 'info';
		var duration = opts.duration != null ? opts.duration : config.duration;
		var position = opts.position || config.position;
		var dismissible = opts.dismissible !== false;
		var host = getHost(position);
		var toast = document.createElement('div');
		toast.className = 'weld-toast ' + type;
		var icon = document.createElement('div');
		icon.className = 'weld-toast-icon';
		icon.textContent = ICONS[type] || ICONS.info;
		toast.appendChild(icon);
		var msg = document.createElement('div');
		msg.className = 'weld-toast-msg';
		msg.textContent = String(message);
		toast.appendChild(msg);
		if (dismissible) {
			var close = document.createElement('button');
			close.className = 'weld-toast-close';
			close.textContent = '\u00d7';
			close.setAttribute('aria-label', 'close');
			close.addEventListener('click', function() {
				dismiss(handle);
			});
			toast.appendChild(close);
		}
		while (activeToasts.length >= config.max) {
			var oldest = activeToasts.shift();
			if (oldest) dismiss(oldest, true);
		}
		host.appendChild(toast);
		setTimeout(function() {
			toast.classList.add('shown');
		}, 10);
		var timer = null;
		var handle = {
			toast: toast,
			host: host,
			dismissed: false,
			_t: null
		};
		activeToasts.push(handle);
		if (duration > 0) {
			handle._t = setTimeout(function() {
				dismiss(handle);
			}, duration);
		}
		return handle;
	}

	function dismiss(handle, immediate) {
		if (!handle || handle.dismissed) return;
		handle.dismissed = true;
		if (handle._t) clearTimeout(handle._t);
		var idx = activeToasts.indexOf(handle);
		if (idx !== -1) activeToasts.splice(idx, 1);
		if (immediate) {
			if (handle.toast.parentNode) handle.toast.parentNode.removeChild(handle.toast);
			return;
		}
		handle.toast.classList.remove('shown');
		setTimeout(function() {
			if (handle.toast.parentNode) handle.toast.parentNode.removeChild(handle.toast);
		}, 240);
	}

	function clearAll() {
		while (activeToasts.length) {
			dismiss(activeToasts[0], true);
		}
	}

	function buildBackdrop() {
		injectCss();
		var bg = document.createElement('div');
		bg.className = 'weld-toast-backdrop';
		document.body.appendChild(bg);
		setTimeout(function() {
			bg.classList.add('shown');
		}, 10);
		return bg;
	}

	function teardown(bg) {
		bg.classList.remove('shown');
		setTimeout(function() {
			if (bg.parentNode) bg.parentNode.removeChild(bg);
		}, 200);
	}

	function confirmDialog(opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		var title = opts.title || 'Confirm';
		var message = opts.message || 'Are you sure?';
		var okLabel = opts.okLabel || 'OK';
		var cancelLabel = opts.cancelLabel || 'Cancel';
		var danger = opts.danger === true;
		return new Promise(function(resolve) {
			var bg = buildBackdrop();
			var modal = document.createElement('div');
			modal.className = 'weld-toast-modal';
			var h = document.createElement('h4');
			h.textContent = title;
			modal.appendChild(h);
			var p = document.createElement('p');
			p.textContent = message;
			modal.appendChild(p);
			var actions = document.createElement('div');
			actions.className = 'weld-toast-actions';
			var cancel = document.createElement('button');
			cancel.className = 'weld-toast-btn ghost';
			cancel.textContent = cancelLabel;
			cancel.addEventListener('click', function() {
				teardown(bg);
				resolve(false);
			});
			actions.appendChild(cancel);
			var ok = document.createElement('button');
			ok.className = 'weld-toast-btn ' + (danger ? 'danger' : 'primary');
			ok.textContent = okLabel;
			ok.addEventListener('click', function() {
				teardown(bg);
				resolve(true);
			});
			actions.appendChild(ok);
			modal.appendChild(actions);
			bg.appendChild(modal);
			setTimeout(function() {
				ok.focus();
			}, 100);
		});
	}

	function promptDialog(opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		var title = opts.title || 'Prompt';
		var message = opts.message || '';
		var placeholder = opts.placeholder || '';
		var defaultValue = opts.defaultValue || '';
		var multiline = opts.multiline === true;
		var okLabel = opts.okLabel || 'OK';
		var cancelLabel = opts.cancelLabel || 'Cancel';
		return new Promise(function(resolve) {
			var bg = buildBackdrop();
			var modal = document.createElement('div');
			modal.className = 'weld-toast-modal';
			var h = document.createElement('h4');
			h.textContent = title;
			modal.appendChild(h);
			if (message) {
				var p = document.createElement('p');
				p.textContent = message;
				modal.appendChild(p);
			}
			var input;
			if (multiline) {
				input = document.createElement('textarea');
			} else {
				input = document.createElement('input');
				input.type = 'text';
			}
			input.placeholder = placeholder;
			input.value = defaultValue;
			modal.appendChild(input);
			var actions = document.createElement('div');
			actions.className = 'weld-toast-actions';
			var cancel = document.createElement('button');
			cancel.className = 'weld-toast-btn ghost';
			cancel.textContent = cancelLabel;
			cancel.addEventListener('click', function() {
				teardown(bg);
				resolve(null);
			});
			actions.appendChild(cancel);
			var ok = document.createElement('button');
			ok.className = 'weld-toast-btn primary';
			ok.textContent = okLabel;
			ok.addEventListener('click', function() {
				teardown(bg);
				resolve(input.value);
			});
			actions.appendChild(ok);
			modal.appendChild(actions);
			bg.appendChild(modal);
			setTimeout(function() {
				input.focus();
				input.select();
			}, 100);
			if (!multiline) {
				input.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
					if (e.key === 'Enter') {
						teardown(bg);
						resolve(input.value);
					}
					if (e.key === 'Escape') {
						teardown(bg);
						resolve(null);
					}
				});
			}
		});
	}
	window.weld.toast = {
		show: show,
		info: function(m, o) {
			o = o || {};
			o.type = 'info';
			return show(m, o);
		},
		success: function(m, o) {
			o = o || {};
			o.type = 'success';
			return show(m, o);
		},
		warn: function(m, o) {
			o = o || {};
			o.type = 'warn';
			return show(m, o);
		},
		error: function(m, o) {
			o = o || {};
			o.type = 'error';
			return show(m, o);
		},
		dismiss: dismiss,
		clear: clearAll,
		configure: configure,
		confirm: confirmDialog,
		prompt: promptDialog,
		version: '1.0.0'
	};
})();
(function() {
	'use strict';
	if (!window.weld) window.weld = {};
	if (window.weld.comments) return window.weld.comments;
	var EMBED_BASE = 'https:
	var STYLE_ID = 'weld-comments-style';
	var overlay = null;

	function hash(text) {
		text = String(text == null ? '' : text);
		var h = 5381;
		for (var i = 0; i < text.length; i++) h = ((h << 5) + h + text.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
		return Math.abs(h).toString(36);
	}

	function channel(key, prefix) {
		prefix = prefix || defaultApp();
		return prefix + '-' + hash(key);
	}

	function defaultApp() {
		try {
			if (window.generatorName) return String(window.generatorName);
		} catch (e) {}
		return 'weld';
	}

	function resolveTheme(theme) {
		if (theme === 'light' || theme === 'dark') return theme;
		try {
			if (document.body && document.body.classList.contains('light-theme')) return 'light';
		} catch (e) {}
		return 'dark';
	}

	function defaultConfig() {
		return {
			loadFonts: 'Open Sans, Inter',
			containerStyle: 'width:100%;height:100%;',
			inputAreaStyle: 'height:68px;',
			commentPlaceholderText: 'Add a comment...',
			submitButtonText: 'comment',
			allowBots: false
		};
	}

	function buildSrc(app, chan, config) {
		config = config || {};
		var merged = {};
		var d = defaultConfig();
		for (var k in d) merged[k] = d[k];
		for (var k2 in config) merged[k2] = config[k2];
		merged.channel = chan;
		var adminHash = merged.adminPasswordHash || '';
		var encoded = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(merged));
		var src = EMBED_BASE + app + '+' + chan + '?';
		if (adminHash) src += 'adminPasswordHash=' + adminHash;
		return src + '#' + encoded;
	}

	function ensureStyle() {
		if (typeof document === 'undefined' || document.getElementById(STYLE_ID)) return;
		var st = document.createElement('style');
		st.id = STYLE_ID;
		st.textContent = '.weld-comments-ov{z-index:99999;position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.72);display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:14px;box-sizing:border-box;}' + '.weld-comments-card{width:min(980px,100%);height:min(760px,92dvh);background:var(--background-color,var(--bg-color,#111));color:var(--text-color,#eee);border:1px solid var(--border-color,#444);border-radius:12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.35);}' + '.weld-comments-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;padding:9px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color,#444);}' + '.weld-comments-title{font-weight:700;font-size:14px;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;}' + '.weld-comments-x{border:1px solid var(--border-color,#444);background:var(--input-bg,#222);color:var(--text-color,#eee);border-radius:8px;width:30px;height:30px;cursor:pointer;}' + '.weld-comments-layout{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(180px,34%) 1fr;min-height:0;flex:1;}' + '.weld-comments-preview{border-right:1px solid var(--border-color,#444);background:var(--input-bg,#151515);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:0;padding:10px;}' + '.weld-comments-preview img{max-width:100%;max-height:100%;object-fit:contain;border-radius:8px;}' + '.weld-comments-wrap{position:relative;min-width:0;min-height:0;}' + '.weld-comments-frame{width:100%;height:100%;border:none;background:transparent;}' + '.weld-comments-loading{position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:2;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;background:var(--background-color,var(--bg-color,#111));color:var(--text-color,#eee);font:13px/1.4 Open Sans,sans-serif;transition:opacity .18s ease;pointer-events:none;}' + '.weld-comments-spin{width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:999px;border:2px solid rgba(128,128,128,.35);border-top-color:currentColor;animation:weldCommentsSpin .8s linear infinite;}' + '@keyframes weldCommentsSpin{to{transform:rotate(360deg);}}' + '@media (max-width:700px){.weld-comments-card{width:calc(100vw - 24px);max-width:calc(100vw - 24px);height:min(720px,calc(100dvh - 32px));}.weld-comments-layout{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.weld-comments-preview{display:none;}}';
		document.head.appendChild(st);
	}

	function ensureOverlay() {
		if (overlay) return overlay;
		ensureStyle();
		overlay = document.createElement('div');
		overlay.className = 'weld-comments-ov';
		overlay.innerHTML = '<div class="weld-comments-card">' + '<div class="weld-comments-head"><div class="weld-comments-title" data-title>Comments</div>' + '<button class="weld-comments-x" data-close>x</button></div>' + '<div class="weld-comments-layout">' + '<div class="weld-comments-preview" data-preview><img data-img></div>' + '<div class="weld-comments-wrap">' + '<div class="weld-comments-loading" data-loading><div class="weld-comments-spin"></div><div>Loading comments...</div></div>' + '<iframe class="weld-comments-frame" data-frame allow="clipboard-read *; clipboard-write *"></iframe>' + '</div></div></div>';
		overlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			if (e.target === overlay) close();
		});
		overlay.querySelector('[data-close]').addEventListener('click', close);
		var frame = overlay.querySelector('[data-frame]');
		var loading = overlay.querySelector('[data-loading]');
		frame.addEventListener('load', function() {
			loading.style.opacity = '0';
			setTimeout(function() {
				if (loading.style.opacity === '0') loading.style.display = 'none';
			}, 200);
		});
		document.body.appendChild(overlay);
		return overlay;
	}

	function open(opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		if (opts.disabled) {
			notifyDisabled();
			return;
		}
		if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
		var app = opts.app || defaultApp();
		var chan = channel(opts.key, opts.prefix || app);
		var cfg = {};
		var base = opts.config || {};
		for (var k in base) cfg[k] = base[k];
		cfg.forceColorScheme = resolveTheme(opts.theme);
		var src = buildSrc(app, chan, cfg);
		var ov = ensureOverlay();
		ov.querySelector('[data-title]').textContent = opts.title || 'Comments';
		var preview = ov.querySelector('[data-preview]');
		var img = ov.querySelector('[data-img]');
		if (opts.image) {
			preview.style.display = '';
			img.src = opts.image;
		} else {
			preview.style.display = 'none';
		}
		var frame = ov.querySelector('[data-frame]');
		var loading = ov.querySelector('[data-loading]');
		ov.style.display = 'flex';
		if (frame.getAttribute('data-src') !== src) {
			loading.style.display = 'flex';
			loading.style.opacity = '1';
			frame.setAttribute('data-src', src);
			frame.src = src;
		}
		return chan;
	}

	function close() {
		if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'none';
	}

	function inline(target, opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		var el = (typeof target === 'string') ? document.querySelector(target) : target;
		if (!el) return null;
		var app = opts.app || defaultApp();
		var chan = channel(opts.key, opts.prefix || app);
		var cfg = {};
		var base = opts.config || {};
		for (var k in base) cfg[k] = base[k];
		cfg.forceColorScheme = resolveTheme(opts.theme);
		var frame = document.createElement('iframe');
		frame.style.cssText = opts.style || 'width:100%;height:100%;border:none;background:transparent;';
		frame.setAttribute('allow', 'clipboard-read *; clipboard-write *');
		frame.src = buildSrc(app, chan, cfg);
		el.appendChild(frame);
		return frame;
	}

	function notifyDisabled() {
		if (window.weld && window.weld.toast) {
			window.weld.toast.show('Comments are disabled for this item.', 'info');
			return;
		}
		if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
		var p = document.createElement('div');
		p.style.cssText = 'z-index:100000;position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:18px;transform:translateX(-50%);max-width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 28px));padding:10px 13px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--border-color,#444);background:var(--input-bg,#222);color:var(--text-color,#eee);box-shadow:0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.35);font:13px/1.45 Open Sans,sans-serif;opacity:0;transition:opacity .18s ease;pointer-events:none;';
		p.textContent = 'Comments disabled for this item.';
		document.body.appendChild(p);
		requestAnimationFrame(function() {
			p.style.opacity = '1';
		});
		setTimeout(function() {
			p.style.opacity = '0';
			setTimeout(function() {
				p.remove();
			}, 250);
		}, 2400);
	}
	var attachedSecret = null;

	function attach(deps) {
		if (deps && deps.secret) attachedSecret = deps.secret;
		return window.weld.comments;
	}

	function resolveSecret() {
		var sp = attachedSecret || (window.weld && window.weld._secret) || window.secret;
		if (!sp || typeof sp.encrypt !== 'function') throw new Error('weld.comments: secret-plugin not attached -- call weld.comments.attach({ secret: secretPlugin })');
		return sp;
	}
	var CIPHER_RE = /^ENCRYPTED_[0-9]+_[\s\S]+_ENCRYPTED_END$/;

	function isCiphertext(text) {
		return typeof text === 'string' && CIPHER_RE.test(text);
	}

	function sha256Hex(text) {
		var bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(String(text == null ? '' : text));
		return Promise.resolve(crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes)).then(function(buf) {
			var arr = new Uint8Array(buf),
				out = '';
			for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) out += arr[i].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
			return out;
		});
	}
	var cryptoApi = {
		generateKeyPair: function() {
			var k = resolveSecret().generateKeyPair();
			return {
				publicKey: k.public || k.publicKey,
				privateKey: k.private || k.privateKey
			};
		},
		encrypt: function(text, publicKey) {
			return String(resolveSecret().encrypt(String(text), publicKey));
		},
		decrypt: function(ciphertext, privateKey) {
			return String(resolveSecret().decrypt(ciphertext, privateKey));
		},
		keyPairMatches: function(publicKey, privateKey) {
			try {
				var sp = resolveSecret(),
					probe = 'weld:' + Date.now() + ':' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
				return String(sp.decrypt(sp.encrypt(probe, publicKey), privateKey)) === probe;
			} catch (e) {
				return false;
			}
		},
		publicKeyHash: function(publicKey) {
			return sha256Hex(String(publicKey));
		},
		isCiphertext: isCiphertext
	};

	function channelString(id, opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		var base = String(id == null ? '' : id),
			mods = [];
		if (opts.u) mods.push('u:' + String(opts.u).toLowerCase());
		else if (opts.auth) mods.push('auth:' + String(opts.auth));
		if (opts.ids !== false) mods.push('ids:channel');
		return mods.length ? base + '+' + mods.join(',') : base;
	}

	function store(opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		var ctrl = opts.controller;
		if (!ctrl || typeof ctrl.submit !== 'function') throw new Error('weld.comments.store: a comments-plugin controller with submit() is required');
		var keys = opts.keys || null,
			anon = opts.anonymous !== false;

		function post(record) {
			var payload = (typeof record === 'string') ? record : JSON.stringify(record);
			if (keys) payload = cryptoApi.encrypt(payload, keys.publicKey);
			var pre = Promise.resolve();
			if (anon && typeof ctrl.setNicknameForNextComment === 'function') pre = Promise.resolve(ctrl.setNicknameForNextComment(''));
			return pre.then(function() {
				return ctrl.submit(payload);
			});
		}

		function rawComments() {
			if (typeof ctrl.getComments === 'function') return ctrl.getComments() || [];
			if (Array.isArray(ctrl.comments)) return ctrl.comments;
			return [];
		}

		function list(o) {
			o = o || {};
			var blocked = o.blocked || {},
				out = [];
			rawComments().forEach(function(c) {
				if (!c || typeof c.message !== 'string') return;
				var msg = c.message.trim(),
					uid = (c.user && typeof c.user.id === 'string') ? c.user.id : '';
				if (uid && blocked[uid]) return;
				var value = msg;
				if (keys) {
					if (!isCiphertext(msg)) return;
					try {
						value = cryptoApi.decrypt(msg, keys.privateKey);
					} catch (e) {
						return;
					}
				}
				if (o.parse !== false) {
					try {
						value = JSON.parse(value);
					} catch (e) {}
				}
				out.push({
					value: value,
					userId: uid,
					time: Number((c.time || c.createdAt || 0)),
					raw: c
				});
			});
			if (o.sort !== false) out.sort(function(a, b) {
				return b.time - a.time;
			});
			return out;
		}

		function onNew(cb) {
			if (typeof ctrl.onComment === 'function') return ctrl.onComment(cb);
			if (typeof ctrl.onUpdate === 'function') return ctrl.onUpdate(function() {
				cb(list());
			});
			return function() {};
		}
		return {
			post: post,
			list: list,
			onNew: onNew,
			channel: opts.channel || null
		};
	}
	window.weld.comments = {
		channel: channel,
		hash: hash,
		buildSrc: buildSrc,
		open: open,
		inline: inline,
		close: close,
		attach: attach,
		crypto: cryptoApi,
		channelString: channelString,
		isCiphertext: isCiphertext,
		store: store,
		version: '1.1.0'
	};
	return window.weld.comments;
})();

	'use strict';
	var api = factory();
	if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
	root.ChloeT0 = api;

	'use strict';

	function snowflakeCmp(a, b) {
		var x = BigInt(a),
			y = BigInt(b);
		return x < y ? -1 : x > y ? 1 : 0;
	}

	function maxSnowflake(a, b) {
		if (a == null) return b;
		if (b == null) return a;
		return snowflakeCmp(a, b) >= 0 ? a : b;
	}
	var DISCORD_EPOCH = 1420070400000;

	function snowflakeTime(id) {
		try {
			return Number(BigInt(id) >> 22 n) + DISCORD_EPOCH;
		} catch (e) {
			return null;
		}
	}
	window.RookLobes = (function() {
		var KEY = 'rooklobes:v1';

		function load() {
			try {
				return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)) || {};
			} catch (e) {
				return {};
			}
		}
		var S = load();
		S.on = (S.on !== false);

		};

		};
		S.drives = S.drives || {

		};

		};

		S.bond = S.bond || {

		S.epistemic = S.epistemic || {

		var _persistFailAt = 0,
