	function withDefaults(saved, defs) {
		var st = Object.assign({}, defs, saved || {});
		Object.keys(defs).forEach(function(k) {
			var d = defs[k];
			if (d && typeof d === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d) && st[k] && typeof st[k] === 'object' && !Array.isArray(st[k])) st[k] = Object.assign({}, d, st[k]);
		});
		return st;
	}

	function loadState() {
		try {
			var s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NS + '__state__'));
			return withDefaults(s, DEF_STATE);
		} catch (e) {
			return Object.assign({}, DEF_STATE);
		}
	}

	function persistState() {
		try {
			localStorage.setItem(NS + '__state__', JSON.stringify(state));
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function makeStore(prefix) {
		var P = prefix || NS;

		function k(key) {
			return P + key;
		}
		return {
			get: function(key) {
				try {
					var v = localStorage.getItem(k(key));
					return Promise.resolve(v == null ? null : JSON.parse(v));
				} catch (e) {
					return Promise.resolve(null);
				}
			},
			set: function(key, val) {
				try {
					localStorage.setItem(k(key), JSON.stringify(val));
				} catch (e) {}
				return Promise.resolve(true);
			},
			del: function(key) {
				try {
					localStorage.removeItem(k(key));
				} catch (e) {}
				return Promise.resolve(true);
			},
			listIndex: function() {
				try {
					var v = localStorage.getItem(P + '__index__');
					return Promise.resolve(v ? JSON.parse(v) : []);
				} catch (e) {
					return Promise.resolve([]);
				}
			},
			setIndex: function(ids) {
				try {
					localStorage.setItem(P + '__index__', JSON.stringify(ids));
				} catch (e) {}
				return Promise.resolve(true);
			}
		};
	}
	var store = makeStore();

	function requestPersistentStorage() {
		try {
			if (navigator.storage && navigator.storage.persist) {
				return navigator.storage.persisted().then(function(already) {
					return already ? true : navigator.storage.persist();
				}).catch(function() {
					return null;
				});
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		return Promise.resolve(null);
	}

	function fmtBytes(n) {
		n = Number(n) || 0;
		if (n < 1024) return n + ' B';
		var u = ['KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'],
			i = -1;
		do {
			n /= 1024;
			i++;
		} while (n >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1);
		return n.toFixed(n < 10 ? 1 : 0) + ' ' + u[i];
	}

	function storageHealth() {
		var out = {
			supported: false,
			persisted: null,
			usage: null,
			quota: null
		};
		try {
			if (!navigator.storage) {
				return Promise.resolve(out);
			}
			out.supported = true;
			var pP = navigator.storage.persisted ? navigator.storage.persisted() : Promise.resolve(null);
			var pE = navigator.storage.estimate ? navigator.storage.estimate() : Promise.resolve({});
			return Promise.all([pP.catch(function() {
				return null;
			}), pE.catch(function() {
				return {};
			})]).then(function(r) {
				out.persisted = r[0];
				out.usage = r[1] && r[1].usage;
				out.quota = r[1] && r[1].quota;
				return out;
			});
		} catch (e) {
			return Promise.resolve(out);
		}
	}

	function storageLine(h) {
		if (!h || !h.supported) return 'storage: this browser doesn\u2019t report storage health - keep an Export backup to be safe';
		var dur = h.persisted === true ? 'durable - persisted, won\u2019t be evicted' : h.persisted === false ? 'best-effort - may be evicted under storage pressure' : 'durability unknown';
		var use = '';
		if (h.usage != null && h.quota) {
			use = ' \u00b7 ' + fmtBytes(h.usage) + ' of ' + fmtBytes(h.quota) + ' used (' + Math.round(h.usage / h.quota * 100) + '%)';
		} else if (h.usage != null) {
			use = ' \u00b7 ' + fmtBytes(h.usage) + ' used';
		}
		return 'storage: ' + dur + use;
	}

	function clearStaleLocks() {
		try {
			store.del('runlock:' + CH);
			store.del('pending:' + CH);
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function browserContext() {
		var now = Date.now();
		var d = new Date();
		var off = -(d.getTimezoneOffset());
		var tz = '';
		try {
			tz = Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '';
		} catch (e) {}
		var locale = '';
		try {
			locale = navigator.language || (navigator.languages && navigator.languages[0]) || '';
		} catch (e) {}
		var dateStr, timeStr;
		try {
			dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString(locale || undefined, {
				weekday: 'long',
				year: 'numeric',
				month: 'long',
				day: 'numeric'
			});
		} catch (e) {
			dateStr = d.toDateString();
		}
		try {
			timeStr = d.toLocaleTimeString(locale || undefined, {
				hour: 'numeric',
				minute: '2-digit'
			});
		} catch (e) {
			timeStr = d.toTimeString().slice(0, 5);
		}
		return {
			now: now,
			offsetMins: off,
			tz: tz,
			locale: locale,
			dateStr: dateStr,
			timeStr: timeStr
		};
	}

	function deviceClockOf(bc) {
		return {
			time: bc.timeStr,
			date: bc.dateStr,
			tz: bc.tz,
			at: bc.now
		};
	}

	function buildInjections() {
		var bc = browserContext();
		var list = [{
			id: 'devicetime',
			at: bc.now,
			ttlMs: 180000,
			text: 'Real-world context from the user\u2019s device: it is ' + bc.timeStr + ' on ' + bc.dateStr + (bc.tz ? ' (' + bc.tz + ')' : '') + '. The user\u2019s language is ' + (bc.locale || 'unknown') + '. This is the actual local date and time — use it when it helps (greetings, scheduling, "today"), but don\u2019t recite it unprompted.'
		}];
		(state.injections || []).forEach(function(it, i) {
			if (it && it.text && String(it.text).trim()) list.push({
				id: it.id || ('inj' + i),
				text: String(it.text).trim()
			});
		});
		try {
			var fd = window.ChloeFrame ? window.ChloeFrame.directive(state.frame) : '';
			if (fd) list.splice(1, 0, {
				id: 'roleframe',
				at: bc.now,
				text: fd,
				priority: 80
			});
		} catch (e) {}
		try {
			if (state.feltVoice !== false && window.ChloeAffect) {
				var ar = getAffect();
				if (ar) {
					var fl = window.ChloeAffect.feltDirective(ar.snapshot());
					if (fl) list.push({
						id: 'feltvoice',
						at: bc.now,
						text: fl
					});
				}
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		try {
			var sr = sceneRoster();
			if (sr) list.push({
				id: 'sceneroster',
				at: bc.now,
				text: sr,
				priority: 70
			});
		} catch (e) {}
		return list;
	}

	function refreshClock() {
		if (!engine) return;
		var bc = browserContext();
		var patch = {
			timezoneOffsetMins: bc.offsetMins,
			deviceClock: deviceClockOf(bc),
			semanticInjections: buildInjections()
		};
		if (engine.updateConfig) {
			try {
				engine.updateConfig(patch);
			} catch (e) {}
		} else {
			buildEngine();
		}
		var el = byId('clockNow');
		if (el) el.textContent = 'She currently knows it\u2019s ' + bc.timeStr + ', ' + bc.dateStr + (bc.tz ? ' · ' + bc.tz : '') + (bc.locale ? ' · ' + bc.locale : '');
	}

	function loreKeysArr(e) {
		var k = e && e.keys;
		if (k == null) k = e && e.key;
		var arr = Array.isArray(k) ? k : (k == null ? [] : String(k).split(','));
		return arr.map(function(x) {
			return String(x).trim();
		}).filter(Boolean);
	}

	function renderLore() {
		var box = byId('loreList');
		if (!box) return;
		box.innerHTML = '';
		var lore = state.lorebook || (state.lorebook = []);
		if (!lore.length) {
			box.innerHTML = '<div class="note">No entries yet. Add one - e.g. triggers <i>Kira, the captain</i> \u2192 \u201cKira is the ship\u2019s captain: sharp, weary, fiercely loyal.\u201d</div>';
			return;
		}
		lore.forEach(function(e, idx) {
			var card = document.createElement('div');
			card.className = 'lore-entry';
			var head = document.createElement('div');
			head.className = 'lore-head';
			var keys = document.createElement('input');
			keys.type = 'text';
			keys.className = 'lore-keys';
			keys.placeholder = 'trigger words, comma-separated';
			keys.value = loreKeysArr(e).join(', ');
			var del = document.createElement('button');
			del.className = 'lore-del';
			del.textContent = '\u2715';
			del.title = 'Delete entry';
			var content = document.createElement('textarea');
			content.className = 'lore-content';
			content.rows = 2;
			content.placeholder = 'what the character should know when a trigger appears';
			content.value = e.content || '';
			keys.addEventListener('change', function() {
				e.keys = keys.value.split(',').map(function(s) {
					return s.trim();
				}).filter(Boolean);
				persistState();
			});
			content.addEventListener('change', function() {
				e.content = content.value;
				persistState();
			});
			del.addEventListener('click', function() {
				state.lorebook.splice(idx, 1);
				persistState();
				renderLore();
			});
			head.appendChild(keys);
			head.appendChild(del);
			card.appendChild(head);
			card.appendChild(content);
			box.appendChild(card);
		});
	}

	function addLore() {
		if (!state.lorebook) state.lorebook = [];
		state.lorebook.push({
			id: 'lore' + Date.now(),
			keys: [],
			content: ''
		});
		persistState();
		renderLore();
	}

	function exportLore() {
		try {
			var blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(state.lorebook || [], null, 2)], {
				type: 'application/json'
			});
			var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
			var a = document.createElement('a');
			a.href = url;
			a.download = 'chloe-lorebook.json';
			document.body.appendChild(a);
			a.click();
			a.remove();
			setTimeout(function() {
				URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
			}, 1500);
			toast('Lorebook exported', 'success');
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function normalizeLore(obj) {
		function mk(keys, content) {
			return {
				id: 'lore' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5),
				keys: (Array.isArray(keys) ? keys : String(keys || '').split(',')).map(function(k) {
					return String(k).trim();
				}).filter(Boolean),
				content: String(content || '').trim()
			};
		}
		if (Array.isArray(obj)) return obj.map(function(e) {
			return mk(e.keys || e.key, e.content || e.text);
		}).filter(function(e) {
			return e.content;
		});
		if (obj && obj.entries && typeof obj.entries === 'object') {
			return Object.keys(obj.entries).map(function(k) {
				var en = obj.entries[k] || {};
				return mk(en.key || en.keys, en.content);
			}).filter(function(e) {
				return e.content;
			});
		}
		return [];
	}

	function importLore(file) {
		var r = new FileReader();
		r.onload = function() {
			try {
				var arr = normalizeLore(JSON.parse(r.result));
				if (!arr.length) {
					toast('No lore entries found in that file', 'error');
					return;
				}
				state.lorebook = (state.lorebook || []).concat(arr);
				persistState();
				renderLore();
				toast(arr.length + ' lore ' + (arr.length === 1 ? 'entry' : 'entries') + ' imported', 'success');
			} catch (e) {
				toast('That didn\u2019t look like a lorebook', 'error');
			}
		};
		r.readAsText(file);
	}

	function renderInjList() {
		var box = byId('injList');
		if (!box) return;
		while (box.firstChild) box.removeChild(box.firstChild);
		var list = state.injections || [];
		if (!list.length) {
			var e = document.createElement('div');
			e.className = 'note';
			e.textContent = 'Nothing yet — only the live date/time above.';
			box.appendChild(e);
			return;
		}
		list.forEach(function(it, idx) {
			var row = document.createElement('div');
			row.className = 'memrow';
			var t = document.createElement('div');
			t.className = 'mt';
			t.textContent = it.text;
			var x = document.createElement('button');
			x.className = 'forget';
			x.title = 'Remove';
			x.textContent = '\u00d7';
			x.addEventListener('click', function() {
				state.injections.splice(idx, 1);
				persistState();
				refreshClock();
				renderInjList();
			});
			row.appendChild(t);
			row.appendChild(x);
			box.appendChild(row);
		});
	}

	function ensureUserPartitionIn(st) {
		return st.get('u:' + USER_ID).then(function(p) {
			if (p) {
				if (state.userName && p.name !== state.userName) {
					p.name = state.userName;
					return st.set('u:' + USER_ID, p);
				}
				return;
			}
			var now = Date.now();
			return st.set('u:' + USER_ID, {
				id: USER_ID,
				name: state.userName || 'You',
				firstSeen: now,
				lastSeen: now,
				interactionCount: 0,
				state: 'active',
				lifecycle: 'quiet',
				recent: [],
				facts: []
			});
		});
	}

	function ensureUserPartition() {
		return ensureUserPartitionIn(store);
	}

	function renderFactList() {
		var box = byId('factList');
		var eng = viewEngine();
		if (!box || !eng || !eng.getFacts) return;
		Promise.resolve(eng.getFacts(USER_ID)).then(function(facts) {
			while (box.firstChild) box.removeChild(box.firstChild);
			if (!facts || !facts.length) {
				var e = document.createElement('div');
				e.className = 'note';
				e.textContent = 'Nothing yet - memories build as you talk.';
				box.appendChild(e);
				return;
			}
			facts.slice().reverse().forEach(function(f) {
				var row = document.createElement('div');
				row.className = 'memrow';
				var t = document.createElement('div');
				t.className = 'mt';
				t.textContent = f.text;
				if (f.source === 'operator') {
					var pin = document.createElement('span');
					pin.className = 'pin';
					pin.textContent = 'pinned';
					t.appendChild(pin);
				}
				var x = document.createElement('button');
				x.className = 'forget';
				x.title = 'Forget this';
				x.textContent = '\u00d7';
				x.addEventListener('click', function() {
					Promise.resolve(eng.forgetFact(USER_ID, f.text)).then(renderFactList);
				});
				row.appendChild(t);
				row.appendChild(x);
				box.appendChild(row);
			});
		});
	}

	function renderInnerState() {
		var box = byId('innerState');
		var eng = viewEngine(),
			st = viewStore();
		if (!box || !eng) return;

		function txt(v) {
			return String(v == null ? '' : v);
		}

		function section(label, items, empty) {
			var sec = document.createElement('div');
			sec.style.cssText = 'margin:10px 0 2px';
			var h = document.createElement('div');
			h.textContent = label;
			h.style.cssText = 'font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--accent-ink);margin-bottom:4px';
			sec.appendChild(h);
			if (!items || !items.length) {
				var e = document.createElement('div');
				e.className = 'note';
				e.textContent = empty;
				sec.appendChild(e);
			} else items.forEach(function(it) {
				var r = document.createElement('div');
				r.style.cssText = 'font-size:13px;color:var(--ink);padding:3px 0';
				r.textContent = it;
				sec.appendChild(r);
			});
			return sec;
		}
		var get = function(fn, arg) {
			try {
				return fn ? Promise.resolve(fn(arg)) : Promise.resolve(null);
			} catch (e) {
				return Promise.resolve(null);
			}
		};
		Promise.all([
			get(st.get, 'mood:' + CH),
			get(eng.getPersonaNote),
			get(eng.getMemory, USER_ID),
			get(eng.goalsForOwner, USER_ID),
			get(eng.getSelfIntents)
		]).then(function(r) {
			var mood = r[0],
				persona = r[1],
				mem = r[2],
				goals = r[3],
				intents = r[4];
			while (box.firstChild) box.removeChild(box.firstChild);
			var moodStr = (mood && eng.moodDescriptor) ? eng.moodDescriptor(mood) : '';
			box.appendChild(section('Mood', moodStr ? ['Feeling ' + moodStr + ' right now.'] : [], 'Still settling — mood forms as you talk.'));
			var personaText = persona ? (typeof persona === 'string' ? persona : (persona.text || '')) : '';
			box.appendChild(section('Self-image', personaText ? [txt(personaText)] : [], 'No self-note yet.'));
			var insights = (mem && mem.insights || []).map(function(x) {
				return txt(x.text);
			});
			box.appendChild(section('Conclusions about you', insights, 'No standing conclusions yet - just facts so far.'));
			var goalTexts = (goals || []).map(function(g) {
				return txt(g.text);
			});
			box.appendChild(section('To follow up on', goalTexts, 'Nothing on the list yet.'));
			var now = Date.now();
			var pend = (intents || []).map(function(it) {
				var subj = it.subject ? (' - ' + txt(it.subject)) : '';
				var when = (it.dueAt && it.dueAt > now) ? (' (in ~' + Math.max(1, Math.round((it.dueAt - now) / 60000)) + ' min)') : '';
				return txt(it.kind) + subj + when;
			});
			box.appendChild(section('Planning to revisit', pend, 'Nothing queued to come back to.'));
			if (lastNationTrace && lastNationTrace.length) box.appendChild(section('Council - last turn', lastNationTrace, ''));
		});
	}

	function snow(ms) {
		return String((BigInt(ms - SNOW_EPOCH) << 22 n) + BigInt((snowSeq++) % 4096));
	}

	function snowCmp(a, b) {
		var A = BigInt(a),
			B = BigInt(b);
		return A < B ? -1 : (A > B ? 1 : 0);
	}
	var queue = loadQueue();

	function loadQueue() {
		try {
			var v = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NS + '__queue__'));
			return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
		} catch (e) {
			return [];
		}
	}

	function persistQueue() {
		try {
			localStorage.setItem(NS + '__queue__', JSON.stringify(queue.slice(-400)));
		} catch (e) {}
	}
	var transport = {
		getMessagesAfter: function(chId, afterId, limit) {
			var out = queue.filter(function(m) {
				return !afterId || snowCmp(m.id, afterId) > 0;
			});
			return Promise.resolve(out.slice(0, limit || 50));
		},
		getMessagesBefore: function() {
			return Promise.resolve([]);
		}
	};

	function enqueue(authorId, name, bot, text) {
		var ms = Date.now();
		var m = {
			id: snow(ms),
			channelId: CH,
			author: {
				id: authorId,
				username: name,
				bot: !!bot
			},
			content: text,
			timestamp: new Date(ms).toISOString()
		};
		queue.push(m);
		persistQueue();
		if (isGroup()) {
			try {
				planTurn(m);
			} catch (e) {}
		}
		return m;
	}
	var SAFE_RATING = 'pg13';

	function isAdultContent() {
		var a = parseInt(state.userAge, 10);
		return isFinite(a) && a >= 21;
	}

	function effectiveRating() {
		var m = state.moderation || {};
		return isAdultContent() ? (m.rating || 'x') : SAFE_RATING;
	}
	var Moderation = (function() {
		function weldList() {
			try {
				if (window.weld && window.weld.banlist && window.weld.banlist.create) {
					var m = state.moderation || {};
					return window.weld.banlist.create({
						phrases: m.phrases || [],
						negativePhrases: [],
						users: [],
						rating: effectiveRating()
					});
				}
			} catch (e) {}
			return null;
		}

		function checkText(text) {
			var m = state.moderation || {};
			var list = weldList();
			var active = m.enabled || !isAdultContent();
			if (!active) return {
				ok: true
			};
			if (list && list.check) {
				var r = list.check(String(text || ''));
				return (r && r.ok === false) ? {
					ok: false,
					reason: (r.reason || r.matched || 'blocked')
				} : {
					ok: true
				};
			}
			if (!m.enabled) return {
				ok: true
			};
			var t = String(text || '').toLowerCase();
			var phrases = m.phrases || [];
			for (var i = 0; i < phrases.length; i++) {
				var p = String(phrases[i] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
				if (!p) continue;
				var hit = p.indexOf('*') >= 0 ? new RegExp(p.replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\*/g, '.*'), 'i').test(t) : t.indexOf(p) >= 0;
				if (hit) return {
					ok: false,
					reason: 'blocked term: ' + p
				};
			}
			return {
				ok: true
			};
		}
		return {
			checkText: checkText
		};
	})();

	function validResolution(r) {
		r = String(r || '');
		return ({
			'512x512': 1,
			'512x768': 1,
			'768x512': 1,
			'768x768': 1
		})[r] ? r : '768x768';
	}

	function autoResolution(p) {
		p = String(p || '');
		if (/\b(portrait|selfie|headshot)\b/i.test(p)) return '512x768';
		if (/\b(landscape|wide.?angle|panorama|scenery|vista)\b/i.test(p)) return '768x512';
		return '768x768';
	}

	function paintablePrompt(s) {
		return String(s || '').replace(/\([a-zA-Z]+:::[^)]*\)/g, '').trim();
	}

	function alphaCropRect(alpha, w, h, opts) {
		opts = opts || {};
		var pad = (opts.pad != null ? opts.pad : 0.06),
			aspect = opts.aspect || null,
			A = (opts.threshold != null ? opts.threshold : 12);
		var minX = w,
			minY = h,
			maxX = -1,
			maxY = -1,
			x, y;
		for (y = 0; y < h; y++) {
			for (x = 0; x < w; x++) {
				if (alpha[y * w + x] > A) {
					if (x < minX) minX = x;
					if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
					if (y < minY) minY = y;
					if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
				}
			}
		}
		if (maxX < minX || maxY < minY) return null;
		var bw = maxX - minX + 1,
			bh = maxY - minY + 1;
		var px = Math.round(bw * pad),
			py = Math.round(bh * pad);
		var x0 = Math.max(0, minX - px),
			y0 = Math.max(0, minY - py),
			x1 = Math.min(w, maxX + 1 + px),
			y1 = Math.min(h, maxY + 1 + py);
		if (aspect) {
			var cw = x1 - x0,
				ch = y1 - y0;
			if (ch * aspect > cw) {
				var add = ch * aspect - cw;
				x0 = Math.max(0, x0 - add / 2);
				x1 = Math.min(w, x1 + add / 2);
			} else {
				var addy = cw / aspect - ch;
				if (addy > 0) {
					y0 = Math.max(0, y0 - addy / 2);
					y1 = Math.min(h, y1 + addy / 2);
				}
			}
		}
		x0 = Math.round(x0);
		y0 = Math.round(y0);
		var rw = Math.round(x1 - x0),
			rh = Math.round(y1 - y0);
		if (rw <= 0 || rh <= 0) return null;
		return {
			x: x0,
			y: y0,
			w: rw,
			h: rh
		};
	}

	function cropCanvasToAlpha(canvas, opts) {
		if (!canvas || !canvas.getContext || !canvas.width || !canvas.height) return null;
		var w = canvas.width,
			h = canvas.height,
			img;
		try {
			img = canvas.getContext('2d').getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
		} catch (e) {
			return null;
		}
		var alpha = new Uint8Array(w * h),
			d = img.data,
			i;
		for (i = 0; i < w * h; i++) alpha[i] = d[i * 4 + 3];
		var r = alphaCropRect(alpha, w, h, opts);
		if (!r) return null;
		if (r.x === 0 && r.y === 0 && r.w === w && r.h === h) return null;
		var out = document.createElement('canvas');
		out.width = r.w;
		out.height = r.h;
		out.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 0, 0, r.w, r.h);
		return out;
	}
	var PAINT_TIMEOUT_MS = 90000;

	function installPainter() {
		if (window.paintImage) return;
		var IMAGE_MAX_CONCURRENCY = 1;
		var q = [],
			active = 0;

		function pump() {
			while (active < IMAGE_MAX_CONCURRENCY && q.length) {
				active++;
				runPaintJob(q.shift()).then(slotFreed, slotFreed);
			}
		}

		function slotFreed() {
			active--;
			pump();
		}

		function runPaintJob(req) {
			return new Promise(function(resolve) {
				var reqId = req && req.reqId,
					done = false;

				function finish(result) {
					if (done) return;
					done = true;
					try {
						window.__chloePaint.set(reqId, result);
					} catch (e) {}
					resolve();
				}
				var mod = Moderation.checkText(req && req.prompt);
				if (!mod.ok) return finish({
					ok: false,
					reason: mod.reason
				});
				var t2i = null;
				try {
					if (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root && typeof root.textToImagePlugin === 'function') t2i = root.textToImagePlugin;
				} catch (e) {}
				if (!t2i) {
					try {
						if (typeof window.textToImagePlugin === 'function') t2i = window.textToImagePlugin;
					} catch (e) {}
				}
				if (!t2i) {
					var d = String(req && req.resolution || '512x512').split('x'),
						w = +d[0] || 512,
						h = +d[1] || 512;
					var label = String(req && req.prompt || '').replace(/[<&]/g, ' ').slice(0, 48);
					var svg = '<svg xmlns="http:
					return finish({
						ok: true,
						dataUrl: 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,' + btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(svg)))
					});
				}
				if (!paintablePrompt(req && req.prompt)) return finish({
					ok: false,
					reason: 'empty image prompt — nothing to draw'
				});
				var opts = {
					prompt: String(req && req.prompt || ''),
					resolution: validResolution(req.resolution || autoResolution(req.prompt))
				};
				if (req.guidanceScale != null) opts.guidanceScale = req.guidanceScale;
				if (req.removeBackground) opts.removeBackground = true;
				var timer = setTimeout(function() {
					finish({
						ok: false,
						reason: 'image generation timed out'
					});
				}, PAINT_TIMEOUT_MS);
				try {
					Promise.resolve(t2i(opts)).then(function(out) {
						clearTimeout(timer);
						var data = (out && out.dataUrl != null) ? String(out.dataUrl) : (out && out.url != null) ? String(out.url) : '';
						var canvas = out && out.canvas;
						if (req.removeBackground && canvas && canvas.getContext) {
							var cropped = cropCanvasToAlpha(canvas, {
								pad: 0.06,
								aspect: (req.cropAspect != null ? req.cropAspect : 3 / 4)
							});
							if (cropped) {
								canvas = cropped;
								try {
									data = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
								} catch (e) {}
							}
						}
						if (canvas && typeof canvas.toBlob === 'function') {
							try {
								canvas.toBlob(function(blob) {
									finish(blob ? {
										ok: true,
										dataUrl: data,
										blob: blob
									} : (data ? {
										ok: true,
										dataUrl: data
									} : {
										ok: false,
										reason: 'the image plugin returned no image data'
									}));
								}, req.removeBackground ? 'image/png' : 'image/jpeg', 0.92);
								return;
							} catch (e) {}
						}
						finish(data ? {
							ok: true,
							dataUrl: data
						} : {
							ok: false,
							reason: 'the image plugin returned no image data'
						});
					}, function(e) {
						clearTimeout(timer);
						finish({
							ok: false,
							reason: String((e && e.message) || e) || 'image generation failed'
						});
					});
				} catch (e) {
					clearTimeout(timer);
					finish({
						ok: false,
						reason: String((e && e.message) || e) || 'image generation failed'
					});
				}
			});
		}
		window.paintImage = function(req) {
			try {
				if (!window.__chloePaint) window.__chloePaint = new Map();
			} catch (e) {
				return;
			}
			q.push(req);
			pump();
		};
		try {
			if (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root && typeof root === 'object' && !root.paintImage) root.paintImage = window.paintImage;
		} catch (e) {}
	}
	var pageCountTokens = null,
		pageMaxContextTokens = null;

	function wireTokenizer() {
		var ai = null;
		try {
			ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root && root.aiTextPlugin);
		} catch (e) {}
		if (typeof ai !== 'function') return;
		try {
			var meta = ai({
				getMetaObject: true
			});
			if (meta && typeof meta.countTokens === 'function') {
				pageCountTokens = meta.countTokens;
			}
			if (meta && typeof meta.idealMaxContextTokens === 'number') {
				pageMaxContextTokens = meta.idealMaxContextTokens;
			}
		} catch (e) {}
	}
	var demoMode = false;

	function installMockIfNeeded() {
		var has = false;
		try {
			has = !!(window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin));
		} catch (e) {}
		demoMode = !has;
		if (has) {
			setText('herRole', 'a companion who remembers');
			return;
		}
		setText('herRole', 'demo mode — no language model connected');
		try {
			if (typeof window.root === 'undefined') window.root = window;
		} catch (e) {}
		var lines = ["I'm here, though I'm running in demo mode — there's no language model wired in yet. Drop this onto a Perchance page with the ai-text plugin and I'll answer for real.", "Noted. (Demo mode: I can hold the conversation and remember it, but my words are placeholders until a model is connected.)", "I'm following along. On Perchance, with the free ai-text plugin, this is where my real reply would be.", "Got it. Watch the mind panel — even in demo mode the thinking is real; only the words are stand-ins."];
		var n = 0;
		window.aiTextPlugin = function(opts) {
			var instr = String(opts && opts.instruction || '');
			var sw = String(opts && opts.startWith || '');
			if (/"action"\s*:/.test(instr)) return Promise.resolve('"action":"reply","confidence":0.9,"emoji":""');
			if (/JSON array of question strings/i.test(instr)) return Promise.resolve('["What\'s the heart of it?","What would change my view?","What\'s the next small step?"]');
			if (/Answer this one question|thinking quietly to yourself/i.test(instr)) return Promise.resolve('A small consideration: it depends on what matters most here.');
			if (/"type":\s*"insight\|goal\|none"|tag it: "insight"/i.test(instr) || (sw === '{' && /synthesi|recompose|one or two sentences/i.test(instr))) return Promise.resolve('"text":"a quiet realisation worth keeping in mind","type":"insight"');
			if (/durable facts|notable episodes|JSON array/i.test(instr)) return Promise.resolve('[]');
			if (/running summary|brief summary of what/i.test(instr)) return Promise.resolve('They have been getting acquainted; nothing major yet.');
			if (/tidying your memory|consolidating memories/i.test(instr)) return Promise.resolve('[]');
			if (sw === '{') return Promise.resolve('{}');
			if (sw === '[') return Promise.resolve('[]');
			var m = instr.match(/Recent conversation:\n([\s\S]*?)\n\n/);
			var lastUser = '';
			if (m) {
				var ls = m[1].trim().split('\n');
				lastUser = (ls[ls.length - 1] || '').replace(/^[^:]+:\s*/, '');
			}
			var reply = lines[(n++) % lines.length];
			if (lastUser) reply = '“' + lastUser.slice(0, 80) + '” — ' + reply;
			return Promise.resolve(reply);
		};
	}
	var liveWrap = null,
		liveBody = null,
		liveText = '',
		liveActive = false,
		replyExpected = false;

	function herName() {
		return state.botName || 'Chloe';
	}

	function dropLiveOrphan() {
		if (liveWrap && liveWrap.parentNode && liveWrap.__pending) {
			liveWrap.parentNode.removeChild(liveWrap);
		}
		liveWrap = null;
		liveBody = null;
		liveText = '';
	}

	function beginLiveReply() {
		dropLiveOrphan();
		liveActive = true;
	}

	function ensureLiveBubble() {
		if (liveWrap) return;
		liveWrap = renderMessage('her', herName(), '', {
			streaming: true
		});
		liveWrap.__pending = true;
		liveBody = liveWrap.querySelector('.body');
		lastBotWrap = liveWrap;
		paintMsgFace(liveWrap);
		try {
			byId('typingRow').classList.remove('on');
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function updateLiveReply(t) {
		ensureLiveBubble();
		liveText = t;
		if (liveBody) liveBody.textContent = t;
		scrollThread(false);
	}

	function finalizeLiveReply(finalText) {
		if (!liveWrap) return false;
		if (liveBody) setBody(liveBody, finalText, true);
		liveWrap.classList.remove('streaming');
		liveWrap.__pending = false;
		attachFeedback(liveWrap, finalText);
		var lmid = liveWrap.getAttribute('data-mid');
		if (lmid) attachRegen(liveWrap, lmid, false);
		attachRemove(liveWrap);
		applyEmotionCue(liveWrap, finalText);
		showReaction(detectEmotion(finalText) || 'normal');
		liveWrap = null;
		liveBody = null;
		liveText = '';
		liveActive = false;
		scrollThread(false);
		return true;
	}

	function wrapAiForStreaming() {
		var holders = [];
		try {
			if (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root && typeof root.aiTextPlugin === 'function') holders.push(root);
		} catch (e) {}
		try {
			if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.aiTextPlugin === 'function' && holders.indexOf(window) < 0) holders.push(window);
		} catch (e) {}
		holders.forEach(function(h) {
			var base = h.aiTextPlugin;
			if (base.__streamWrapped) return;
			var wrapped = function(opts) {
				var conversational = opts && opts.startWith === '' && !opts.getMetaObject && !isGroup();
				if (!conversational || !replyExpected) return base(opts);
				beginLiveReply();
				var o = {};
				for (var k in opts)
					if (opts.hasOwnProperty(k)) o[k] = opts[k];
				var userChunk = opts.onChunk;
				o.onChunk = function(c) {
					try {
						if (!(c && c.isFromStartWith)) {
							var full = (c && c.fullTextSoFar != null) ? String(c.fullTextSoFar) : (c && c.textChunk != null) ? (liveText + String(c.textChunk)) : null;
							if (full != null) updateLiveReply(full);
						}
					} catch (e) {}
					if (typeof userChunk === 'function') {
						try {
							userChunk(c);
						} catch (e) {}
					}
				};
				return base(o);
			};
			wrapped.__streamWrapped = true;
			h.aiTextPlugin = wrapped;
		});
	}

	function scrubTokens(s) {
		return String(s || '').replace(/<@!?&?[0-9a-z_-]+>\s*/gi, '').replace(/<#[0-9a-z_-]+>/gi, '').replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ').trim();
	}

	function soloSend(chId, body) {
		setThinking(false);
		var text = scrubTokens(body);
		if (text) {
			var m = enqueue(BOT_ID, herName(), true, text);
			if (liveActive && liveWrap) liveWrap.setAttribute('data-mid', m.id);
			if (!(liveActive && finalizeLiveReply(text))) {
				var w = renderMessage('her', herName(), text, {
					mid: m.id
				});
				if (w) {
					w.setAttribute('data-cid', BOT_ID);
					lastBotWrap = w;
					if (isGroup() || state.botColor) styleSpeaker(w, BOT_ID, state.herAvatar);
					paintMsgFace(w);
					showReaction(detectEmotion(text) || 'normal');
				}
			}
		}
		liveActive = false;
		replyExpected = false;
		return Promise.resolve(true);
	}

	function soloTyping() {
		if (engineThinking) byId('typingRow').classList.add('on');
		return Promise.resolve(true);
	}
	var Gallery = (function() {
		var DB = 'chloe-solo-gallery',
			BLOBS = 'blobs',
			META = 'meta',
			MAX = 300;
		var weld = (window.weld && window.weld.gallery && window.weld.gallery.create) ? window.weld.gallery.create({
			dbName: DB,
			maxItems: MAX
		}) : null;
		var manifest = [],
			dbP = null,
			ready = null;

		function openDb() {
			if (dbP) return dbP;
			dbP = new Promise(function(res, rej) {
				var r = indexedDB.open(DB, 1);
				r.onupgradeneeded = function(e) {
					var db = e.target.result;
					if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(BLOBS)) db.createObjectStore(BLOBS);
					if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(META)) db.createObjectStore(META);
				};
				r.onsuccess = function(e) {
					res(e.target.result);
				};
				r.onerror = function(e) {
					dbP = null;
					rej(e.target.error);
				};
			});
			return dbP;
		}

		function op(store, mode, fn) {
			return openDb().then(function(db) {
				return new Promise(function(res, rej) {
					var tx = db.transaction(store, mode),
						s = tx.objectStore(store),
						q = fn(s);
					if (q && 'onsuccess' in q) {
						q.onsuccess = function() {
							res(q.result);
						};
						q.onerror = function() {
							rej(q.error);
						};
					} else {
						tx.oncomplete = function() {
							res();
						};
						tx.onerror = function() {
							rej(tx.error);
						};
					}
				});
			});
		}

		function uid() {
			return 'img_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
		}

		function saveMan() {
			return op(META, 'readwrite', function(s) {
				return s.put(manifest, 'manifest');
			});
		}

		function loadMan() {
			return op(META, 'readonly', function(s) {
				return s.get('manifest');
			}).then(function(m) {
				manifest = m || [];
				return manifest;
			});
		}

		function init() {
			if (ready) return ready;
			ready = weld ? Promise.resolve(weld.init()) : loadMan();
			return ready;
		}

		function store(blob, meta) {
			meta = meta || {};
			if (weld) return Promise.resolve(weld.store(blob, meta));
			var entry = {
				id: uid(),
				prompt: meta.prompt || '',
				resolution: meta.resolution || null,
				createdAt: Date.now(),
				favorited: false
			};
			return op(BLOBS, 'readwrite', function(s) {
				return s.put(blob, entry.id);
			}).then(function() {
				while (manifest.length >= MAX) {
					var i = manifest.findIndex(function(e) {
						return !e.favorited;
					});
					if (i < 0) i = 0;
					var rm = manifest.splice(i, 1)[0];
					op(BLOBS, 'readwrite', function(s) {
						return s.delete(rm.id);
					}).catch(function() {});
				}
				manifest.push(entry);
				return saveMan();
			}).then(function() {
				return entry.id;
			});
		}

		function getManifest() {
			return weld ? Promise.resolve(weld.getManifest()) : Promise.resolve(manifest.slice());
		}

		function getBlobUrl(id) {
			if (weld) return Promise.resolve(weld.getBlobUrl(id));
			return op(BLOBS, 'readonly', function(s) {
				return s.get(id);
			}).then(function(b) {
				return b ? URL.createObjectURL(b) : null;
			});
		}

		function toggleFavorite(id) {
			if (weld) return Promise.resolve(weld.toggleFavorite(id));
			var e = manifest.filter(function(x) {
				return x.id === id;
			})[0];
			if (!e) return Promise.resolve(null);
			e.favorited = !e.favorited;
			return saveMan().then(function() {
				return e.favorited;
			});
		}

		function remove(id) {
			if (weld) return Promise.resolve(weld.remove(id));
			manifest = manifest.filter(function(e) {
				return e.id !== id;
			});
			return Promise.all([op(BLOBS, 'readwrite', function(s) {
				return s.delete(id);
			}).catch(function() {}), saveMan()]).then(function() {
				return true;
			});
		}

		function clear() {
			if (weld) return Promise.resolve(weld.clear());
			manifest = [];
			return Promise.all([op(BLOBS, 'readwrite', function(s) {
				return s.clear();
			}), saveMan()]);
		}
		return {
			init: init,
			store: store,
			getManifest: getManifest,
			getBlobUrl: getBlobUrl,
			toggleFavorite: toggleFavorite,
			remove: remove,
			clear: clear,
			usingWeld: function() {
				return !!weld;
			}
		};
	})();

	function makeKvBlobStore(dbName, storeName) {
		var dbP = null;

		function openDb() {
			if (dbP) return dbP;
			dbP = new Promise(function(res, rej) {
				var r = indexedDB.open(dbName, 1);
				r.onupgradeneeded = function(e) {
					var db = e.target.result;
					if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(storeName)) db.createObjectStore(storeName, {
						keyPath: 'id'
					});
				};
				r.onsuccess = function(e) {
					res(e.target.result);
				};
				r.onerror = function(e) {
					dbP = null;
					rej(e.target.error);
				};
			});
			return dbP;
		}

		function op(mode, fn) {
			return openDb().then(function(db) {
				return new Promise(function(res, rej) {
					var tx = db.transaction(storeName, mode),
						s = tx.objectStore(storeName),
						q = fn(s);
					if (q && 'onsuccess' in q) {
						q.onsuccess = function() {
							res(q.result);
						};
						q.onerror = function() {
							rej(q.error);
						};
					} else {
						tx.oncomplete = function() {
							res();
						};
						tx.onerror = function() {
							rej(tx.error);
						};
					}
				});
			});
		}
		return {
			put: function(rec) {
				return op('readwrite', function(s) {
					return s.put(rec);
				});
			},
			get: function(id) {
				return op('readonly', function(s) {
					return s.get(id);
				});
			},
			all: function() {
				return op('readonly', function(s) {
					return s.getAll();
				});
			},
			del: function(id) {
				return op('readwrite', function(s) {
					return s.delete(id);
				});
			},
			clear: function() {
				return op('readwrite', function(s) {
					return s.clear();
				});
			}
		};
	}
	var REACTION_EXPRESSION = {
		normal: 'calm neutral expression, relaxed, gentle gaze',
		happy: 'bright warm smile, sparkling eyes, cheerful',
		bored: 'half-lidded eyes, unimpressed flat look, cheek resting on hand',
		worried: 'anxious furrowed brow, slight frown, uneasy eyes',
		lonely: 'wistful distant gaze, faint sad smile, looking away',
		sad: 'downcast eyes, gentle frown, sorrowful',
		crying: 'tears streaming down, scrunched eyes, trembling lips, sobbing',
		shy: 'blushing cheeks, shy averted glance, fingertips near lips',
		excited: 'wide bright eyes, big open smile, energetic, hands raised',
		celebrate: 'joyful cheer, arms thrown up, confetti, beaming party expression'
	};

			expr: 'cheerful, bunny-ear headband, pastel spring colours, holding a decorated egg',
			from: [3, 22],
			to: [4, 26]
		},
		halloween: {

			to: [10, 31]

	function expressionFor(emotion) {
		return REACTION_EXPRESSION[emotion] || (SEASONAL[emotion] && SEASONAL[emotion].expr) || REACTION_EXPRESSION.normal;
	}

	function reactionEmotions() {
		return Object.keys(REACTION_EXPRESSION);
	}

	function learnedStyleNotes() {
		try {
			var n = (state.imageStyleNotes || []);
			return n.length ? (', ' + n.join(', ')) : '';
		} catch (e) {
			return '';
		}
	}
	var STYLE_VOCAB = ['cinematic', 'dramatic lighting', 'soft light', 'golden hour', 'backlit', 'moody', 'high contrast', 'neon', 'candlelit', 'natural light', 'studio lighting', 'rim light', 'volumetric', 'chiaroscuro', 'subsurface scattering', 'soft shadows', 'dramatic shadows', 'ambient light', 'god rays', 'ethereal', 'dreamy', 'gritty', 'somber', 'vibrant', 'muted', 'atmospheric', 'minimalist', 'surreal', 'painterly', 'photorealistic', 'watercolor', 'oil painting', 'anime', 'illustrated', 'digital art', 'sketch', 'line art', 'impressionist', 'concept art', 'cel shaded', '3d render', 'low poly', 'matte painting', 'gouache', 'pencil sketch', 'screencap', 'semi-realistic', 'kawaii', 'digital painting', 'airbrushed', 'alla prima', 'pastel', 'monochrome', 'sepia', 'vivid colors', 'earth tones', 'jewel tones', 'warm tones', 'cool tones', 'saturated', 'desaturated', 'iridescent', 'high saturation', 'bokeh', 'depth of field', 'wide shot', 'close-up', 'macro', 'intricate', 'film grain', 'portrait', 'full body', 'low angle', 'establishing shot', 'subtle imperfections', 'glossy'];

	function harvestStyleTerms(prompt) {
		var p = String(prompt || '').toLowerCase();
		return STYLE_VOCAB.filter(function(t) {
			return p.indexOf(t) !== -1;
		});
	}

	function learnLikedStyle(prompt) {
		var terms = harvestStyleTerms(prompt);
		if (!terms.length) return [];
		state.imageLikedStyles = state.imageLikedStyles || [];
		var fresh = terms.filter(function(t) {
			return state.imageLikedStyles.indexOf(t) < 0;
		});
		terms.forEach(function(t) {
			var i = state.imageLikedStyles.indexOf(t);
			if (i >= 0) state.imageLikedStyles.splice(i, 1);
			state.imageLikedStyles.unshift(t);
		});
		while (state.imageLikedStyles.length > 6) state.imageLikedStyles.pop();
		persistState();
		return fresh;
	}

	function likedStyleNotes() {
		try {
			var n = (state.imageLikedStyles || []);
			return n.length ? n.join(', ') : '';
		} catch (e) {
			return '';
		}
	}
	var IMG_QUALITY = ['(highly detailed:1.2), sharp focus, intricate, {natural|true-to-life} texture, subtle imperfections', '(highly detailed:1.2), sharp focus, fine detail, {cinematic|editorial|natural|candid} framing, {soft|volumetric|directional} light, depth of field', '(highly detailed:1.3), {cinematic|dramatic|painterly|moody} style, {bold|rich|ethereal} atmosphere, {expressive brushwork|striking composition|chiaroscuro}'];

	function imageStyleString() {
		var id = state.imageStyle || '';
		for (var i = 0; i < IMG_STYLES.length; i++) {
			if (IMG_STYLES[i].id === id) return IMG_STYLES[i].prompt;
		}
		return '';
	}

	function enrichImagePrompt(raw) {
		raw = String(raw || '').trim();
		if (raw.length < 2) return raw;
		var styleStr = imageStyleString();
		if (!state.imagePromptExpand) {
			if (!styleStr) return raw;
			var so = scrubImagePrompt(styleStr + ', ' + raw).slice(0, 400);
			return (so && so.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/ig, '').length >= 3) ? so : raw;
		}
		var cre = Math.max(0, Math.min(2, state.imagePromptCreativity | 0));
		var notes = learnedStyleNotes().replace(/^,\s*/, ''),
			liked = likedStyleNotes();
		var bits = [];
		if (styleStr) bits.push(styleStr);
		bits.push(raw, IMG_QUALITY[cre]);
		if (notes) bits.push(notes);
		if (liked) bits.push(liked);
		var out = scrubImagePrompt(bits.join(', ')).slice(0, 400);
		return (out && out.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/ig, '').length >= 3) ? out : raw;
	}

	function reactionPrompt(desc, emotion) {
		var base = String(desc || 'a warm, thoughtful presence').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 200);
		return 'anime visual novel character sprite, ' + base + ', ' + expressionFor(emotion) + learnedStyleNotes() + ', upper body, facing viewer, simple flat background, soft cel shading, clean lineart';
	}

	function reactionJobs(character) {
		var desc = (character && character.instruction) ? character.instruction : null;
		return reactionEmotions().map(function(emotion) {
			return {
				emotion: emotion,
				prompt: reactionPrompt(desc, emotion)
			};
		});
	}

	function seasonalJob(character, key) {
		if (!SEASONAL[key]) return null;
		var desc = (character && character.instruction) ? character.instruction : null;
		return {
			emotion: key,
			prompt: reactionPrompt(desc, key)
		};
	}

	function activeSeasonal(date) {
		date = date || new Date();
		var cur = (date.getMonth() + 1) * 100 + date.getDate();
		var keys = Object.keys(SEASONAL);
		for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
			var sn = SEASONAL[keys[i]],
				a = sn.from[0] * 100 + sn.from[1],
				b = sn.to[0] * 100 + sn.to[1];
			if (a <= b ? (cur >= a && cur <= b) : (cur >= a || cur <= b)) return keys[i];
		}
		return null;
	}

	function nextReactionJob(jobs, have) {
		var has = {};
		(have || []).forEach(function(e) {
			has[e] = 1;
		});
		for (var i = 0; i < jobs.length; i++) {
			if (!has[jobs[i].emotion]) return jobs[i];
		}
		return null;
	}
	var ReactionStore = (function() {
		var kv = makeKvBlobStore('chloe-solo-reactions', 'reactions');

		function key(charId, emotion) {
			return String(charId || 'default') + ':' + emotion;
		}
		return {
			put: function(charId, emotion, blob, prompt, desc) {
				return kv.put({
					id: key(charId, emotion),
					charId: String(charId || 'default'),
					emotion: emotion,
					blob: blob,
					prompt: prompt || '',
					desc: desc || null,
					updatedAt: Date.now()
				});
			},
			get: function(charId, emotion) {
				return kv.get(key(charId, emotion));
			},
			list: function(charId) {
				var cid = String(charId || 'default');
				return kv.all().then(function(rs) {
					return (rs || []).filter(function(r) {
						return r.charId === cid;
					});
				});
			},
			remove: function(charId, emotion) {
				return kv.del(key(charId, emotion));
			},
			clear: function() {
				return kv.clear();
			}
		};
	})();

	function renderReaction(charId, job) {
		var prompt = (typeof scrubImagePrompt === 'function') ? scrubImagePrompt(job.prompt) : job.prompt;
		return Promise.resolve(paint({
			prompt: prompt,
			resolution: '512x768',
			removeBackground: true
		})).then(function(r) {
			if (!(r && r.ok && r.value)) return {
				ok: false,
				emotion: job.emotion,
				reason: (r && r.reason) || 'no image'
			};
			var blob = r.blob || dataUrlToBlob(String(r.value));
			if (!blob) return {
				ok: false,
				emotion: job.emotion,
				reason: 'not a raster image'
			};
			return ReactionStore.put(charId, job.emotion, blob, job.prompt, job.desc).then(function() {
				return {
					ok: true,
					emotion: job.emotion
				};
			});
		});
	}
	var REACTION_IDLE_MS = 25000,
		reactionBusy = false,
		lastUserActivity = Date.now();

	function noteUserActivity() {
		lastUserActivity = Date.now();
	}

	function reactionCharId() {
		return (state.character && (state.character.id || state.character.name)) ? String(state.character.id || state.character.name) : 'default';
	}

	function reactionIdleTick() {
		if (reactionBusy || demoMode || engineThinking) return;
		if (state.reactionPortraits === false) return;
		if (Date.now() - lastUserActivity < REACTION_IDLE_MS) return;
		if (typeof paint !== 'function') return;
		var character = state.character || null,
			charId = reactionCharId();
		var jobs = reactionJobs(character);
		var sk = activeSeasonal();
		if (sk) {
			var sj = seasonalJob(character, sk);
			if (sj) jobs = jobs.concat(sj);
		}
		reactionBusy = true;
		ReactionStore.list(charId).then(function(have) {
			var next = nextReactionJob(jobs, (have || []).map(function(r) {
				return r.emotion;
			}));
			if (!next) {
				reactionBusy = false;
				return;
			}
			pushThought('[chloe.react] painting the ' + next.emotion + ' portrait while it\u2019s quiet');
			return renderReaction(charId, next).then(function() {
				reactionBusy = false;
				try {
					if (settingsOpenOnTab('character')) renderReactionGallery();
				} catch (e) {}
			});
		}).catch(function() {
			reactionBusy = false;
		});
	}
	var _reactionUrl = null,
		_reactionEmo = null;
	var _faceUrl = null,
		_faceEmo = null,
		lastBotWrap = null;

	function blobToDataUrl(blob) {
		return new Promise(function(res, rej) {
			try {
				var fr = new FileReader();
				fr.onload = function() {
					res(fr.result);
				};
				fr.onerror = function() {
					rej(fr.error);
				};
				fr.readAsDataURL(blob);
			} catch (e) {
				rej(e);
			}
		});
	}
	var BACKUP_DBS = ['chloe-solo-gallery', 'chloe-solo-saves', 'chloe-solo-reactions', 'chloe-solo-characters'];

	function idbOpen(name, version, upgrade) {
		return new Promise(function(res, rej) {
			var r = version ? indexedDB.open(name, version) : indexedDB.open(name);
			if (upgrade) r.onupgradeneeded = function(e) {
				try {
					upgrade(e.target.result, e);
				} catch (err) {}
			};
			r.onsuccess = function(e) {
				res(e.target.result);
			};
			r.onerror = function() {
				rej(r.error);
			};
			r.onblocked = function() {};
		});
	}

	function dataUrlToBlob(durl) {
		try {
			var c = String(durl).indexOf(',');
			if (c < 0) return null;
			var meta = durl.slice(5, c),
				b64 = durl.slice(c + 1),
				type = (meta.split(';')[0]) || 'application/octet-stream';
			var bin = atob(b64),
				n = bin.length,
				u8 = new Uint8Array(n);
			for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) u8[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
			return new Blob([u8], {
				type: type
			});
		} catch (e) {
			return null;
		}
	}

	function dumpDb(name) {
		return idbOpen(name).then(function(db) {
			var stores = Array.prototype.slice.call(db.objectStoreNames);
			if (!stores.length) {
				db.close();
				return null;
			}
			return Promise.all(stores.map(function(sn) {
				return new Promise(function(res) {
					var tx = db.transaction(sn, 'readonly'),
						s = tx.objectStore(sn),
						keyPath = s.keyPath,
						rows = [];
					var cur = s.openCursor();
					cur.onsuccess = function(e) {
						var k = e.target.result;
						if (!k) {
							res({
								name: sn,
								keyPath: keyPath,
								rows: rows
							});
							return;
						}
						rows.push({
							key: (keyPath != null ? undefined : k.key),
							value: k.value
						});
						k.continue();
					};
					cur.onerror = function() {
						res({
							name: sn,
							keyPath: keyPath,
							rows: rows
						});
					};
				});
			})).then(function(sd) {
				db.close();
				return Promise.all(sd.map(function(st) {
					return Promise.all(st.rows.map(function(row) {
						if (row.value instanceof Blob) {
							return blobToDataUrl(row.value).then(function(durl) {
								row.value = {
									__blob: 1,
									durl: durl
								};
							});
						}
						return Promise.resolve();
					})).then(function() {
						return st;
					});
				})).then(function(enc) {
					return {
						db: name,
						stores: enc
					};
				});
			});
		}).catch(function() {
			return null;
		});
	}

	function restoreDb(dump) {
		if (!dump || !dump.db || !Array.isArray(dump.stores)) return Promise.resolve();
		var name = dump.db,
			stores = dump.stores;
		return idbOpen(name).then(function(db) {
			var have = Array.prototype.slice.call(db.objectStoreNames);
			var missing = stores.filter(function(s) {
					return have.indexOf(s.name) < 0;
				}),
				v = db.version;
			db.close();
			var ensure = missing.length ? idbOpen(name, v + 1, function(udb) {
				missing.forEach(function(s) {
					if (!udb.objectStoreNames.contains(s.name)) udb.createObjectStore(s.name, s.keyPath != null ? {
						keyPath: s.keyPath
					} : undefined);
				});
			}) : idbOpen(name);
			return ensure.then(function(db2) {
				return Promise.all(stores.map(function(sd) {
					return new Promise(function(res) {
						if (!db2.objectStoreNames.contains(sd.name)) {
							res();
							return;
						}
						var tx = db2.transaction(sd.name, 'readwrite'),
							s = tx.objectStore(sd.name);
						(sd.rows || []).forEach(function(row) {
							var val = (row.value && row.value.__blob && typeof row.value.durl === 'string') ? dataUrlToBlob(row.value.durl) : row.value;
							try {
								if (s.keyPath != null) s.put(val);
								else s.put(val, row.key);
							} catch (e) {}
						});
						tx.oncomplete = function() {
							res();
						};
						tx.onerror = function() {
							res();
						};
					});
				})).then(function() {
					db2.close();
				});
			});
		}).catch(function() {});
	}

	function herFace() {
		return _faceUrl || state.herAvatar || (state.character && state.character.avatar) || '';
	}

	function paintMsgFace(wrap) {
		if (!wrap) return;
		var who = wrap.querySelector('.who');
		if (!who) return;
		var url = herFace(),
			img = who.querySelector('.spk-av');
		if (!url) {
			if (img && img.parentNode) img.parentNode.removeChild(img);
			return;
		}
		if (!img) {
			img = document.createElement('img');
			img.className = 'spk-av';
			img.alt = '';
			who.insertBefore(img, who.firstChild);
		}
		if (img.getAttribute('src') !== url) img.src = url;
	}

	function forgetReaction() {
		if (_reactionUrl) {
			try {
				URL.revokeObjectURL(_reactionUrl);
			} catch (e) {}
			_reactionUrl = null;
		}
		_reactionEmo = null;
	}

	function revertAvatar() {
		var img = byId('herAvatar');
		if (!img) return;
		forgetReaction();
		var av = state.herAvatar || (state.character && state.character.avatar);
		if (av) {
			img.src = av;
			img.style.display = '';
		} else img.style.display = 'none';
	}

	function showReaction(emotion) {
		if (state.reactionPortraits === false) return;
		var img = byId('herAvatar');
		var emo = emotion || 'normal';
		if (emo === _reactionEmo) {
			paintMsgFace(lastBotWrap);
			return;
		}
		ReactionStore.get(reactionCharId(), emo).then(function(rec) {
			if (!rec || !rec.blob) {
				if (emo !== 'normal') showReaction('normal');
				else {
					_faceUrl = null;
					_faceEmo = null;
					revertAvatar();
					paintMsgFace(lastBotWrap);
				}
				return;
			}
			var url = URL.createObjectURL(rec.blob);
			if (_reactionUrl) {
				try {
					URL.revokeObjectURL(_reactionUrl);
				} catch (e) {}
			}
			_reactionUrl = url;
			_reactionEmo = emo;
			if (img) {
				img.src = url;
				img.style.display = '';
			}
			blobToDataUrl(rec.blob).then(function(durl) {
				_faceUrl = durl;
				_faceEmo = emo;
				paintMsgFace(lastBotWrap);
			}, function() {
				paintMsgFace(lastBotWrap);
			});
		}).catch(function() {});
	}
	var _rxThumbUrls = [];

	function rxBaseDesc() {
		return (state.character && state.character.instruction) ? String(state.character.instruction) : 'a warm, thoughtful presence';
	}

	function renderReactionGallery() {
		var grid = byId('reactionGrid');
		if (!grid) return;
		var pc = byId('reactionPortraitsChk');
		if (pc) pc.checked = state.reactionPortraits !== false;
		var fc = byId('reactionFeedbackChk');
		if (fc) fc.checked = !!state.reactionFeedback;
		_rxThumbUrls.forEach(function(u) {
			try {
				URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
			} catch (e) {}
		});
		_rxThumbUrls = [];
		var charId = reactionCharId();
		var emotions = reactionEmotions();
		var sk = activeSeasonal();
		if (sk) emotions = emotions.concat(sk);
		ReactionStore.list(charId).then(function(recs) {
			var byEmo = {};
			(recs || []).forEach(function(r) {
				byEmo[r.emotion] = r;
			});
			grid.innerHTML = '';
			emotions.forEach(function(emotion) {
				var rec = byEmo[emotion],
					seasonal = !!SEASONAL[emotion];
				var tile = document.createElement('div');
				tile.className = 'rxTile';
				tile.setAttribute('data-emotion', emotion);
				var thumb = document.createElement('div');
				thumb.className = 'rxThumb';
				if (rec && rec.blob) {
					var url = URL.createObjectURL(rec.blob);
					_rxThumbUrls.push(url);
					var im = document.createElement('img');
					im.src = url;
					im.alt = emotion;
					thumb.appendChild(im);
				} else {
					var em = document.createElement('div');
					em.className = 'rxEmpty';
					em.textContent = (state.reactionPortraits === false) ? 'turned off' : (demoMode ? 'needs the image plugin' : 'painting when idle\u2026');
					thumb.appendChild(em);
				}
				var label = document.createElement('div');
				label.className = 'rxLabel';
				label.textContent = emotion.replace('_', ' ');
				if (seasonal) {
					var sp = document.createElement('span');
					sp.className = 'rxSeason';
					sp.textContent = 'seasonal';
					label.appendChild(sp);
				}
				var desc = document.createElement('textarea');
				desc.className = 'rxDesc';
				desc.rows = 2;
				desc.placeholder = rxBaseDesc().slice(0, 80);
				desc.value = (rec && rec.desc) ? rec.desc : '';
				var btn = document.createElement('button');
				btn.className = 'rxRegen';
				btn.textContent = 'Regenerate';
				btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
					regenerateReaction(emotion, desc.value, btn);
				});
				tile.appendChild(thumb);
				tile.appendChild(label);
				tile.appendChild(desc);
				tile.appendChild(btn);
				grid.appendChild(tile);
			});
		}).catch(function() {});
	}

	function regenerateReaction(emotion, descText, btn) {
		if (demoMode) {
			toast('Connect the image plugin on Perchance to paint reactions', 'info');
			return;
		}
		var desc = String(descText || '').trim(),
			charId = reactionCharId(),
			base = rxBaseDesc();
		var job = {
			emotion: emotion,
			desc: desc || null,
			prompt: reactionPrompt(desc || base, emotion)
		};
		if (btn) {
			btn.disabled = true;
			btn.textContent = 'painting\u2026';
		}
		if (state.reactionFeedback && desc) learnFromEdit(desc);
		Promise.resolve(renderReaction(charId, job)).then(function(res) {
			if (btn) {
				btn.disabled = false;
				btn.textContent = 'Regenerate';
			}
			if (res && res.ok) {
				toast('Repainted ' + emotion.replace('_', ' '), 'success');
				renderReactionGallery();
			} else toast((res && res.reason) ? res.reason : 'no image - connect the image plugin', 'error');
		});
	}

	function addStyleNote(desc) {
		desc = String(desc || '').trim();
		if (!desc) return false;
		state.imageStyleNotes = state.imageStyleNotes || [];
		var norm = desc.toLowerCase();
		if (state.imageStyleNotes.some(function(n) {
				return String(n).toLowerCase() === norm;
			})) return false;
		state.imageStyleNotes.push(desc);
		while (state.imageStyleNotes.length > 8) state.imageStyleNotes.shift();
		persistState();
		return true;
	}

	function removeStyleNote(desc) {
		state.imageStyleNotes = (state.imageStyleNotes || []).filter(function(n) {
			return n !== desc;
		});
		persistState();
	}

	function learnFromEdit(desc) {
		desc = String(desc || '').trim();
		if (!addStyleNote(desc)) return;
		try {
			if (engine && engine.addFacts) engine.addFacts(USER_ID, [{
				text: 'Prefers ' + herName() + ' portrayed as: ' + desc,
				importance: 6
			}], 'image-feedback');
		} catch (e) {}
		pushThought('[chloe.learn] a look to keep in mind: ' + desc);
	}

	function renderLearnedLooks() {
		var box = byId('learnedLooksList');
		if (!box) return;
		var notes = state.imageStyleNotes || [];
		box.innerHTML = '';
		if (!notes.length) {
			var e = document.createElement('div');
			e.className = 'learnEmpty';
			e.textContent = 'Nothing learned yet — turn on Provide feedback when you edit a reaction, or teach her a look below.';
			box.appendChild(e);
			return;
		}
		notes.forEach(function(n) {
			var chip = document.createElement('span');
			chip.className = 'learnChip';
			var t = document.createElement('span');
			t.textContent = n;
			chip.appendChild(t);
			var x = document.createElement('button');
			x.className = 'learnX';
			x.textContent = '×';
			x.title = 'forget this look';
			x.addEventListener('click', function() {
				removeStyleNote(n);
				renderLearnedLooks();
			});
			chip.appendChild(x);
			box.appendChild(chip);
		});
	}
	var PIN_ACTIVE = 3;

	function renderPinned() {
		var box = byId('pinList');
		if (!box) return;
		var clr = byId('pinClearBtn');
		if (!engine || typeof engine.listHighlights !== 'function') {
			box.innerHTML = '';
			if (clr) clr.style.display = 'none';
			return;
		}
		Promise.resolve(engine.listHighlights()).then(function(list) {
			list = list || [];
			box.innerHTML = '';
			if (clr) {
				clr.style.display = list.length ? '' : 'none';
				clr.textContent = 'Clear all';
				clr.__armed = false;
			}
			if (!list.length) {
				var e = document.createElement('div');
				e.className = 'pinEmpty';
				e.textContent = 'Nothing pinned yet - tap \u2605 on any of her lines to keep it in mind.';
				box.appendChild(e);
				return;
			}
			list.slice().reverse().forEach(function(h, idx) {
				var item = document.createElement('div');
				item.className = 'pinItem' + (idx < PIN_ACTIVE ? ' active' : '');
				var txt = document.createElement('div');
				txt.className = 'pinTxt';
				var main = document.createElement('span');
				main.textContent = '\u201c' + String(h.text || '') + '\u201d';
				txt.appendChild(main);
				var bits = [];
				if (h.authorName) bits.push(h.authorName);
				if (h.note) bits.push(h.note);
				if (idx < PIN_ACTIVE) bits.push('in her context now');
				if (bits.length) {
					var meta = document.createElement('span');
					meta.className = 'pinMeta';
					meta.textContent = bits.join(' \u00b7 ');
					txt.appendChild(meta);
				}
				item.appendChild(txt);
				var x = document.createElement('button');
				x.className = 'pinX';
				x.type = 'button';
				x.textContent = '\u00d7';
				x.title = 'Unpin';
				x.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Unpin');
				x.addEventListener('click', function() {
					unpinHighlight(h.id);
				});
				item.appendChild(x);
				box.appendChild(item);
			});
		});
	}

	function unpinHighlight(id) {
		if (engine && typeof engine.removeHighlight === 'function') Promise.resolve(engine.removeHighlight(id)).then(function() {
			renderPinned();
		});
		else renderPinned();
	}

	function renderAdaptation() {
		var wrap = byId('adaptWrap');
		if (!wrap) return;
		if (!engine || typeof engine.feedbackSnapshot !== 'function') {
			wrap.textContent = '';
			return;
		}
		engine.feedbackSnapshot().then(function(s) {
			wrap.innerHTML = '';
			if (!s.enabled) {
				var off = document.createElement('div');
				off.className = 'adaptEmpty';
				off.textContent = 'Style learning is off — she keeps a steady voice. Turn on feedback learning to let her adapt to what lands with you.';
				wrap.appendChild(off);
				return;
			}
			[
				['Length', s.length],
				['Tone', s.tone]
			].forEach(function(pair) {
				var d = pair[1];
				var dim = document.createElement('div');
				dim.className = 'adaptDim';
				var head = document.createElement('div');
				head.className = 'adaptHead';
				var ttl = document.createElement('span');
				ttl.className = 'tl';
				ttl.textContent = pair[0];
				head.appendChild(ttl);
				var lean = document.createElement('span');
				lean.className = 'adaptLean';
				if (d.lean && d.lean.label) {
					lean.textContent = (d.lean.mode === 'explore' ? 'trying ' + d.lean.label : 'leaning ' + d.lean.label);
					lean.classList.add(d.lean.mode === 'explore' ? 'isExplore' : 'isExploit');
				} else {
					lean.textContent = 'no clear preference yet';
				}
				head.appendChild(lean);
				dim.appendChild(head);
				d.arms.forEach(function(a) {
					var row = document.createElement('div');
					row.className = 'adaptRow';
					var lbl = document.createElement('div');
					lbl.className = 'adaptLbl';
					lbl.textContent = a.label;
					row.appendChild(lbl);
					var bar = document.createElement('div');
					bar.className = 'adaptBar';
					var fill = document.createElement('div');
					fill.className = 'adaptFill';
					fill.style.width = Math.round((Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, a.r)) + 1) / 2 * 100) + '%';
					if (a.r < 0) fill.classList.add('neg');
					bar.appendChild(fill);
					row.appendChild(bar);
					var nn = document.createElement('div');
					nn.className = 'adaptN';
					nn.textContent = a.n + (a.n === 1 ? ' signal' : ' signals');
					row.appendChild(nn);
					dim.appendChild(row);
				});
				wrap.appendChild(dim);
			});
		});
	}
	var Saves = (function() {
		var DB = 'chloe-solo-saves',
			SLOTS = 'slots',
			dbP = null;

		function openDb() {
			if (dbP) return dbP;
			dbP = new Promise(function(res, rej) {
				var r = indexedDB.open(DB, 1);
				r.onupgradeneeded = function(e) {
					var db = e.target.result;
					if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(SLOTS)) db.createObjectStore(SLOTS, {
						keyPath: 'id'
					});
				};
				r.onsuccess = function(e) {
					res(e.target.result);
				};
				r.onerror = function(e) {
					dbP = null;
					rej(e.target.error);
				};
			});
			return dbP;
		}

		function op(mode, fn) {
			return openDb().then(function(db) {
				return new Promise(function(res, rej) {
					var tx = db.transaction(SLOTS, mode),
						s = tx.objectStore(SLOTS),
						q = fn(s);
					if (q && 'onsuccess' in q) {
						q.onsuccess = function() {
							res(q.result);
						};
						q.onerror = function() {
							rej(q.error);
						};
					} else {
						tx.oncomplete = function() {
							res();
						};
						tx.onerror = function() {
							rej(tx.error);
						};
					}
				});
			});
		}
		return {
			list: function() {
				return op('readonly', function(s) {
					return s.getAll();
				}).then(function(a) {
					return sortSlotsNewestFirst(a || []);
				});
			},
			put: function(slot) {
				return op('readwrite', function(s) {
					return s.put(slot);
				});
			},
			del: function(id) {
				return op('readwrite', function(s) {
					return s.delete(id);
				});
			},
			get: function(id) {
				return op('readonly', function(s) {
					return s.get(id);
				});
			}
		};
	})();

	function sortSlotsNewestFirst(a) {
		return a.slice().sort(function(x, y) {
			return (y.ts || 0) - (x.ts || 0);
		});
	}

	function autosToPrune(all, keep) {
		var autos = sortSlotsNewestFirst(all.filter(function(s) {
			return s.kind === 'auto';
		}));
		return autos.slice(Math.max(1, keep || 1)).map(function(s) {
			return s.id;
		});
	}

	function snapshotNS() {
		var d = {};
		try {
			for (var i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
				var k = localStorage.key(i);
				if (k && k.indexOf(NS) === 0) d[k] = localStorage.getItem(k);
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		return d;
	}

	function makeSlot(kind, label) {
		return {
			id: kind.charAt(0) + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5),
			kind: kind,
			label: String(label || '').slice(0, 80),
			ts: Date.now(),
			msgCount: (queue ? queue.length : 0),
			version: SOLO_VERSION,
			data: snapshotNS()
		};
	}

	function saveSlot(kind, label) {
		var slot = makeSlot(kind, label);
		return Saves.put(slot).then(function() {
			return (kind === 'auto') ? pruneAuto().then(function() {
				return slot;
			}) : slot;
		});
	}

	function pruneAuto() {
		return Saves.list().then(function(all) {
			return Promise.all(autosToPrune(all, state.autoSaveKeep || 10).map(function(id) {
				return Saves.del(id);
			}));
		});
	}

	function restoreSlot(id) {
		return Saves.get(id).then(function(slot) {
			if (!slot || !slot.data) return false;
			var del = [];
			try {
				for (var i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
					var k = localStorage.key(i);
					if (k && k.indexOf(NS) === 0) del.push(k);
				}
			} catch (e) {}
			del.forEach(function(k) {
				try {
					localStorage.removeItem(k);
				} catch (e) {}
			});
			Object.keys(slot.data).forEach(function(k) {
				try {
					localStorage.setItem(k, slot.data[k]);
				} catch (e) {}
			});
			location.reload();
			return true;
		});
	}
	var autoTimer = null,
		lastAutoCount = -1;

	function startAutoSave() {
		if (autoTimer) {
			clearInterval(autoTimer);
			autoTimer = null;
		}
		if (!state.autoSave) return;
		var mins = Math.max(1, state.autoSaveMins || 10);
		autoTimer = setInterval(function() {
			if (!queue || queue.length === lastAutoCount) return;
			lastAutoCount = queue.length;
			saveSlot('auto').then(function() {
				if (settingsOpenOnTab('character')) renderSaves();
			}).catch(function() {});
		}, mins * 60 * 1000);
	}

	function dataUrlToBlob(dataUrl) {
		try {
			var parts = String(dataUrl).split(',');
			var head = parts[0] || '',
				b64 = parts[1] || '';
			var mime = (head.match(/data:([^;]+)/) || [])[1] || 'image/png';
			var bin = atob(b64),
				n = bin.length,
				u8 = new Uint8Array(n);
			while (n--) u8[n] = bin.charCodeAt(n);
			return new Blob([u8], {
				type: mime
			});
		} catch (e) {
			return null;
		}
	}

	function extractPrompt(caption) {
		var m = String(caption || '').match(/[\u201c"]([^\u201d"]+)[\u201d"]/);
		return m ? m[1] : '';
	}

	function scrubImagePrompt(s) {
		s = String(s == null ? '' : s);
		var guard = 0;
		while (guard++ < 50 && /\{[^{}]*\}|\[[^\[\]]*\]/.test(s)) {
			s = s.replace(/\{([^{}]*)\}|\[([^\[\]]*)\]/g, function(m, c1, c2) {
				var inner = (c1 != null) ? c1 : c2;
				if (inner.indexOf('|') !== -1) {
					var o = inner.split('|');
					return o[Math.floor(Math.random() * o.length)];
				}
				var r = inner.match(/^\s*(-?\d+)\s*-\s*(-?\d+)\s*$/);
				if (r) {
					var a = +r[1],
						b = +r[2];
					if (a > b) {
						var t = a;
						a = b;
						b = t;
					}
					return String(a + Math.floor(Math.random() * (b - a + 1)));
				}
				return inner;
			});
		}
		return s.replace(/[{}\[\]]/g, ' ').replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ').trim();
	}
	try {
		window.__chloeScrubPrompt = scrubImagePrompt;
	} catch (e) {}
	var VARY_TAGS = ['soft detail', 'fine detail', 'crisp detail', 'natural light', 'gentle light', 'subtle shading', 'clean rendering', 'balanced tones'];

	function varyPrompt(p, n) {
		return String(p || '') + ', ' + VARY_TAGS[((n || 0) % VARY_TAGS.length + VARY_TAGS.length) % VARY_TAGS.length];
	}

	function cleanImageText(s) {
		var t = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\{\{\s*user\s*\}\}/gi, state.userName || 'You').replace(/\{\{\s*char\s*\}\}/gi, herName());
		return scrubImagePrompt(t);
	}

	function persistImage(dataUrl, caption, blob) {
		var d = String(dataUrl || '');
		if (d.indexOf('data:image/') !== 0 || d.indexOf('data:image/svg') === 0) return Promise.resolve(null);
		blob = blob || dataUrlToBlob(d);
		if (!blob) return Promise.resolve(null);
		return Gallery.init().then(function() {
			return Gallery.store(blob, {
				prompt: extractPrompt(caption)
			});
		}).then(function(id) {
			toast('Saved to gallery', 'success');
			if (galleryOpen) renderGallery();
			return id || null;
		}, function() {
			return null;
		});
	}

	function soloSendImage(chId, dataUrl, caption, blob) {
		setThinking(false);
		dropLiveOrphan();
		var cap = cleanImageText(caption);
		var m = enqueue(BOT_ID, herName(), true, cap || '(shared an image)');
		var w = renderMessage('her', herName(), cap, {
			image: dataUrl,
			mid: m.id
		});
		if (w) w.setAttribute('data-cid', BOT_ID);
		persistImage(dataUrl, cap, blob).then(function(id) {
			if (id) {
				m.image = id;
				persistQueue();
			}
		});
		liveActive = false;
		replyExpected = false;
		return Promise.resolve(true);
	}
	var engine = null,
		engineThinking = false;

	function narrativeLull(ctx) {
		try {
			if (!window.ChloeFrame || !window.ChloeFrame.drives(state.frame)) return {
				ok: false
			};
			var sinceUser = 0;
			for (var i = queue.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
				if (queue[i].author && queue[i].author.bot) sinceUser++;
				else break;
			}
			if (sinceUser >= 3) return {
				ok: false
			};
			var ai = null;
			try {
				ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin);
			} catch (e) {}
			if (typeof ai !== 'function') return {
				ok: false
			};
			var lines = (ctx && ctx.channelRecent) ? ctx.channelRecent : [];
			var transcript = lines.map(function(l) {
				return l.who + ': ' + l.text;
			}).join('\n');
			if (!transcript) {
				transcript = queue.slice(-12).filter(function(x) {
					return x && x.content;
				}).map(function(x) {
					return (x.author && x.author.bot ? (x.author.name || herName()) : userNameStr()) + ': ' + x.content;
				}).join('\n');
			}
			if (!transcript) return {
				ok: false
			};
			var pin = (state.character && state.character.instruction) || 'a warm companion who remembers';
			var instr = window.ChloeFrame.beatInstruction({
				frame: state.frame,
				who: herName(),
				pin: pin,
				transcript: transcript
			});
			if (!instr) return {
				ok: false
			};
			return Promise.resolve(ai({
				instruction: instr,
				startWith: '',
				stopSequences: ['\n\n[' + '[', '\n[' + '[']
			})).then(function(r) {
				var text = (typeof cleanReply === 'function') ? cleanReply(r) : String(r || '').trim();
				return text ? {
					ok: true,
					value: text
				} : {
					ok: false
				};
			}, function() {
				return {
					ok: false
				};
			});
		} catch (e) {
			return {
				ok: false
			};
		}
	}

	function frameDrivesNow() {
		try {
			return !!(window.ChloeFrame && window.ChloeFrame.drives(state.frame));
		} catch (e) {
			return false;
		}
	}

	function brainHooks() {
		return {
			respond: respond,
			judge: judge,
			paint: paint,
			recapFn: recap,
			editPrompt: editPrompt,
			factFn: facts,
			summaryFn: channelSummary,
			episodeFn: episodes,
			reflectFn: reflect,
			consolidateFn: consolidate,
			decomposeFn: decompose,
			subAnswerFn: subanswer,
			reduceFn: reduce,
			mapFn: function(list) {
				return Promise.all((list || []).map(function(job) {
					return Promise.resolve(subanswer(job));
				}));
			},
			greetFn: greet,
			lullFn: lull,
			checkinFn: checkin,
			beatFn: beat
		};
	}

	var extraEngines = {};
	var turnPlan = {
		content: null,
		allowed: {},
		depth: 0

	function activeExtras() {
		return (state.cast || []).filter(function(c) {
			return c && c.active !== false;
		});
	}

	function botInScene() {
		return state.botActive !== false;
	}

	function activeMembers() {
		var out = [];
		if (botInScene()) out.push({
			id: BOT_ID,
			name: herName(),
			aliases: state.botAliases || [],
			isBot: true
		});
		activeExtras().forEach(function(c) {
			out.push({
				id: c.id,
				name: c.name,
				aliases: c.aliases || [],
				isBot: false,
				ref: c
			});
		});
		return out;
	}

	function sceneRoster() {
		var ms = activeMembers();
		if (ms.length < 2) return '';
		var bits = ms.map(function(m) {
			var instr = m.isBot ? ((state.character && state.character.instruction) || '') : ((m.ref && m.ref.instruction) || '');
			var flat = String(instr || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(),
				cut = flat.match(/^[^.!?]{0,90}/);
			var gist = (cut ? cut[0] : flat).trim();
			return m.name + (gist ? (' - ' + gist) : '');
		});
		return 'Characters present in this scene: ' + bits.join('; ') + '. Each is their own person with their own memory - speak only as yourself, and address the others by name when it fits.';
	}

	function primaryIdEff() {
		var act = activeMembers();
		if (state.primaryId && act.some(function(m) {
				return m.id === state.primaryId;
			})) return state.primaryId;
		return act.length ? act[0].id : BOT_ID;
	}

	function isGroup() {
		return activeMembers().length > 1 || !botInScene();
	}
	var NAME_TITLES = {
		doctor: 1,
		dr: 1,
		captain: 1,
		capt: 1,
		mr: 1,
		mrs: 1,
		ms: 1,
		miss: 1,
		lady: 1,
		lord: 1,
		sir: 1,
		madam: 1,
		madame: 1,
		professor: 1,
		prof: 1,
		master: 1,
		mistress: 1,
		the: 1,
		king: 1,
		queen: 1,
		prince: 1,

		saint: 1,
		st: 1,
		general: 1,

		agent: 1,
		officer: 1
	};

	function nameTokens(name, aliases) {
		var full = String(name || '').trim();
		if (!full) return [];
		var out = [full],
			words = full.split(/\s+/),
			personal = null;
		for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
			if (!NAME_TITLES[words[i].toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, '')]) {
				personal = words[i];
				break;
			}
		}
		if (personal && personal.length >= 2 && personal.toLowerCase() !== full.toLowerCase()) out.push(personal);
		(aliases || []).forEach(function(a) {
			a = String(a || '').trim();
			if (a) out.push(a);
		});
		var seen = {},
			dedup = [];
		out.forEach(function(t) {
			var k = t.toLowerCase();
			if (!seen[k]) {
				seen[k] = 1;
				dedup.push(t);
			}
		});
		return dedup;
	}

	function castRoster() {
		var pid = primaryIdEff();
		return activeMembers().map(function(m) {
			return {
				id: m.id,
				name: m.name,
				primary: m.id === pid,
				tokens: nameTokens(m.name, m.aliases)
			};
		});
	}

	function namedIn(text, name) {
		try {
			return new RegExp('(^|[^a-z0-9_])' + name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + '([^a-z0-9_]|$)', 'i').test(String(text || ''));
		} catch (e) {
			return false;
		}
	}
