	function atTagged(content, token) {
		try {
			return new RegExp('@' + token.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + '(\\b|$)', 'i').test(content);
		} catch (e) {
			return false;
		}
	}

	function addressedBy(content, member) {
		var c = String(content || '');
		if (member.id && c.indexOf('<@' + member.id + '>') >= 0) return true;
		var toks = member.tokens || nameTokens(member.name, member.aliases);
		return toks.some(function(t) {
			if (atTagged(c, t)) return true;
			if (COMMON_WORDS[t.toLowerCase()]) return false;
			return namedIn(c, t);
		});
	}

	function firstMentionIndex(content, member) {
		var c = String(content || '').toLowerCase(),
			best = Infinity,
			i;
		if (member.id) {
			i = c.indexOf('<@' + member.id.toLowerCase() + '>');
			if (i >= 0) best = Math.min(best, i);
		}
		(member.tokens || []).forEach(function(t) {
			i = c.indexOf(t.toLowerCase());
			if (i >= 0) best = Math.min(best, i);
		});
		return best;
	}
	var CastWeigher = {
		BASE: 50,
		RECENT_PENALTY: 0.6,
		QUIET_BOOST: 1.2,
		RECENT_T: 2,
		QUIET_T: 5,
		BOOST: [{
			v: 30,
			w: ['confident', 'outgoing', 'talkative', 'energetic', 'lively', 'expressive', 'animated', 'enthusiastic', 'bubbly', 'boisterous']
		}, {
			v: 20,
			w: ['social', 'friendly', 'warm', 'engaging', 'welcoming', 'gregarious']
		}],
		PENALTY: [{
			v: -20,
			w: ['reserved', 'quiet', 'shy', 'introverted', 'withdrawn', 'timid', 'bashful']
		}, {
			v: -15,
			w: ['serious', 'stoic', 'grumpy', 'cynical', 'solemn', 'grim']
		}],
		_clamp: function(x) {
			return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, x));
		},
		fromPersona: function(desc) {
			if (!desc || typeof desc !== 'string') return this.BASE;
			var d = desc.toLowerCase(),
				weight = this.BASE;
			[].concat(this.BOOST, this.PENALTY).forEach(function(g) {
				g.w.forEach(function(k) {
					try {
						if (new RegExp('\\b' + k + '\\b', 'i').test(d)) weight += g.v;
					} catch (e) {}
				});
			});
			return this._clamp(weight);
		},
		baseWeight: function(m) {
			return (m && m.idleWeight != null) ? this._clamp(m.idleWeight) : this.fromPersona(m && m.instruction || '');
		},
		_lastSpoke: function(id, hist) {
			for (var i = hist.length - 1; i >= 0; i--)
				if (hist[i] === id) return i;
			return -1;
		},
		recencyAdjust: function(base, id, hist) {
			if (!hist || !hist.length) return base;
			var last = this._lastSpoke(id, hist),
				since = last === -1 ? hist.length : hist.length - 1 - last,
				adj = base;
			if (since <= this.RECENT_T) adj = Math.floor(base * this.RECENT_PENALTY);
			else if (since >= this.QUIET_T) adj = Math.floor(base * this.QUIET_BOOST);
			return this._clamp(adj);
		},
		selectNext: function(members, hist, userId, rng) {
			rng = rng || Math.random;
			var self = this,
				pool = (members || []).filter(function(m) {
					return m.id !== userId;
				});
			if (!pool.length) return null;
			var weights = pool.map(function(m) {
				return {
					id: m.id,
					w: self.recencyAdjust(self.baseWeight(m), m.id, hist || [])
				};
			});
			var total = weights.reduce(function(s, x) {
				return s + x.w;
			}, 0);
			if (total === 0) return pool[Math.floor(rng() * pool.length)].id;
			var r = rng() * total;
			for (var i = 0; i < weights.length; i++) {
				r -= weights[i].w;
				if (r <= 0) return weights[i].id;
			}
			return weights[weights.length - 1].id;
		}
	};

	function speakerHistory() {
		return queue.filter(function(m) {
			return m && m.content;
		}).slice(-12).map(function(m) {
			return (m.author && m.author.bot) ? m.author.id : USER_ID;
		});
	}

	function weigherMembers() {
		return activeMembers().map(function(m) {
			var ref = m.ref || {};
			return {
				id: m.id,
				instruction: m.isBot ? ((state.character && state.character.instruction) || '') : (ref.instruction || ''),
				idleWeight: m.isBot ? (state.botIdleWeight != null ? state.botIdleWeight : null) : (ref.idleWeight != null ? ref.idleWeight : null)
			};
		});
	}

	function planTurn(msg) {
		var roster = castRoster(),
			fromHuman = !(msg.author && msg.author.bot),
			content = String(msg.content || ''),
			allowed = {};
		var named = roster.filter(function(r) {
			return r.id !== msg.author.id && addressedBy(content, r);
		});
		var maxSp = Math.max(1, state.castMaxSpeakers || 2);
		if (fromHuman) {
			turnPlan.depth = 0;
			if (named.length) {
				named.sort(function(a, b) {
					return firstMentionIndex(content, a) - firstMentionIndex(content, b);
				}).slice(0, maxSp).forEach(function(r) {
					allowed[r.id] = 1;
				});
			} else {
				var mode = state.castReplyMode || 'primary';
				if (mode === 'everyone') {
					castRoster().slice(0, maxSp).forEach(function(r) {
						allowed[r.id] = 1;
					});
				} else if (mode === 'roundrobin') {
					var pick = CastWeigher.selectNext(weigherMembers(), speakerHistory(), USER_ID);
					allowed[pick || primaryIdEff()] = 1;
				} else {
					allowed[primaryIdEff()] = 1;
				}
			}
		} else {
			if (named.length && turnPlan.depth < (state.castMaxFollowups || 2)) {
				turnPlan.depth++;
				named.slice(0, maxSp).forEach(function(r) {
					allowed[r.id] = 1;
				});
			} else if (state.castChain && state.castReplyMode === 'roundrobin' && turnPlan.depth < (state.castChainMax || 3)) {
				var nextPick = CastWeigher.selectNext(weigherMembers().filter(function(m) {
					return m.id !== msg.author.id;
				}), speakerHistory(), USER_ID);
				if (nextPick) {
					turnPlan.depth++;
					allowed[nextPick] = 1;
				}
			}
		}
		turnPlan = {
			content: content,
			allowed: allowed,
			depth: turnPlan.depth
		};
	}

	function engageDecideFor(charId) {
		return function(content) {
			if (charId === BOT_ID && !botInScene()) return false;
			if (charId === BOT_ID && state.nation && state.nation.useForReplies && window.ChloeNation) return false;
			if (!isGroup()) return undefined;
			if (String(content || '') !== turnPlan.content) return false;
			return !!turnPlan.allowed[charId];
		};
	}
	var CAST_PALETTE = ['#2E7DD1', '#2E9E7B', '#C0562B', '#8A5BD0', '#B8902A', '#C2417A', '#3C8C8C', '#6B7A2E', '#5B62E0', '#A23E8C'];

	function castColor(seed) {
		var h = 0;
		seed = String(seed || '');
		for (var i = 0; i < seed.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + seed.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
		return CAST_PALETTE[h % CAST_PALETTE.length];
	}

	function styleSpeaker(wrap, cid, avatar) {
		if (!wrap) return;
		var color = colorFor(cid);
		wrap.classList.add('hascolor');
		wrap.style.setProperty('--speaker', color || 'var(--accent-ink)');
		var who = wrap.querySelector('.who');
		if (who) who.style.color = color || '';
		if (avatar && who && !who.querySelector('.spk-av')) {
			var img = document.createElement('img');
			img.className = 'spk-av';
			img.src = avatar;
			img.alt = '';
			who.insertBefore(img, who.firstChild);
		}
	}

	function speakerNameFor(m) {
		var isBot = !!(m && m.author && m.author.bot);
		return isBot ? ((m.author && m.author.username) || state.botName || 'Chloe') : ((m && m.author && m.author.username) || state.userName || 'You');
	}

	function avatarForCid(cid) {
		if (cid === BOT_ID) return state.herAvatar || (state.character && state.character.avatar) || '';
		var c = (state.cast || []).filter(function(x) {
			return x.id === cid;
		})[0];
		return (c && c.avatar) || '';
	}

	function makeSendFor(c) {
		return function(chId, body) {
			setThinking(false);
			var text = scrubTokens(body);
			if (text) {
				var m = enqueue(c.id, c.name, true, text);
				var w = renderMessage('her', c.name, text, {
					mid: m.id
				});
				if (w) {
					w.setAttribute('data-cid', c.id);
					styleSpeaker(w, c.id, c.avatar);
				}
			}
			return Promise.resolve(true);
		};
	}
	var memViewId = BOT_ID;

	function viewEngine() {
		var e = extraEngines[memViewId];
		return (memViewId && memViewId !== BOT_ID && e) ? e.engine : engine;
	}

	function viewStore() {
		var e = extraEngines[memViewId];
		return (memViewId && memViewId !== BOT_ID && e) ? e.store : store;
	}

	function viewChar() {
		return (memViewId === BOT_ID) ? (state.character || null) : ((state.cast || []).filter(function(x) {
			return x.id === memViewId;
		})[0] || null);
	}

	function viewName() {
		var c = viewChar();
		return (memViewId === BOT_ID) ? herName() : (c ? c.name : herName());
	}

	function buildEngine() {
		if (engine && engine.stop) {
			try {
				engine.stop();
			} catch (e) {}
		}
		Object.keys(extraEngines).forEach(function(id) {
			var e = extraEngines[id];
			if (e && e.engine && e.engine.stop) {
				try {
					e.engine.stop();
				} catch (x) {}
			}
		});
		extraEngines = {};
		engine = ChloeT0.createEngine({
			transport: transport,
			store: store,
			clock: {
				now: Date.now
			},
			log: onEngineLog,
			config: buildConfig()
		});
		registerLorebook(engine);
		if (botInScene()) engine.start();
		activeExtras().forEach(function(c) {
			try {
				var st = makeStore(NS + 'c:' + c.id + ':');
				var ex = ChloeT0.createEngine({
					transport: transport,
					store: st,
					clock: {
						now: Date.now
					},
					log: function() {},
					config: buildConfig(c, st)
				});
				registerLorebook(ex);
				ensureUserPartitionIn(st);
				ex.start();
				extraEngines[c.id] = {
					engine: ex,
					store: st,
					char: c
				};
			} catch (e) {
				pushThought('[scene] could not start ' + (c && c.name) + ': ' + (e && e.message));
			}
		});
		if (!activeMembers().some(function(m) {
				return m.id === memViewId;
			})) memViewId = primaryIdEff();
	}

	function slugId(name) {
		return 'c-' + String(name || 'char').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '').slice(0, 24) + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
	}

	function addToScene(ch) {
		if (!ch || !ch.name) return false;
		if (!state.cast) state.cast = [];
		state.cast.push({
			id: slugId(ch.name),
			name: String(ch.name).slice(0, 60),
			instruction: String(ch.instruction || '').slice(0, 8000),
			avatar: String(ch.avatar || ''),
			examples: String(ch.examples || '').slice(0, 6000),
			aliases: Array.isArray(ch.aliases) ? ch.aliases.slice(0, 24) : [],
			idleWeight: (ch.idleWeight == null ? null : ch.idleWeight),
			active: true
		});
		persistState();
		buildEngine();
		renderRoster();
		updateCharLabel();
		toast(ch.name + ' joined the scene', 'success');
		return true;
	}

	function renderRoster() {
		var box = byId('rosterList');
		if (!box) return;
		box.innerHTML = '';
		var pid = primaryIdEff(),
			activeCount = activeMembers().length;

		function avatarEl(name, color, src) {
			if (src) {
				var im = document.createElement('img');
				im.className = 'roster-av';
				im.src = src;
				im.alt = '';
				return im;
			}
			var d = document.createElement('div');
			d.className = 'roster-av';
			d.textContent = (String(name || '?').trim()[0] || '?').toUpperCase();
			if (color) d.style.background = color;
			return d;
		}

		function refreshAll() {
			persistState();
			buildEngine();
			renderRoster();
			renderMemPicker();
			updateCharLabel();
		}

		function row(m) {
			var color = colorFor(m.id);
			var src = m.isBot ? (state.herAvatar || (state.character && state.character.avatar) || '') : (m.ref.avatar || '');
			var isActive = m.isBot ? true : (m.ref.active !== false);
			var r = document.createElement('div');
			r.className = 'roster-row' + (isActive ? '' : ' off');
			var top = document.createElement('div');
			top.className = 'roster-top';
			top.appendChild(avatarEl(m.name, color, src));
			var n = document.createElement('input');
			n.type = 'text';
			n.className = 'roster-name-in';
			n.value = m.name;
			if (color) n.style.color = color;
			n.title = 'Rename';
			n.addEventListener('change', function() {
				var v = n.value.trim();
				if (!v) {
					n.value = m.name;
					return;
				}
				if (m.isBot) state.botName = v;
				else m.ref.name = v;
				refreshAll();
			});
			top.appendChild(n);
			if (m.id === pid) {
				var tag = document.createElement('span');
				tag.className = 'roster-tag';
				tag.textContent = 'primary';
				tag.title = 'Answers when you don\u2019t name anyone';
				top.appendChild(tag);
			} else {
				var mp = document.createElement('button');
				mp.className = 'roster-mp';
				mp.textContent = 'Make primary';
				mp.title = 'Answers when you don\u2019t name anyone';
				mp.addEventListener('click', function() {
					if (!m.isBot) m.ref.active = true;
					state.primaryId = m.id;
					refreshAll();
					toast(m.name + ' now leads the scene', 'success');
				});
				top.appendChild(mp);
			}
			var del = document.createElement('button');
			del.className = 'roster-del';
			del.textContent = '\u2715';
			var canRemove = activeCount > 1 || !isActive;
			del.disabled = !canRemove;
			del.title = canRemove ? (m.isBot ? 'Take the default character out of the scene' : 'Remove this character') : 'Add or enable another character first';
			del.addEventListener('click', function() {
				if (!canRemove) return;
				if (m.isBot) state.botActive = false;
				else {
					var i = state.cast.indexOf(m.ref);
					if (i >= 0) state.cast.splice(i, 1);
				}
				if (state.primaryId === m.id) state.primaryId = null;
				refreshAll();
				toast(m.name + ' left the scene');
			});
			top.appendChild(del);
			r.appendChild(top);
			var ctrls = document.createElement('div');
			ctrls.className = 'roster-ctrls';
			if (!m.isBot) {
				var lab = document.createElement('label');
				lab.className = 'roster-act';
				var cb = document.createElement('input');
				cb.type = 'checkbox';
				cb.checked = isActive;
				cb.addEventListener('change', function() {
					m.ref.active = cb.checked;
					if (!cb.checked && state.primaryId === m.id) state.primaryId = null;
					refreshAll();
				});
				lab.appendChild(cb);
				lab.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' in scene'));
				ctrls.appendChild(lab);
			}
			var cw = document.createElement('label');
			cw.className = 'roster-color-wrap';
			var ci = document.createElement('input');
			ci.type = 'color';
			ci.className = 'roster-color';
			ci.title = 'Highlight colour';
			ci.value = colorFor(m.id) || (m.isBot ? (state.accent || '#5B62E0') : castColor(m.id));
			ci.addEventListener('input', function() {
				if (m.isBot) state.botColor = ci.value;
				else m.ref.color = ci.value;
				persistState();
				recolorMessages();
				renderMemPicker();
			});
			cw.appendChild(ci);
			cw.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' colour'));
			ctrls.appendChild(cw);
			var al = document.createElement('input');
			al.type = 'text';
			al.className = 'roster-alias';
			al.placeholder = 'also answers to\u2026 (comma-separated)';
			al.value = (m.isBot ? (state.botAliases || []) : (m.ref.aliases || [])).join(', ');
			al.addEventListener('change', function() {
				var arr = al.value.split(',').map(function(s) {
					return s.trim();
				}).filter(Boolean);
				if (m.isBot) state.botAliases = arr;
				else m.ref.aliases = arr;
				persistState();
				buildEngine();
			});
			ctrls.appendChild(al);
			var iw = document.createElement('input');
			iw.type = 'number';
			iw.className = 'roster-idle';
			iw.min = '0';
			iw.max = '100';
			iw.placeholder = 'auto';
			iw.title = 'Round-robin only: how readily this character takes an unaddressed turn (0\u2013100). Blank = inferred from personality.';
			var iwv = m.isBot ? state.botIdleWeight : m.ref.idleWeight;
			iw.value = (iwv == null ? '' : iwv);
			iw.addEventListener('change', function() {
				var v = iw.value.trim() === '' ? null : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, parseInt(iw.value, 10) || 0));
				if (m.isBot) state.botIdleWeight = v;
				else m.ref.idleWeight = v;
				persistState();
			});
			var iwl = document.createElement('label');
			iwl.className = 'roster-idle-wrap';
			iwl.appendChild(iw);
			iwl.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' speaks up'));
			ctrls.appendChild(iwl);
			r.appendChild(ctrls);
			box.appendChild(r);
		}
		if (botInScene()) row({
			id: BOT_ID,
			name: herName(),
			isBot: true
		});
		(state.cast || []).forEach(function(c) {
			row({
				id: c.id,
				name: c.name,
				isBot: false,
				ref: c
			});
		});
		if (!botInScene()) {
			var rr = document.createElement('div');
			rr.className = 'roster-restore';
			var s = document.createElement('span');
			s.textContent = 'The default character is out of the scene.';
			rr.appendChild(s);
			var b = document.createElement('button');
			b.className = 'btn';
			b.textContent = 'Bring back ' + (state.botName || 'Chloe');
			b.addEventListener('click', function() {
				state.botActive = true;
				persistState();
				buildEngine();
				renderRoster();
				renderMemPicker();
				updateCharLabel();
			});
			rr.appendChild(b);
			box.appendChild(rr);
		}
		var ms = byId('castMaxSpeakers'),
			mf = byId('castMaxFollowups');
		if (ms) {
			ms.value = state.castMaxSpeakers || 2;
			if (!ms.__w) {
				ms.__w = 1;
				ms.addEventListener('change', function() {
					state.castMaxSpeakers = Math.max(1, Math.min(6, parseInt(ms.value, 10) || 2));
					persistState();
				});
			}
		}
		if (mf) {
			mf.value = (state.castMaxFollowups == null ? 2 : state.castMaxFollowups);
			if (!mf.__w) {
				mf.__w = 1;
				mf.addEventListener('change', function() {
					state.castMaxFollowups = Math.max(0, Math.min(6, parseInt(mf.value, 10) || 0));
					persistState();
				});
			}
		}
		var rm = byId('castReplyMode');
		if (rm) {
			rm.value = state.castReplyMode || 'primary';
			if (!rm.__w) {
				rm.__w = 1;
				rm.addEventListener('change', function() {
					var v = rm.value;
					state.castReplyMode = (v === 'roundrobin' || v === 'everyone') ? v : 'primary';
					persistState();
				});
			}
		}
		var cch = byId('castChain'),
			cmx = byId('castChainMax');
		if (cch) {
			cch.checked = !!state.castChain;
			if (!cch.__w) {
				cch.__w = 1;
				cch.addEventListener('change', function() {
					state.castChain = !!cch.checked;
					persistState();
				});
			}
		}
		if (cmx) {
			cmx.value = (state.castChainMax == null ? 3 : state.castChainMax);
			if (!cmx.__w) {
				cmx.__w = 1;
				cmx.addEventListener('change', function() {
					state.castChainMax = Math.max(1, Math.min(6, parseInt(cmx.value, 10) || 3));
					persistState();
				});
			}
		}
	}

	function refreshMemViews() {
		renderFactList();
		renderInnerState();
		renderContextBudget();
	}

	function renderMemPicker() {
		var card = byId('memViewCard'),
			box = byId('memPicker');
		if (!isGroup()) {
			memViewId = BOT_ID;
			if (card) card.style.display = 'none';
			return;
		}
		if (!activeMembers().some(function(m) {
				return m.id === memViewId;
			})) memViewId = primaryIdEff();
		if (card) card.style.display = '';
		if (!box) return;
		box.innerHTML = '';
		var roster = activeMembers().map(function(m) {
			return {
				id: m.id,
				name: m.name
			};
		});
		roster.forEach(function(m) {
			var b = document.createElement('button');
			b.className = 'mempick' + (m.id === memViewId ? ' on' : '');
			b.textContent = m.name;
			b.style.setProperty('--speaker', colorFor(m.id) || 'var(--accent-ink)');
			b.addEventListener('click', function() {
				memViewId = m.id;
				renderMemPicker();
				refreshMemViews();
			});
			box.appendChild(b);
		});
	}

	function registerLorebook(eng) {
		if (!eng || typeof eng.registerProvider !== 'function') return;
		var band = (eng.BANDS && eng.BANDS.SITUATION != null) ? eng.BANDS.SITUATION : 50;
		eng.registerProvider({
			id: 'lorebook',
			priority: band,
			enabled: function() {
				return Array.isArray(state.lorebook) && state.lorebook.length > 0;
			},
			gather: function(g) {
				var entries = state.lorebook || [];
				if (!entries.length) return null;
				var parts = [];
				if (g && g.p && g.p.content) parts.push(String(g.p.content));
				if (g && g.lines && g.lines.length) parts.push(g.lines.slice(-6).join(' '));
				var scan = parts.join(' ').toLowerCase();
				if (!scan) return null;
				var hits = [];
				entries.forEach(function(e) {
					var keys = loreKeysArr(e).map(function(k) {
						return k.toLowerCase();
					});
					if (!keys.length || !e.content) return;
					if (keys.some(function(k) {
							return scan.indexOf(k) >= 0;
						})) hits.push(String(e.content).trim());
				});
				if (!hits.length) return null;
				var text = ('Relevant background (use only if it fits naturally; never recite it): ' + hits.join(' ')).slice(0, 1400);
				return {
					text: text,
					tokens: (eng.estimateTokens ? eng.estimateTokens(text) : Math.ceil(text.length / 4))
				};
			}
		});
	}

	function applyConfig(patch) {
		Object.assign(state, patch);
		persistState();
		buildEngine();
	}

	function call(method, args) {
		args = args || {};
		if (method === 'config.setCharacter') {
			if (args.name == null) {
				state.character = null;
				state.botName = 'Chloe';
			} else {
				state.character = {
					name: String(args.name).slice(0, 80),
					instruction: String(args.instruction || '').slice(0, 8000),
					avatar: String(args.avatar || '').slice(0, 2000),
					examples: String(args.examples || '').slice(0, 6000)
				};
				state.botName = state.character.name;
				if (state.character.avatar) state.herAvatar = state.character.avatar;
			}
			persistState();
			buildEngine();
			updateCharLabel();
			toast(state.character ? ('Now chatting as ' + state.character.name) : 'Reset to default Chloe', 'success');
			var bn = byId('botNameInput');
			if (bn) bn.value = state.botName;
			if (Array.isArray(args.initialMessages) && args.initialMessages.length) seedOpeningMessages(args.initialMessages);
			return Promise.resolve({
				ok: true,
				value: {
					character: state.character ? {
						name: state.character.name
					} : null
				}
			});
		}
		if (method === 'character.seedMemories') {
			var mems = Array.isArray(args.memories) ? args.memories : [];
			if (!engine || !mems.length) return Promise.resolve({
				ok: true,
				value: {
					seeded: 0
				}
			});
			return Promise.resolve(engine.seedCharacterMemories(String(args.name || 'this character'), mems)).then(function(nn) {
				return {
					ok: true,
					value: {
						seeded: nn
					}
				};
			});
		}
		return Promise.resolve({
			ok: false,
			reason: 'unknown method ' + method
		});
	}

	function seedOpeningMessages(list) {
		(list || []).forEach(function(m) {
			var author = String((m && (m.author || m.role)) || 'ai').toLowerCase();
			var content = String((m && (m.content || m.message || m.text)) || '').trim();
			if (!content) return;
			if (author === 'ai' || author === 'assistant' || author === 'bot' || author === 'char' || author === 'character') {
				var hm = enqueue(BOT_ID, herName(), true, content);
				renderMessage('her', herName(), content, {
					mid: hm.id
				});
			} else if (author === 'system') {
				renderSystemLine(content);
			} else {
				var um = enqueue(USER_ID, userNameStr(), false, content);
				renderMessage('you', userNameStr(), content, {
					mid: um.id
				});
			}
		});
		scrollThread(true);
	}

	function updateCharLabel() {
		var nm = state.botName || 'Chloe';
		setText('charCurrent', state.character ? state.character.name : 'default Chloe');
		setText('herName', nm);
		setText('aboutTitle', 'My Girl - ' + nm);
		try {
			document.title = 'My Girl - ' + nm;
		} catch (e) {}
		var img = byId('herAvatar');
		var av = state.herAvatar || (state.character && state.character.avatar);
		if (img) {
			if (av) {
				img.src = av;
				img.style.display = '';
			} else {
				img.style.display = 'none';
			}
		}
		if (typeof forgetReaction === 'function') forgetReaction();
		syncFavicon(av);
	}
	var _favSrc = null,
		_favOut = null;

	function setFaviconHref(href) {
		try {
			var link = document.querySelector('link[rel="icon"]');
			if (!link) {
				link = document.createElement('link');
				link.rel = 'icon';
				(document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(link);
			}
			if (link.getAttribute('href') !== href) link.setAttribute('href', href);
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function syncFavicon(av) {
		if (typeof av !== 'string' || !/^(data:image\/|https?:|blob:)/.test(av)) return;
		if (av === _favSrc) {
			if (_favOut) setFaviconHref(_favOut);
			return;
		}
		_favSrc = av;
		try {
			var im = new Image();
			im.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
			im.onload = function() {
				try {
					var c = document.createElement('canvas');
					c.width = c.height = 32;
					var ctx = c.getContext('2d');
					var s = Math.min(im.width, im.height) || 32,
						sx = (im.width - s) / 2,
						sy = (im.height - s) / 2;
					ctx.drawImage(im, sx, sy, s, s, 0, 0, 32, 32);
					_favOut = c.toDataURL('image/png');
				} catch (e) {
					_favOut = av;
				}
				setFaviconHref(_favOut);
			};
			im.onerror = function() {
				_favOut = av;
				setFaviconHref(av);
			};
			im.src = av;
		} catch (e) {
			_favOut = av;
			setFaviconHref(av);
		}
	}
	var streamEl, lastIdle = true;

	function makeSticky(scrollEl, btn) {
		var stuck = true;

		function near() {
			return (scrollEl.scrollHeight - scrollEl.scrollTop - scrollEl.clientHeight) < 80;
		}
		scrollEl.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
			stuck = near();
			if (stuck && btn) btn.classList.remove('show');
		});

		function jump() {
			scrollEl.scrollTop = scrollEl.scrollHeight;
			stuck = true;
			if (btn) btn.classList.remove('show');
		}
		if (btn) btn.addEventListener('click', jump);
		return {
			notify: function() {
				if (stuck) scrollEl.scrollTop = scrollEl.scrollHeight;
				else if (btn) btn.classList.add('show');
			},
			jump: jump
		};
	}
	var threadScroller = null,
		mindScroller = null;

	function scrollThread(force) {
		if (!threadScroller) {
			try {
				byId('thread').scrollTop = byId('thread').scrollHeight;
			} catch (e) {}
			return;
		}
		force ? threadScroller.jump() : threadScroller.notify();
	}

	function scrollMind() {
		if (!mindScroller) {
			try {
				streamEl.scrollTop = streamEl.scrollHeight;
			} catch (e) {}
			return;
		}
		mindScroller.notify();
	}

	function onEngineLog(line) {
		pushThought(String(line));
	}

	function pushThought(line) {
		line = String(line);
		if (/^\[chloe\.T0\]\s*poll(?!\s*error)/.test(line)) return;
		renderThought(line);
	}
	var CATMAP = {
		ctx: 'cog',
		T0: 'flow',
		T1: 'flow',
		T2: 'flow',
		T3: 'flow',
		tok: 'cog',
		lock: 'flow',
		poll: 'io',
		fact: 'mem',
		episode: 'mem',
		mem: 'mem',
		summary: 'mem',
		goal: 'mem',
		trust: 'mem',
		reflect: 'mem',
		consolidate: 'mem',
		semantic: 'mem',
		persona: 'cog',
		character: 'cog',
		think: 'cog',
		affect: 'cog',
		mood: 'cog',
		intent: 'cog',
		feedback: 'cog',
		mode: 'flow',
		work: 'flow',
		remind: 'io',
		greet: 'io',
		lull: 'io',
		checkin: 'io',
		note: 'page',
		stance: 'cog',
		topics: 'mem',
		measure: 'cog'
	};

	function renderThought(line) {
		if (!streamEl) return;
		if (lastIdle) {
			streamEl.innerHTML = '';
			lastIdle = false;
		}
		var tag = 'page',
			txt = line,
			m = String(line).match(/^\[chloe\.?([a-zA-Z0-9]*)\]\s*([\s\S]*)$/);
		if (m) {
			tag = m[1] || 'flow';
			txt = m[2];
		}
		var cls = CATMAP[tag] || 'flow';
		var pretty = prettifyThought(tag, txt);
		var row = document.createElement('div');
		row.className = 'thought';
		var t = document.createElement('span');
		t.className = 'tag ' + cls;
		t.textContent = tag || '·';
		var x = document.createElement('span');
		x.className = 'txt';
		x.textContent = pretty || txt;
		if (pretty) row.title = line;
		row.appendChild(t);
		row.appendChild(x);
		streamEl.appendChild(row);
		while (streamEl.children.length > 400) streamEl.removeChild(streamEl.firstChild);
		scrollMind();
	}

	function prettifyThought(tag, txt) {
		var t = String(txt || '');

		function num(re) {
			var mm = t.match(re);
			return mm ? mm[1] : null;
		}
		switch (tag) {
			case 'ctx':
				{
					var c = num(/packed\s+(\d+)\s+line/);
					return c ? ('weighing ' + c + ' things from our talk') : 'gathering what matters right now';
				}
			case 'think':
				{
					if (/nothing worth breaking/i.test(t)) return 'turning it over';
					var q = num(/into\s+(\d+)\s+question/);
					if (q) return 'breaking it into ' + q + ' parts';
					return 'thinking it over';
				}
			case 'img':
				return /fail|error|no image/i.test(t) ? 'my drawing didn\u2019t come through' : (/done|finish|ready|sent/i.test(t) ? 'finished a picture' : 'drawing something');
			case 'reflect':
				return 'reflecting on what I\u2019ve learned';
			case 'consolidate':
				return 'tidying my memory';
			case 'sum':
			case 'summary':
				return 'summarizing where we\u2019ve got to';
			case 'epi':
				return 'saving this moment to remember';
			case 'fact':
				return 'noting something about you';
			case 'goal':
				return 'keeping your goal in mind';
			case 'trust':
				return 'updating how well I know you';
			case 'affect':
			case 'mood':
				return 'checking my own mood';
			case 'feedback':
				return 'learning from how that landed';
			case 'archive':
				return 'filing away older context';
			case 'excise':
				return 'forgetting what you asked me to';
			case 'remind':
				return 'setting something aside to remember';
			case 'intent':
				return 'making a note to come back to this';
			case 'react':
				return 'reacting to that';
			case 'greet':
				return 'thinking how to say hi';
			case 'checkin':
				return 'wondering how you\u2019re doing';
			case 'lull':
				return 'sitting with the quiet';
			case 'sleep':
				return 'resting - quiet for now';
			default:
				return null;
		}
	}
	var threadCol, emptyState;

	function setThinking(on) {
		engineThinking = on;
		byId('pulse').classList.toggle('on', on);
		byId('mindTab').classList.toggle('thinking', on);
		if (!on) byId('typingRow').classList.remove('on');
	}

	function mdEsc(s) {
		return String(s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
	}

	function mdSafeUrl(u) {
		u = String(u || '').trim();
		return /^(https?:|mailto:)/i.test(u) ? u : (u.indexOf(':') < 0 ? u : '#');
	}

	function mdInline(s) {
		var codes = [];
		s = s.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, function(_, c) {
			codes.push(c);
			return '\u0001C' + (codes.length - 1) + '\u0001';
		});
		s = s.replace(/!\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/g, function(_, alt, u) {
			return '<img src="' + mdSafeUrl(u) + '" alt="' + alt + '">';
		});
		s = s.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/g, function(_, t, u) {
			return '<a href="' + mdSafeUrl(u) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">' + t + '</a>';
		});
		s = s.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>').replace(/__([^_]+)__/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
		s = s.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*(?!\*)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>').replace(/(^|[^_])_([^_\n]+)_(?!_)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
		s = s.replace(/~~([^~]+)~~/g, '<del>$1</del>');
		s = s.replace(/\u0001C(\d+)\u0001/g, function(_, i) {
			return '<code>' + codes[+i] + '</code>';
		});
		return s;
	}

	function mdFallback(src) {
		src = String(src).replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n');
		var blocks = [];
		src = src.replace(/```([\s\S]*?)```/g, function(_, c) {
			blocks.push(c.replace(/^\n/, ''));
			return '\u0001B' + (blocks.length - 1) + '\u0001';
		});
		var lines = src.split('\n'),
			out = [],
			i = 0;

		function list(tag, items) {
			if (items.length) out.push('<' + tag + '>' + items.map(function(x) {
				return '<li>' + mdInline(mdEsc(x)) + '</li>';
			}).join('') + '</' + tag + '>');
		}
		while (i < lines.length) {
			var ln = lines[i];
			if (/^\u0001B\d+\u0001$/.test(ln.trim())) {
				out.push('<pre><code>' + mdEsc(blocks[+ln.trim().replace(/[\u0001B]/g, '')]) + '</code></pre>');
				i++;
				continue;
			}
			var h = ln.match(/^(#{1,3})\s+(.*)$/);
			if (h) {
				out.push('<h' + h[1].length + '>' + mdInline(mdEsc(h[2])) + '</h' + h[1].length + '>');
				i++;
				continue;
			}
			if (/^\s*>\s?/.test(ln)) {
				var q = [];
				while (i < lines.length && /^\s*>\s?/.test(lines[i])) {
					q.push(lines[i].replace(/^\s*>\s?/, ''));
					i++;
				}
				out.push('<blockquote>' + mdInline(mdEsc(q.join(' '))) + '</blockquote>');
				continue;
			}
			if (/^\s*[-*+]\s+/.test(ln)) {
				var ul = [];
				while (i < lines.length && /^\s*[-*+]\s+/.test(lines[i])) {
					ul.push(lines[i].replace(/^\s*[-*+]\s+/, ''));
					i++;
				}
				list('ul', ul);
				continue;
			}
			if (/^\s*\d+\.\s+/.test(ln)) {
				var ol = [];
				while (i < lines.length && /^\s*\d+\.\s+/.test(lines[i])) {
					ol.push(lines[i].replace(/^\s*\d+\.\s+/, ''));
					i++;
				}
				list('ol', ol);
				continue;
			}
			if (ln.trim() === '') {
				i++;
				continue;
			}
			var para = [];
			while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() !== '' && !/^\s*[-*+]\s+|^\s*\d+\.\s+|^\s*>\s?|^#{1,3}\s+|^\u0001B\d+\u0001$/.test(lines[i])) {
				para.push(lines[i]);
				i++;
			}
			out.push('<p>' + mdInline(mdEsc(para.join('\n'))).replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</p>');
		}
		return out.join('');
	}

	function mdRender(src) {
		src = String(src == null ? '' : src);
		try {
			if (window.weld && window.weld.markdown && window.weld.markdown.render) return window.weld.markdown.render(src);
		} catch (e) {}
		return mdFallback(src);
	}

	function setBody(el, text, rich) {
		if (rich) {
			el.innerHTML = mdRender(text);
		} else {
			el.textContent = text;
		}
	}
	var EMOTION_KEYWORDS = {
		happy: 'happy,joy,smile,smiles,smiling,grin,grins,grinning,laugh,laughs,laughing,laughter,giggle,giggles,giggling,haha,hehe,glad,delight,delighted,content,pleased,cheerful,beaming,lovely,chuckle,chuckles,chuckling,hug,cuddle,adore,:D,^_^',
		excited: 'excited,exciting,thrilled,thrilling,ecstatic,eager,pumped,stoked,exhilarated,buzzing,amazed,wow,whoa,omg,awesome,fantastic,incredible,cant wait,cannot wait',
		celebrate: 'celebrate,celebrating,celebration,congrats,congratulations,hooray,hurray,woohoo,yay,party,cheers,victory,winner,triumphant,achievement,accomplished,we did it,nailed it',
		shy: 'shy,blush,blushes,blushing,bashful,timid,flustered,embarrass,embarrassed,embarrassing,awkward,sheepish,mortified,stutter,stammer,stammering',
		bored: 'bored,boring,bore,tedious,dull,uninterested,uninteresting,meh,sigh,sighs,sighing,yawn,yawns,yawning,monotonous,listless,restless',
		worried: 'worried,worry,worries,worrying,anxious,anxiety,nervous,uneasy,afraid,scared,fear,fearful,frightened,concern,concerned,dread,apprehensive,fret,fretting,tense,stressed,panic,panicking',
		lonely: 'lonely,alone,lonesome,loneliness,isolated,isolation,abandoned,forsaken,nobody,longing,yearning',
		sad: 'sad,sadness,downcast,gloomy,glum,heartbreak,heartbroken,sorrow,sorrowful,melancholy,miserable,grief,grieving,mourning,hollow,wistful,disappointed,dejected,despair',
		crying: 'cry,cries,crying,cried,sob,sobs,sobbing,weep,weeping,wept,tears,teary,tearful,bawl,bawling,whimper,whimpering,sniffle,sniffling'
	};
	var EMOTION_CUE = {
		happy: ['😊', 'happy'],
		excited: ['\uD83E\uDD29', 'excited'],
		celebrate: ['\uD83C\uDF89', 'celebrating'],
		shy: ['\uD83D\uDE33', 'shy'],
		bored: ['\uD83D\uDE10', 'bored'],
		worried: ['\uD83D\uDE1F', 'worried'],
		lonely: ['\uD83E\uDD7A', 'lonely'],
		sad: ['\uD83D\uDE41', 'a little down'],
		crying: ['\uD83D\uDE22', 'tearful']
	};

	function detectEmotion(text) {
		if (!text) return null;
		var best = null,
			maxIndex = -1,
			maxLen = 0;
		Object.keys(EMOTION_KEYWORDS).forEach(function(emotion) {
			var words = EMOTION_KEYWORDS[emotion].split(',').map(function(w) {
				return w.trim();
			}).filter(Boolean);
			if (!words.length) return;
			var safe = words.map(function(w) {
				return w.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
			});
			var pattern = new RegExp('(^|[^a-zA-Z0-9_])(' + safe.join('|') + ')(?![a-zA-Z0-9_])', 'gi'),
				m;
			while ((m = pattern.exec(text)) !== null) {
				var len = m[2].length;
				if (m.index > maxIndex) {
					maxIndex = m.index;
					best = emotion;
					maxLen = len;
				} else if (m.index === maxIndex && len > maxLen) {
					best = emotion;
					maxLen = len;
				}
			}
		});
		var symbols = {
			'?!': 'excited',
			'!?': 'excited'
		};
		Object.keys(symbols).forEach(function(sym) {
			var idx = text.lastIndexOf(sym);
			if (idx > maxIndex) {
				maxIndex = idx;
				best = symbols[sym];
			}
		});
		return best;
	}

	function applyEmotionCue(wrap, text) {
		if (!wrap) return;
		var who = wrap.querySelector('.who');
		if (!who) return;
		var old = who.querySelector('.emocue');
		if (old) old.parentNode.removeChild(old);
		if (state.showEmotion === false) return;
		var emo = detectEmotion(String(text || ''));
		if (!emo || !EMOTION_CUE[emo]) return;
		var span = document.createElement('span');
		span.className = 'emocue';
		span.textContent = ' ' + EMOTION_CUE[emo][0];
		span.title = 'she seems ' + EMOTION_CUE[emo][1];
		who.appendChild(span);
	}

	function refreshEmotionCues() {
		if (!threadCol) return;
		var msgs = threadCol.querySelectorAll('.msg.her');
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(msgs, function(wrap) {
			if (wrap.querySelector('.avatar')) return;
			var body = wrap.querySelector('.body');
			applyEmotionCue(wrap, body ? (body.textContent || '') : '');
		});
	}

	function renderMessage(role, who, text, opts) {
		if (emptyState && emptyState.parentNode) {
			emptyState.parentNode.removeChild(emptyState);
			emptyState = null;
		}
		var roleCls = role === 'her' ? 'her' : (role === 'system' ? 'system' : 'you');
		var wrap = document.createElement('div');
		wrap.className = 'msg ' + roleCls + ((opts && opts.nar) ? ' nar' : '') + ((opts && opts.streaming) ? ' streaming' : '');
		if (opts && opts.mid != null) wrap.setAttribute('data-mid', opts.mid);
		var w = document.createElement('div');
		w.className = 'who';
		w.textContent = who;
		wrap.appendChild(w);
		var b = document.createElement('div');
		b.className = 'body';
		setBody(b, text, role === 'her' && !(opts && opts.image));
		wrap.appendChild(b);
		if (opts && opts.image) {
			var im = document.createElement('img');
			im.className = 'avatar';
			im.src = opts.image;
			wrap.appendChild(im);
		}
		var typing = byId('typingRow');
		threadCol.insertBefore(wrap, typing);
		scrollThread(role === 'you');
		if (role === 'her' && !(opts && opts.image) && !(opts && opts.streaming) && text) {
			attachFeedback(wrap, text);
			applyEmotionCue(wrap, text);
		}
		if (role === 'her' && !(opts && opts.streaming) && (opts && opts.mid != null)) attachRegen(wrap, opts.mid, !!(opts && opts.image));
		if (!(opts && opts.streaming)) attachRemove(wrap);
		return wrap;
	}

	function msgActions(wrap) {
		var r = wrap.querySelector('.acts');
		if (!r) {
			r = document.createElement('div');
			r.className = 'acts';
			wrap.appendChild(r);
		}
		return r;
	}

	function attachRemove(wrap) {
		if (!wrap || wrap.querySelector('.msg-del')) return;
		var b = document.createElement('button');
		b.className = 'msg-del';
		b.type = 'button';
		b.title = 'Remove this message';
		b.textContent = '\u2715';
		b.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			e.stopPropagation();
			removeMessage(wrap);
		});
		var row = wrap.querySelector('.acts');
		if (row) row.appendChild(b);
		else wrap.appendChild(b);
	}

	function removeMessage(wrap) {
		if (!wrap) return;
		var mid = wrap.getAttribute('data-mid');
		if (mid) {
			for (var i = queue.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
				if (String(queue[i].id) === String(mid)) queue.splice(i, 1);
			}
			persistQueue();
		}
		if (wrap.parentNode) wrap.parentNode.removeChild(wrap);
	}

	function attachFeedback(wrap, text) {
		if (!engine || typeof engine.recordFeedback !== 'function' || typeof engine.styleOf !== 'function') return;
		if (wrap.__fb) return;
		wrap.__fb = true;
		var measureTags = signatureNow();
		var row = msgActions(wrap),
			voteBtns = [];

		function mk(label, reward) {
			var btn = document.createElement('button');
			btn.type = 'button';
			btn.textContent = label;
			btn.title = reward > 0 ? 'Tell her that one landed' : 'Tell her to ease off that style';
			btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
				try {
					engine.recordFeedback(engine.styleOf(text), reward);
				} catch (e) {}
				try {
					if (nationArmy) nationArmy.ingestReaction({
						kind: reward > 0 ? 'up' : 'down',
						toward: lastNationSpeaker
					});
				} catch (e) {}
				recordMeasure(measureTags, reward);
				row.classList.add('voted');
				voteBtns.forEach(function(c) {
					c.classList.remove('on');
				});
				btn.classList.add('on');
			});
			voteBtns.push(btn);
			return btn;
		}
		row.appendChild(mk('\ud83d\udc4d', 1));
		row.appendChild(mk('\ud83d\udc4e', -1));
		if (engine && typeof engine.addHighlight === 'function') {
			var star = document.createElement('button');
			star.type = 'button';
			star.textContent = '\u2605';
			star.title = 'Save this line to highlights';
			star.addEventListener('click', function() {
				Promise.resolve(engine.addHighlight({
					text: text,
					authorName: herName()
				}, USER_ID, userNameStr(), '')).then(function() {
					star.classList.add('on');
				});
			});
			row.appendChild(star);
		}
	}

	function msgById(mid) {
		for (var i = queue.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
			if (String(queue[i].id) === String(mid)) return queue[i];
		}
		return null;
	}

	function lastMsg(pred) {
		for (var i = queue.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
			if (pred(queue[i])) return queue[i];
		}
		return null;
	}

	function lastUserMsg() {
		return lastMsg(function(m) {
			return m.author && !m.author.bot;
		});
	}

	function lastBotMsg() {
		return lastMsg(function(m) {
			return m.author && m.author.bot;
		});
	}

	function wrapFor(m) {
		return (m && threadCol) ? threadCol.querySelector('[data-mid="' + m.id + '"]') : null;
	}

	function vNext(len, i, dir) {
		return Math.max(0, Math.min(len - 1, i + dir));
	}

	function ensureVariants(m, isImage, seedImage) {
		if (m.variants && m.variants.length) return;
		m.vkind = isImage ? 'image' : 'text';
		m.variants = [isImage ? (seedImage || '') : String(m.content || '')];
		m.vi = 0;
	}

	function applyVariant(wrap, m) {
		var v = m.variants[m.vi];
		if (m.vkind === 'image') {
			var im = wrap.querySelector('img.avatar');
			if (im && v) im.src = v;
		} else {
			var body = wrap.querySelector('.body');
			if (body) {
				setBody(body, v, true);
			}
			m.content = v;
		}
		var nav = wrap.querySelector('.vnav'),
			cnt = wrap.querySelector('.vcount'),
			acts = wrap.querySelector('.acts');
		var many = m.variants.length > 1;
		if (nav) nav.classList.toggle('show', many);
		if (acts) acts.classList.toggle('has', many);
		if (cnt) cnt.textContent = (m.vi + 1) + '/' + m.variants.length;
		persistQueue();
	}

	function attachRegen(wrap, mid, isImage) {
		if (!wrap || wrap.__rgx) return;
		wrap.__rgx = true;
		var m = msgById(mid);
		if (!m) return;
		var seedImg = isImage ? (function() {
			var im = wrap.querySelector('img.avatar');
			return im ? im.src : '';
		})() : '';
		ensureVariants(m, isImage, seedImg);
		var row = msgActions(wrap);
		var regen = document.createElement('button');
		regen.type = 'button';
		regen.textContent = '\uD83D\uDD04';
		regen.title = 'Regenerate - a fresh take, kept as a new variant';
		var nav = document.createElement('div');
		nav.className = 'vnav';
		var prev = document.createElement('button');
		prev.type = 'button';
		prev.textContent = '\u2039';
		prev.title = 'Previous version';
		var cnt = document.createElement('span');
		cnt.className = 'vcount';
		var next = document.createElement('button');
		next.type = 'button';
		next.textContent = '\u203A';
		next.title = 'Next version';
		nav.appendChild(prev);
		nav.appendChild(cnt);
		nav.appendChild(next);
		row.appendChild(regen);
		row.appendChild(nav);
		prev.addEventListener('click', function() {
			m.vi = vNext(m.variants.length, m.vi, -1);
			applyVariant(wrap, m);
		});
		next.addEventListener('click', function() {
			m.vi = vNext(m.variants.length, m.vi, +1);
			applyVariant(wrap, m);
		});
		regen.addEventListener('click', function() {
			regenerateMessage(wrap, m, regen);
		});
		addSwipe(wrap, function(dir) {
			if (m.variants.length > 1) {
				m.vi = vNext(m.variants.length, m.vi, dir);
				applyVariant(wrap, m);
			}
		});
		applyVariant(wrap, m);
	}

	function speakerInfoById(id) {
		if (id && id !== BOT_ID) {
			var c = (state.cast || []).filter(function(x) {
				return x.id === id;
			})[0];
			if (c) return {
				name: c.name,
				instruction: c.instruction || ('a character named ' + c.name)
			};
		}
		return {
			name: herName(),
			instruction: (state.character && state.character.instruction) || 'a warm, thoughtful companion who remembers'
		};
	}

	function regenerateMessage(wrap, m, btn) {
		if (m.__regen) return;
		m.__regen = true;
		if (btn) {
			btn.disabled = true;
			btn.textContent = '\u2026';
		}

		function done(payload) {
			m.__regen = false;
			if (btn) {
				btn.disabled = false;
				btn.textContent = '\uD83D\uDD04';
			}
			if (payload == null || payload === '') {
				toast('Couldn\u2019t regenerate that one - try again', 'info');
				return;
			}
			m.variants.push(payload);
			m.vi = m.variants.length - 1;
			applyVariant(wrap, m);
		}
		if (m.vkind === 'image') {
			var prompt = extractPrompt(m.content);
			if (!prompt) {
				m.__regen = false;
				if (btn) {
					btn.disabled = false;
					btn.textContent = '\uD83D\uDD04';
				}
				toast('No saved prompt for this image to redraw from', 'info');
				return;
			}
			Promise.resolve(paint({
				prompt: varyPrompt(prompt, (m.variants ? m.variants.length : 1))
			})).then(function(r) {
				done(r && r.ok ? String(r.value || '') : '');
			}, function() {
				done('');
			});
		} else {
			var ai = null;
			try {
				ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin);
			} catch (e) {}
			if (typeof ai !== 'function') {
				done('');
				return;
			}
			var idx = queue.indexOf(m);
			var who = speakerInfoById(m.author && m.author.id);
			var prior = queue.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 12), idx < 0 ? queue.length : idx).filter(function(x) {
				return x && x.content && !(x.author && x.author.id === 'system');
			}).map(function(x) {
				return (x.author && x.author.bot ? (x.author.username || herName()) : userNameStr()) + ': ' + x.content;
			}).join('\n');
			var instr = 'You are ' + who.name + '. ' + who.instruction + '\n\n' + (prior ? ('Conversation so far:\n' + prior + '\n\n') : '') + 'Write ' + who.name + '\u2019s next reply: in character, natural, just the message text - no name prefix, no quotation marks. Give a fresh take on the moment, different wording from before.';
			Promise.resolve(ai({
				instruction: instr,
				startWith: ''
			})).then(function(r) {
				done(String(r || '').trim().replace(/^[\s"'\u201c]+|[\s"'\u201d]+$/g, ''));
			}, function() {
				done('');
			});
		}
	}

	function addSwipe(el, cb) {
		var x0 = 0,
			y0 = 0,
			tracking = false;
		el.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
			var t = e.changedTouches[0];
			x0 = t.clientX;
			y0 = t.clientY;
			tracking = true;
		}, {
			passive: true
		});
		el.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
			if (!tracking) return;
			tracking = false;
			var t = e.changedTouches[0];
			var dx = t.clientX - x0,
				dy = t.clientY - y0;
			if (Math.abs(dx) > 55 && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) * 1.6) cb(dx < 0 ? +1 : -1);
		}, {
			passive: true
		});
	}

	function userNameStr() {
		return state.userName || 'You';
	}

	function renderSystemLine(text, nar) {
		return renderMessage('system', '', String(text), {
			system: true,
			nar: !!nar
		});
	}

	function herContext() {
		if (engine && typeof engine.assembleContext === 'function') return Promise.resolve(engine.assembleContext({
			authorId: USER_ID,
			authorName: userNameStr(),
			content: ''
		}));
		return Promise.resolve(null);
	}

	function proactive(kind, idea) {
		setThinking(true);
		herContext().then(function(actx) {
			if (!actx) {
				setThinking(false);
				renderSystemLine('(connect a language model for that)');
				return;
			}
			var p;
			if (kind === 'recap') p = recap({
				recent: actx
			});
			else {
				actx.prompt = idea || 'Share a small, natural thought or observation right now.';
				p = beat(actx);
			}
			Promise.resolve(p).then(function(r) {
				setThinking(false);
				if (r && r.ok && r.value) soloSend(CH, String(r.value));
				else renderSystemLine('(' + ((r && r.reason) || 'nothing to say just now') + ')');
			}, function() {
				setThinking(false);
				renderSystemLine('(that didn\u2019t work just now)');
			});
		});
	}

	function writeForMe() {
		var ai = null;
		try {
			ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin);
		} catch (e) {}
		if (typeof ai !== 'function') {
			renderSystemLine('(connect a language model to draft for you)');
			return;
		}
		var recent = queue.slice(-12).map(function(m) {
			return (m.author.bot ? herName() : userNameStr()) + ': ' + m.content;
		}).join('\n');
		var instr = 'You are writing as ' + userNameStr() + ', the human, in a 1:1 conversation with ' + herName() + '.\n' + (recent ? ('Conversation so far:\n' + recent + '\n\n') : '') + 'Write ' + userNameStr() + '\u2019s next message: natural, in their own voice, one short message. No name prefix, no quotation marks - just the message text.';
		var btn = byId('tbWrite');
		if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
		Promise.resolve(ai({
			instruction: instr,
			startWith: ''
		})).then(function(r) {
			if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
			var t = String(r || '').trim().replace(/^[\s"'\u201c]+|[\s"'\u201d]+$/g, '');
			if (!t) {
				renderSystemLine('(couldn\u2019t draft one - try again)');
				return;
			}
			var inp = byId('input');
			inp.value = t;
			inp.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
			inp.focus();
		}, function() {
			if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
			renderSystemLine('(draft failed)');
		});
	}

	function doImage(args) {
		var prompt = String(args && args.prompt || '').trim();
		if (prompt.length < 2) {
			renderSystemLine('(give me something to draw, e.g. /img a red fox in snow)');
			return;
		}
		var statusLine = renderSystemLine('drawing: ' + prompt.slice(0, 90), true);
		setThinking(true);

		function clearStatus() {
			if (statusLine && statusLine.parentNode) statusLine.parentNode.removeChild(statusLine);
			statusLine = null;
		}
		var finalPrompt = enrichImagePrompt(prompt);
		Promise.resolve(paint({
			prompt: finalPrompt,
			resolution: args.resolution,
			guidanceScale: args.guidanceScale,
			removeBackground: !!args.removeBackground
		})).then(function(r) {
			setThinking(false);
			clearStatus();
			if (r && r.ok && r.value) soloSendImage(CH, String(r.value), prompt, r.blob);
			else renderSystemLine('(' + ((r && r.reason) || 'image failed') + ')');
		}, function() {
			setThinking(false);
			clearStatus();
			renderSystemLine('(image failed)');
		});
	}

	function addMemory(text) {
		text = String(text || '').trim();
		if (!text) {
			renderSystemLine('(what should the character remember? e.g. /mem I love sci-fi)');
			return;
		}
		if (engine && engine.addFacts) Promise.resolve(engine.addFacts(USER_ID, [{
			text: text,
			importance: 8
		}], 'operator')).then(function() {
			renderSystemLine('remembered: ' + text);
			try {
				renderFactList();
				renderInnerState();
			} catch (e) {}
		});
	}

	function addSysDirective(text) {
		text = String(text || '').trim();
		if (/^clear$/i.test(text)) {
			state.injections = [];
			persistState();
			refreshClock();
			try {
				renderInjList();
			} catch (e) {}
			renderSystemLine('system directives cleared');
			return;
		}
		if (!text) {
			renderSystemLine('(a standing directive, e.g. /sys We are aboard a starship in 2400)');
			return;
		}
		if (!state.injections) state.injections = [];
		state.injections.push({
			id: 'inj' + Date.now(),
			text: text
		});
		persistState();
		refreshClock();
		try {
			renderInjList();
		} catch (e) {}
		renderSystemLine('system directive added: ' + text);
	}

	function addNarration(text) {
		text = String(text || '').trim();
		if (!text) {
			renderSystemLine('(a line of scene/narration, e.g. /nar Rain begins to fall outside)');
			return;
		}
		renderSystemLine(text, true);
		enqueue(USER_ID, userNameStr(), false, '*' + text + '*');
		dropLiveOrphan();
		replyExpected = true;
		setThinking(true);
		if (engine && engine.pollOnce) {
			try {
				engine.pollOnce();
			} catch (e) {}
		}
	}

	function goalCmd(text) {
		text = String(text || '').trim();
		var m = text.match(/^(done|complete|finish|drop|remove)\s+(.+)$/i);
		if (m) {
			closeGoalByRef(m[2].trim(), /drop|remove/i.test(m[1]) ? 'dropped' : 'done');
			return;
		}
		if (!text) {
			renderSystemLine('(a goal, e.g. /goal ask how my exam went - or /goal done 2 to close one)');
			return;
		}
		if (engine && engine.addGoal) Promise.resolve(engine.addGoal(text, USER_ID, userNameStr(), 'command')).then(function() {
			renderSystemLine('noted a goal: ' + text);
			try {
				renderInnerState();
			} catch (e) {}
		});
	}

	function resolveGoalRef(list, ref) {
		list = list || [];
		ref = String(ref || '').trim();
		if (/^\d+$/.test(ref)) {
			var i = parseInt(ref, 10) - 1;
			if (i >= 0 && i < list.length) return list[i];
		}
		return list.filter(function(g) {
			return g.id === ref;
		})[0] || null;
	}

	function closeGoalByRef(ref, status) {
		if (!(engine && engine.closeGoal && engine.goalsForOwner)) {
			renderSystemLine('(can\u2019t close goals right now)');
			return;
		}
		Promise.resolve(engine.goalsForOwner(USER_ID)).then(function(list) {
			var goal = resolveGoalRef(list || [], ref);
			if (!goal) {
				renderSystemLine('(no open goal \u201c' + ref + '\u201d - /goals to list them)');
				return;
			}
			Promise.resolve(engine.closeGoal(goal.id, USER_ID, status)).then(function(r) {
				renderSystemLine((r && r.ok) ? ((status === 'dropped' ? 'dropped' : 'done') + ': ' + goal.text) : ('(' + ((r && r.reason) || 'couldn\u2019t close that') + ')'));
				try {
					renderInnerState();
				} catch (e) {}
			});
		});
	}

	function forgetCmd(rest) {
		rest = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (/^me$/i.test(rest)) {
			if (confirm('Forget everything the character has learned about you?') && engine && engine.getFacts) {
				Promise.resolve(engine.getFacts(USER_ID)).then(function(fs) {
					(fs || []).forEach(function(f) {
						try {
							engine.forgetFact(USER_ID, f.text);
						} catch (e) {}
					});
					renderSystemLine('forgotten everything about you');
					try {
						renderFactList();
						renderInnerState();
					} catch (e) {}
				});
			}
			return;
		}
		if (!rest) {
			renderSystemLine('(/forget <a thing>  or  /forget me)');
			return;
		}
		if (engine && engine.forgetFact) Promise.resolve(engine.forgetFact(USER_ID, rest)).then(function() {
			renderSystemLine('forgotten: ' + rest);
			try {
				renderFactList();
			} catch (e) {}
		});
	}

	function aboutMe() {
		if (!(engine && engine.getMemory)) return;
		Promise.resolve(engine.getMemory(USER_ID)).then(function(m) {
			var facts = (m && m.facts || []).map(function(f) {
				return f.text;
			});
			var ins = (m && m.insights || []).map(function(x) {
				return x.text;
			});
			var parts = [];
			if (ins.length) parts.push(ins.join('; '));
			if (facts.length) parts.push(facts.join('; '));
			soloSend(CH, parts.length ? ('Here\u2019s what I know about you: ' + parts.join('. ') + '.') : 'I haven\u2019t learned much about you yet - keep talking to me.');
		});
	}

	function showGoals() {
		if (!(engine && engine.goalsForOwner)) return;
		Promise.resolve(engine.goalsForOwner(USER_ID)).then(function(g) {
			g = g || [];
			if (!g.length) {
				renderSystemLine('No goals on the list yet - /goal <text> adds one.');
				return;
			}
			renderSystemLine('Open goals (/goal done <n> or /goal drop <n> to close):\n' + g.map(function(x, i) {
				return (i + 1) + '. ' + x.text;
			}).join('\n'), true);
		});
	}

	function highlightLast(note) {
		var last = lastBotMsg();
		if (!last) last = queue[queue.length - 1];
		if (!last) {
			renderSystemLine('(nothing to highlight yet)');
			return;
		}
		if (engine && engine.addHighlight) Promise.resolve(engine.addHighlight({
			text: last.content,
			authorName: last.author.username || herName()
		}, USER_ID, userNameStr(), note || '')).then(function(r) {
			renderSystemLine((r && r.ok === false) ? ('(' + r.reason + ')') : ('\u2605 highlighted: \u201c' + String(last.content).slice(0, 60) + '\u201d' + (note ? (' - ' + note) : '')));
		});
	}

	function showNeurons(rest) {
		rest = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (!engine || !engine.nervous) {
			renderSystemLine('(no Brain attached yet)');
			return;
		}
		var nv = engine.nervous;
		if (rest) {
			var d = nv.describe(rest);
			if (!d) {
				renderSystemLine('no neuron called \u201c' + rest + '\u201d - try /neurons');
				return;
			}
			var lines = [d.id + '  \u00b7  ' + d.kind + '  \u00b7  ' + nv.health(d.id)];
			if (d.role) lines.push(d.role);
			(d.does || []).forEach(function(x) {
				lines.push('  \u2022 ' + x.name + ' - ' + x.how);
			});
			renderSystemLine(lines.join('\n'));
			return;
		}
		var list = nv.list();
		if (!list.length) {
			renderSystemLine('(no neurons registered)');
			return;
		}
		var byKind = {};
		list.forEach(function(n) {
			(byKind[n.kind] = byKind[n.kind] || []).push(n);
		});
		var out = ['The Brain has ' + list.length + ' neurons - /neurons <id> to inspect one:'];
		['organ', 'store', 'process', 'provider'].filter(function(k) {
			return byKind[k];
		}).forEach(function(k) {
			out.push(k + ' - ' + byKind[k].map(function(n) {
				return n.id + (n.state !== 'active' ? (' [' + n.state + ']') : '');
			}).join(', '));
		});
		renderSystemLine(out.join('\n'));
	}

	function councilStanceInstruction(stance, prompt) {
		var persona = (state.character && state.character.instruction) ? state.character.instruction : 'a warm, thoughtful companion who remembers';
		var recent = queue.slice(-10).filter(function(x) {
			return x && x.content;
		}).map(function(x) {
			return (x.author && x.author.bot ? herName() : userNameStr()) + ': ' + x.content;
		}).join('\n');
		var flavor = {
			warm: 'Lean warm and emotionally attuned - validate first, hold space.',
			grounded: 'Lean clear, honest and practical - say the useful thing plainly, no fluff.',
			spark: 'Lean curious and lightly playful - keep it alive, end with genuine interest.'
		}[stance] || '';
		return 'You are ' + herName() + '. ' + persona + '\n\n' + (recent ? ('Conversation so far:\n' + recent + '\n\n') : '') + (prompt ? ('The user just said: ' + prompt + '\n\n') : '') + 'Draft ' + herName() + '\u2019s next reply. ' + flavor + ' Just the message text - no name prefix, no quotes.';
	}

	function scoreReply(text, w) {
		text = String(text || '');
		var len = text.trim().length;
		var band = len < 8 ? 0 : (len > 600 ? 0.3 : Math.min(1, 0.4 + (Math.min(len, 240) / 240) * 0.6));
		var question = /\?\s*$/.test(text.trim()) ? 1 : 0;
		var warmth = (text.match(/\b(here|with you|understand|sorry|glad|proud|care|okay|together|listening)\b/gi) || []).length;
		var action = /\*[^*]+\*/.test(text) ? 1 : 0;
		return (w.len || 0) * band + (w.q || 0) * question + (w.warm || 0) * Math.min(1, warmth / 3) + (w.act || 0) * action;
	}

	function vetoReply(p) {
		var t = String(p && p.text || '').trim();
		if (!t) return {
			veto: true,
			reason: 'empty'
		};
		if (t.length > 1200) return {
			veto: true,
			reason: 'too long'
		};
		return {
			veto: false
		};
	}

	function councilMembers(prompt) {
		var ai = null;
		try {
			ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin);
		} catch (e) {}
		var Cc = window.ChloeBrain;

		function proposer(stance) {
			return function() {
				if (typeof ai !== 'function') return null;
				return Promise.resolve(ai({
					instruction: councilStanceInstruction(stance, prompt),
					startWith: ''
				})).then(function(r) {
					var t = String(r || '').trim().replace(/^[\s"'\u201c]+|[\s"'\u201d]+$/g, '');
					return t ? {
						text: t,
						conf: 0.5
					} : null;
				}, function() {
					return null;
				});
			};
		}
		return [
			Cc.member('warm', 'warm - empathic', {
				propose: proposer('warm'),
				vote: function(p) {
					return scoreReply(p.text, {
						len: .3,
						warm: .6,
						q: .1
					});
				},
				veto: vetoReply
			}),
			Cc.member('grounded', 'grounded - clear & honest', {
				propose: proposer('grounded'),
				vote: function(p) {
					return scoreReply(p.text, {
						len: .6,
						warm: .2,
						act: .2
					});
				},
				veto: vetoReply
			}),
			Cc.member('spark', 'spark - curious & playful', {
				propose: proposer('spark'),
				vote: function(p) {
					return scoreReply(p.text, {
						len: .3,
						q: .5,
						act: .2
					});
				},
				veto: vetoReply
			})
		];
	}

	function runCouncil(rest) {
		if (!window.ChloeBrain || !window.ChloeT0) {
			renderSystemLine('(council unavailable)');
			return;
		}
		var ai = null;
		try {
			ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin);
		} catch (e) {}
		if (typeof ai !== 'function') {
			renderSystemLine('(connect a language model for the council to deliberate)');
			return;
		}
		var prompt = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (!prompt) {
			var _lu = lastUserMsg();
			if (_lu) prompt = _lu.content;
		}
		setThinking(true);
		var nv = window.ChloeBrain.createNervous(function() {});
		var council = window.ChloeBrain.createCouncil({
			members: councilMembers(prompt),
			nervous: nv
		});
		council.deliberate({
			prompt: prompt
		}).then(function(dec) {
			setThinking(false);
			if (!dec || !dec.text) {
				renderSystemLine('(' + ((dec && dec.status) || 'the council reached no reply') + ')');
				return;
			}
			var lines = ['\ud83e\udde0 council - ' + dec.status + (dec.consensus ? ' (unanimous)' : '') + ':'];
			(dec.transcript.proposals || []).forEach(function(p) {
				var t = (dec.tally && dec.tally[p.by] != null) ? (' \u00b7 ' + dec.tally[p.by].toFixed(1)) : '';
				var v = (dec.vetoed || []).indexOf(p.by) >= 0 ? ' \u00b7 vetoed' : '';
				lines.push((p.by === dec.winnerId ? '\u2714 ' : '\u00b7 ') + p.by + t + v);
			});
			if (dec.dissent && dec.dissent.length) lines.push('dissent: ' + dec.dissent.join(', '));
			renderSystemLine(lines.join('\n'));
			var m = enqueue(BOT_ID, herName(), true, dec.text);
			var w = renderMessage('her', herName(), dec.text, {
				mid: m.id
			});
			if (w) {
				w.setAttribute('data-cid', BOT_ID);
				if (state.botColor) styleSpeaker(w, BOT_ID, state.herAvatar);
			}
		}, function() {
			setThinking(false);
			renderSystemLine('(the council failed to convene)');
		});
	}
	var EMOTIONS = [
		['joy', {
			len: .3,
			warm: .6,
			q: .1
		}],
		['sadness', {
			len: .4,
			warm: .5
		}],
		['fear', {
			len: .5,
			warm: .2
		}],
		['anger', {
			len: .6,
			warm: .1
		}],
		['disgust', {
			len: .5,
			act: .2
		}],
		['anxiety', {
			len: .4,
			q: .3
		}],
		['envy', {
			len: .4,
			warm: .3
		}],
		['ennui', {
			len: .6
		}],
		['embarrassment', {
			len: .4,
			warm: .3
		}]
	];

	function emotionMembers() {
		var Cc = window.ChloeBrain;
		return EMOTIONS.map(function(e) {
			return Cc.member(e[0], e[0], {
				propose: function() {
					return null;
				},
				veto: vetoReply,
				vote: (function(w) {
					return function(p) {
						return scoreReply(p.text, w);
					};
				})(e[1])
			});
		});
	}

	function runSociety(rest) {
		if (!window.ChloeBrain || !window.ChloeT0) {
			renderSystemLine('(society unavailable)');
			return;
		}
		var ai = null;
		try {
			ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin);
		} catch (e) {}
		if (typeof ai !== 'function') {
			renderSystemLine('(connect a language model so a few voices can propose)');
			return;
		}
		var prompt = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (!prompt) {
			var _lu = lastUserMsg();
			if (_lu) prompt = _lu.content;
		}
		setThinking(true);
		var stances = ['warm', 'grounded', 'spark'];
		Promise.all(stances.map(function(s) {
			return Promise.resolve(ai({
				instruction: councilStanceInstruction(s, prompt),
				startWith: ''
			})).then(function(r) {
				var t = String(r || '').trim().replace(/^[\s"'\u201c]+|[\s"'\u201d]+$/g, '');
				return t ? {
					by: s,
					text: t,
					conf: .5
				} : null;
			}, function() {
				return null;
			});
		})).then(function(cands) {
			cands = cands.filter(Boolean);
			if (!cands.length) {
				setThinking(false);
				renderSystemLine('(no candidate replies to weigh)');
				return;
			}
			var nv = window.ChloeBrain.createNervous(function() {});
			var society = window.ChloeBrain.createCouncil({
				members: emotionMembers(),
				nervous: nv,
				timeoutMs: 8000,
				idleMs: 60000
			});
			return society.deliberate({
				prompt: prompt
			}, cands).then(function(dec) {
				setThinking(false);
				if (!dec || !dec.text) {
					renderSystemLine('(' + ((dec && dec.status) || 'the society reached no reply') + ')');
					return;
				}
				var lines = ['\ud83c\udfad society of ' + society.size + ' - ' + dec.status + (dec.consensus ? ' (unanimous)' : '') + ':'];
				(dec.transcript.proposals || []).forEach(function(p) {
					var t = (dec.tally && dec.tally[p.by] != null) ? (' \u00b7 ' + dec.tally[p.by].toFixed(1)) : '';
					var v = (dec.vetoed || []).indexOf(p.by) >= 0 ? ' \u00b7 vetoed' : '';
					lines.push((p.by === dec.winnerId ? '\u2714 ' : '\u00b7 ') + p.by + t + v);
				});
				lines.push('all ' + (dec.participation ? dec.participation.length : society.size) + ' emotions cast a vote');
				renderSystemLine(lines.join('\n'));
				var m = enqueue(BOT_ID, herName(), true, dec.text);
				var w = renderMessage('her', herName(), dec.text, {
					mid: m.id
				});
				if (w) {
					w.setAttribute('data-cid', BOT_ID);
					if (state.botColor) styleSpeaker(w, BOT_ID, state.herAvatar);
				}
			});
		}, function() {
			setThinking(false);
			renderSystemLine('(the society could not convene)');
		});
	}

	function showHighlights(rest) {
		rest = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (/^clear$/i.test(rest)) {
			if (engine && engine.clearHighlights) Promise.resolve(engine.clearHighlights()).then(function(n) {
				renderSystemLine(n ? ('cleared ' + n + ' highlight' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's')) : 'no highlights to clear');
			});
			return;
		}
		if (engine && engine.listHighlights) Promise.resolve(engine.listHighlights(10)).then(function(list) {
			list = list || [];
			if (!list.length) {
				renderSystemLine('no highlights yet - /highlight saves the last line');
				return;
			}
			renderSystemLine('\u2605 ' + list.map(function(h) {
				return '\u201c' + String(h.text).slice(0, 56) + '\u201d' + (h.note ? (' (' + h.note + ')') : '');
			}).join('   '));
		});
	}

	function clockAck(kind) {
		var d = new Date();
		renderSystemLine(kind === 'date' ? d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
			weekday: 'long',
			year: 'numeric',
			month: 'long',
			day: 'numeric'
		}) : d.toLocaleTimeString());
	}

	function setLang(code) {
		code = String(code || '').trim();
		if (!(engine && engine.setUserLang)) return;
		if (/^(off|none|clear|default)$/i.test(code)) {
			engine.setUserLang(USER_ID, null);
			renderSystemLine('reply language: default (English)');
			return;
		}
		if (!code) {
			if (engine.getUserLang) {
				Promise.resolve(engine.getUserLang(USER_ID)).then(function(lg) {
					renderSystemLine(lg ? ('reply language: ' + lg + '  \u00b7  /lang off for English') : 'reply language: default (English)  \u00b7  /lang fr, es, de, ja\u2026 to change');
				});
			} else renderSystemLine('reply language: /lang fr, es, de, ja\u2026  \u00b7  /lang off for English');
			return;
		}
		engine.setUserLang(USER_ID, code);
		renderSystemLine('reply language set to ' + code + '  \u00b7  /lang off for English');
	}
	var AUTO_LANGS = {
		fr: 1,
		es: 1,
		de: 1,
		it: 1,
		pt: 1,
		nl: 1,
		ru: 1,
		ja: 1,
		ko: 1,
		zh: 1,
		ar: 1,
		hi: 1,
		tr: 1,
		pl: 1,
		sv: 1,
		uk: 1,
		vi: 1,
		id: 1,
		th: 1,
		el: 1,
		he: 1,
		cs: 1,
		ro: 1,
		fi: 1
	};

	function autoMatchLang() {
		try {
			if (state.matchDeviceLang === false) return;
			if (state.langAutoTried) return;
			state.langAutoTried = true;
			persistState();
			if (!(engine && engine.setUserLang && engine.getUserLang)) return;
			Promise.resolve(engine.getUserLang(USER_ID)).then(function(cur) {
				if (cur) return;
				var code = String(browserContext().locale || '').toLowerCase().split(/[-_]/)[0];
				if (!code || code === 'en' || !AUTO_LANGS[code]) return;
				setLang(code);
			}).catch(function() {});
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function statusCmd() {
		Promise.all([
			(engine && engine.getFacts) ? Promise.resolve(engine.getFacts(USER_ID)).catch(function() {
				return [];
			}) : Promise.resolve([]), (engine && engine.goalsForOwner) ? Promise.resolve(engine.goalsForOwner(USER_ID)).catch(function() {
				return [];
			}) : Promise.resolve([]), (engine && engine.listReminders) ? Promise.resolve(engine.listReminders(USER_ID)).catch(function() {
				return [];
			}) : Promise.resolve([]),
			store.get('mood:' + CH).catch(function() {
				return null;
			}),
			storageHealth()
		]).then(function(r) {
			var facts = r[0] || [],
				goals = r[1] || [],
				rem = r[2] || [],
				mood = r[3],
				h = r[4];
			var moodStr = (mood && engine && engine.moodDescriptor) ? engine.moodDescriptor(mood) : '';
			var bits = [queue.length + ' messages', facts.length + ' facts about you', goals.length + ' goals', rem.length + ' reminders'];
			if (moodStr) bits.push('mood: ' + moodStr);
			if (h && h.supported && h.persisted != null) bits.push(h.persisted ? 'storage durable' : 'storage best-effort');
			if (h && h.usage != null && h.quota) bits.push(fmtBytes(h.usage) + ' used');
			renderSystemLine('status - ' + bits.join(' \u00b7 '));
		});
	}

	function personaCmd(rest) {
		rest = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (/^clear$/i.test(rest)) {
			var cl = (engine && engine.clearPersonaNote) ? engine.clearPersonaNote() : store.del('persona:note');
			Promise.resolve(cl).then(function() {
				renderSystemLine('persona note cleared');
			});
			return;
		}
		if (!rest) {
			var g = (engine && engine.getPersonaNote) ? engine.getPersonaNote() : store.get('persona:note');
			Promise.resolve(g).then(function(pn) {
				renderSystemLine((pn && pn.text) ? ('persona note: ' + pn.text) : '(no persona note - /persona <a style note> to set one)');
			});
			return;
		}
		Promise.resolve(store.set('persona:note', personaNoteRecord(rest))).then(function() {
			renderSystemLine('persona note set: ' + rest);
		});
	}

	function personaNoteRecord(text, by) {
		return {
			msgId: 'manual' + Date.now(),
			text: String(text || '').trim(),
			by: by || userNameStr(),
			at: Date.now(),
			persona: null
		};
	}

	function renderPersonaNote() {
		var ta = byId('personaNoteInput');
		if (!ta) return;
		var g = (engine && engine.getPersonaNote) ? engine.getPersonaNote() : (typeof store !== 'undefined' ? store.get('persona:note') : null);
		Promise.resolve(g).then(function(pn) {
			ta.value = (pn && pn.text) ? pn.text : '';
		}).catch(function() {});
	}

	function savePersonaNote() {
		var ta = byId('personaNoteInput');
		if (!ta) return;
		var v = ta.value.trim();
		if (!v) {
			var cl = (engine && engine.clearPersonaNote) ? engine.clearPersonaNote() : store.del('persona:note');
			Promise.resolve(cl).then(function() {
				toast('Self-note cleared', 'success');
				pushThought('[chloe.learn] cleared how I describe myself');
			});
			return;
		}
		Promise.resolve(store.set('persona:note', personaNoteRecord(v))).then(function() {
			toast('Self-note saved', 'success');
			pushThought('[chloe.learn] updated how I see myself: ' + v);
		});
	}

	function parseDur(s) {
		var m = String(s || '').trim().match(/^(\d+)\s*([mhd])$/i);
		if (!m) return null;
		var n = +m[1],
			u = m[2].toLowerCase();
		return u === 'm' ? n : (u === 'h' ? n * 60 : n * 1440);
	}

	function remindCmd(rest) {
		rest = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (/^clear$/i.test(rest)) {
			if (engine && engine.clearReminders) Promise.resolve(engine.clearReminders(USER_ID)).then(function(n) {
				renderSystemLine(n ? ('cleared ' + n + ' reminder' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's')) : 'no reminders to clear');
			});
			return;
		}
		if (/^(list|)$/i.test(rest) && rest.indexOf(' ') < 0 && !parseDur(rest.split(' ')[0])) {
			if (engine && engine.listReminders) Promise.resolve(engine.listReminders(USER_ID)).then(function(list) {
				list = list || [];
				renderSystemLine(list.length ? ('reminders: ' + list.map(function(r) {
					return r.text;
				}).join(' \u00b7 ')) : 'no reminders set');
			});
			return;
		}
		var m = rest.match(/^(\S+)\s+([\s\S]+)$/);
		var mins = m ? parseDur(m[1]) : null;
		if (!m || !mins) {
			renderSystemLine('(/remind 10m drink water  -  units: m, h, d)');
			return;
		}
		if (engine && engine.scheduleReminder) Promise.resolve(engine.scheduleReminder({
			minutes: mins,
			text: m[2],
			authorId: USER_ID,
			authorName: userNameStr()
		})).then(function(r) {
			renderSystemLine((r && r.ok === false) ? ('(' + r.reason + ')') : ('reminder set in ' + m[1] + ': ' + m[2]));
		});
	}

	function runJsonJob(obj) {
		if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object') {
			renderSystemLine('(that JSON wasn\u2019t a job I recognise)');
			return;
		}
		var type = String(obj.type || obj.cmd || (obj.prompt ? 'image' : '')).toLowerCase();
		if (type === 'image' || type === 'img') {
			doImage(obj);
			return;
		}
		if (type === 'remind' || type === 'reminder') {
			if (engine && engine.scheduleReminder) Promise.resolve(engine.scheduleReminder({
				minutes: Number(obj.minutes || obj.mins) || 0,
				text: obj.text || '',
				authorId: USER_ID,
				authorName: userNameStr()
			})).then(function(r) {
				renderSystemLine((r && r.ok === false) ? ('(' + r.reason + ')') : ('reminder set: ' + (obj.text || '')));
			});
			return;
		}
		if (type === 'mem' || type === 'memory') {
			addMemory(obj.text);
			return;
		}
		if (type === 'goal') {
			addGoalCmd(obj.text);
			return;
		}
		if (type === 'sys' || type === 'system') {
			addSysDirective(obj.text);
			return;
		}
		if (type === 'nar' || type === 'narrate' || type === 'narration') {
			addNarration(obj.text);
			return;
		}
		renderSystemLine('(unrecognised job type: ' + (type || 'none') + ')');
	}
	var COMMANDS = [{

	function retryCmd() {
		var m = lastBotMsg();
		if (!m) {
			renderSystemLine('(no reply to retry yet)');
			return;
		}
		var wrap = wrapFor(m);
		if (!wrap) {
			renderSystemLine('(can\u2019t find that reply to retry)');
			return;
		}
		ensureVariants(m, m.vkind === 'image', (function() {
			var im = wrap.querySelector('img.avatar');
			return im ? im.src : '';
		})());
		regenerateMessage(wrap, m);
	}

	function undoCmd() {
		if (!queue.length) {
			renderSystemLine('(nothing to undo)');
			return;
		}
		var m = queue[queue.length - 1];
		var wrap = wrapFor(m);
		if (wrap) {
			removeMessage(wrap);
		} else {
			queue.pop();
			persistQueue();
			renderSystemLine('(removed the last message)');
		}
	}

	function rollDice(spec) {
		spec = String(spec || '').trim().toLowerCase() || 'd20';
		var mm = spec.match(/^(\d*)d(\d+)([+-]\d+)?$/);
		if (!mm) return null;
		var n = Math.max(1, Math.min(100, parseInt(mm[1] || '1', 10))),
			sides = Math.max(2, Math.min(1000, parseInt(mm[2], 10))),
			mod = parseInt(mm[3] || '0', 10);
		var rolls = [],
			sum = 0;
		for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
			var r = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * sides);
			rolls.push(r);
			sum += r;
		}
		return {
			n: n,
			sides: sides,
			mod: mod,
			rolls: rolls,
			total: sum + mod
		};
	}

	function storageCmd() {
		storageHealth().then(function(h) {
			if (h && h.persisted === false) {
				requestPersistentStorage().then(function() {
					storageHealth().then(function(h2) {
						renderSystemLine(storageLine(h2));
						if (h2.persisted !== true) {
							renderSystemLine('(the browser keeps storage best-effort until it trusts a site - bookmarking this page or installing it as an app usually flips it to durable; either way, Export is your backup)', true);
						}
					});
				});
			} else {
				renderSystemLine(storageLine(h));
			}
		});
	}

	function continueScene(hint) {
		var lead = (hint && String(hint).trim()) || 'Continue the scene yourself: take the next beat - make a move, advance what\u2019s happening, or say the next thing in character. Don\u2019t wait for the user.';
		proactive('beat', lead);
	}

	function rollCmd(rest) {
		var d = rollDice(rest);
		if (!d) {
			renderSystemLine('(roll like /roll d20, /roll 2d6, or /roll d20+3)');
			return;
		}
		var detail = (d.n > 1 || d.mod) ? (' (' + d.rolls.join(' + ') + (d.mod ? (d.mod > 0 ? ' + ' + d.mod : ' - ' + Math.abs(d.mod)) : '') + ')') : '';
		renderSystemLine('\ud83c\udfb2 ' + d.n + 'd' + d.sides + (d.mod ? (d.mod > 0 ? '+' + d.mod : String(d.mod)) : '') + ' \u2192 ' + d.total + detail, true);
	}

	function handleComposerCommand(text) {
		var t = String(text || '').trim();
		if (t.charAt(0) === '{') {
			var obj = null;
			try {
				obj = JSON.parse(t);
			} catch (e) {
				obj = null;
			}
			if (obj) {
				runJsonJob(obj);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		if (t.charAt(0) !== '/') return false;
		var sp = t.indexOf(' ');
		var verb = (sp < 0 ? t.slice(1) : t.slice(1, sp)).toLowerCase();
		var rest = sp < 0 ? '' : t.slice(sp + 1).trim();
		switch (verb) {
			case 'img':
			case 'image':
				if (rest.charAt(0) === '{') {
					var o = null;
					try {
						o = JSON.parse(rest);
					} catch (e) {}
					doImage(o || {
						prompt: rest
					});
				} else doImage({
					prompt: rest
				});
				return true;
			case 'frame':
				{
					var fa = rest.toLowerCase().trim().split(/\s+/);
					if (!rest || fa[0] === 'show') {
						renderSystemLine('(role frame: ' + (window.ChloeFrame ? window.ChloeFrame.describe(state.frame) : 'default') + ' - /frame drive you|shared|she \u00b7 alignment ally|neutral|adversary \u00b7 stature yields|equal|commands \u00b7 /frame off)');
						return true;
					}
					if (fa[0] === 'off' || fa[0] === 'reset' || fa[0] === 'companion') {
						setFrame(null);
						renderFramePanel();
						renderSystemLine('(role frame: back to companion)');
						return true;
					}
					var FDIMS = {
						drive: ['you', 'shared', 'she'],
						alignment: ['ally', 'neutral', 'adversary'],
						stature: ['yields', 'equal', 'commands']
					};
					if (FDIMS[fa[0]] && FDIMS[fa[0]].indexOf(fa[1]) >= 0) {
						var fp = {};
						fp[fa[0]] = fa[1];
						setFrame(fp);
						renderFramePanel();
						renderSystemLine('(role frame: ' + window.ChloeFrame.describe(state.frame) + ')');
						return true;
					}
					renderSystemLine('(frame: drive you|shared|she \u00b7 alignment ally|neutral|adversary \u00b7 stature yields|equal|commands \u00b7 off)');
					return true;
				}
			case 'mem':
			case 'remember':
				addMemory(rest);
				return true;
			case 'stance':
				{
					var sname = rest.toLowerCase().trim();
					if (['companion', 'roleplay', 'author', 'assist', 'auto', 'off'].indexOf(sname) < 0) {
						renderSystemLine('(stance: companion | roleplay | author | assist | auto)');
						return true;
					}
					setStancePin((sname === 'auto' || sname === 'off') ? null : sname);
					renderSystemLine((sname === 'auto' || sname === 'off') ? '(stance: back to auto - reading the conversation again)' : ('(stance pinned: ' + sname + ')'));
					return true;
				}
			case 'sys':
			case 'system':
				addSysDirective(rest);
				return true;
			case 'nar':
			case 'narrate':
				{
					var nt = String(rest || '').trim();
					if (nt) addNarration(nt);
					else continueScene();
					return true;
				}
			case 'continue':
			case 'go':
				continueScene(rest);
				return true;
			case 'me':
			case 'action':
				{
					var act = String(rest || '').trim();
					if (!act) {
						renderSystemLine('(an action, e.g. /me waves hello)');
						return true;
					}
					submitUserText(emoteText(act));
					return true;
				}
			case 'retry':
			case 'regen':
			case 'regenerate':
				retryCmd();
				return true;
			case 'undo':
				undoCmd();
				return true;
			case 'roll':
			case 'dice':
				rollCmd(rest);
				return true;
			case 'recap':
				proactive('recap');
				return true;
			case 'beat':
				proactive('beat', rest);
				return true;
			case 'volunteer':
			case 'nudge':
