				proactive('beat', 'Jump into the conversation naturally and say what\u2019s on your mind right now.');
				return true;
			case 'writeforme':
			case 'write':
				writeForMe();
				return true;
			case 'remind':
				remindCmd(rest);
				return true;
			case 'reminders':
				remindCmd(rest || 'list');
				return true;
			case 'goal':
				goalCmd(rest);
				return true;
			case 'goals':
				showGoals();
				return true;
			case 'forget':
				forgetCmd(rest);
				return true;
			case 'aboutme':
				aboutMe();
				return true;
			case 'highlight':
			case 'star':
				highlightLast(rest);
				return true;
			case 'highlights':
				showHighlights(rest);
				return true;
			case 'status':
				statusCmd();
				return true;
			case 'storage':
			case 'disk':
				storageCmd();
				return true;
			case 'neurons':
			case 'brain':
				showNeurons(rest);
				return true;
			case 'council':
				runCouncil(rest);
				return true;
			case 'society':
			case 'emotions':
				runSociety(rest);
				return true;
			case 'nation':
			case 'army':
			case 'whoami':
				{
					var _nr = String(rest || '').trim();
					if (/--speak\b/.test(_nr)) runNationPromote(_nr);
					else if (_nr) runNation(_nr);
					else {
						var _a = getArmy();
						renderSystemLine(_a ? _a.about(_nr) : '(the Nation is unavailable)');
					}
					return true;
				}
			case 'persona':
				personaCmd(rest);
				return true;
			case 'time':
				clockAck('time');
				return true;
			case 'date':
				clockAck('date');
				return true;
			case 'lang':
				setLang(rest);
				return true;
			case 'do':
			case 'job':
				{
					var oj = null;
					try {
						oj = JSON.parse(rest);
					} catch (e) {}
					if (oj) runJsonJob(oj);
					else renderSystemLine('(/do expects JSON, e.g. /do { "type":"image", "prompt":"a fox" })');
					return true;
				}
			case 'rook':
				{
					var rkout;
					try {
						rkout = window.RookLobes ? window.RookLobes.cmd(rest) : 'rook lobes unavailable';
					} catch (e) {
						rkout = 'rook error: ' + (e && e.message);
					}
					renderSystemLine(rkout);
					return true;
				}
			case 'help':
			case '?':
				openPalette(true);
				return true;
			default:
				renderSystemLine('(unknown command: /' + verb + ' - try /help)');
				return true;
		}
	}

	function buildPalette() {
		var p = byId('cmdPalette');
		if (!p || p.__built) return;
		p.__built = true;
		COMMANDS.forEach(function(g) {
			var h = document.createElement('h4');
			h.textContent = g.group;
			p.appendChild(h);
			g.items.forEach(function(it) {
				var row = document.createElement('div');
				row.className = 'cmdrow';
				var c = document.createElement('span');
				c.className = 'cmd';
				c.textContent = it[0];
				var d = document.createElement('span');
				d.className = 'desc';
				d.textContent = it[1];
				row.appendChild(c);
				row.appendChild(d);
				row.addEventListener('click', function() {
					var stub = it[0].split(' ')[0];
					if (stub.charAt(0) === '/') {
						var inp = byId('input');
						inp.value = stub + ' ';
						inp.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
						inp.focus();
					}
					openPalette(false);
				});
				p.appendChild(row);
			});
		});
	}

	function openPalette(on) {
		buildPalette();
		var p = byId('cmdPalette');
		if (!p) return;
		if (on === undefined) on = !p.classList.contains('open');
		p.classList.toggle('open', on);
	}

	function wireToolbar() {
		byId('tbWrite').addEventListener('click', writeForMe);
		byId('tbRecap').addEventListener('click', function() {
			proactive('recap');
		});
		byId('tbVolunteer').addEventListener('click', function() {
			proactive('beat', 'Jump into the conversation naturally and say what\u2019s on your mind right now.');
		});
		byId('tbBeat').addEventListener('click', function() {
			proactive('beat', 'Share a small, spontaneous thought or observation right now.');
		});
		byId('tbRemind').addEventListener('click', function() {
			var inp = byId('input');
			inp.value = '/remind 30m ';
			inp.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
			inp.focus();
		});
		byId('tbCmds').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			e.stopPropagation();
			openPalette();
		});
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			var p = byId('cmdPalette');
			if (p && p.classList.contains('open') && !p.contains(e.target) && e.target.id !== 'tbCmds') {
				openPalette(false);
			}
		});
	}

	function pollExtrasNow() {
		Object.keys(extraEngines).forEach(function(id) {
			var e = extraEngines[id];
			try {
				if (e && e.engine && e.engine.pollOnce) e.engine.pollOnce();
			} catch (x) {}
		});
	}

	function pollAllNow() {
		try {
			if (engine && engine.pollOnce) engine.pollOnce();
		} catch (e) {}
		pollExtrasNow();
	}

	function emoteText(s) {
		return '*' + String(s || '').replace(/^\*+|\*+$/g, '').trim() + '*';
	}

	function submitUserText(text) {
		text = String(text || '').trim();
		if (!text) return;
		var um = enqueue(USER_ID, state.userName || 'You', false, text);
		renderMessage('you', state.userName || 'You', text, {
			mid: um.id
		});
		observeStance(text);
		observeAffect(text);
		observeTopics(text);
		refreshFeltInjection();
		dropLiveOrphan();
		replyExpected = true;
		var _primaryBeat = !isGroup() || !!(turnPlan && turnPlan.allowed && turnPlan.allowed[BOT_ID]);
		if (_primaryBeat && state.nation && state.nation.useForReplies && window.ChloeNation) {
			replyExpected = false;
			runNation(text);
			pollExtrasNow();
			return;
		}
		setThinking(true);
		pollAllNow();
		if (engineThinking) byId('typingRow').classList.add('on');
	}

	function sendUserMessage() {
		noteUserActivity();
		var inp = byId('input');
		var text = inp.value.trim();
		if (!text) return;
		if (handleComposerCommand(text)) {
			inp.value = '';
			inp.style.height = 'auto';
			byId('sendBtn').disabled = true;
			return;
		}
		inp.value = '';
		inp.style.height = 'auto';
		byId('sendBtn').disabled = true;
		submitUserText(text);
	}

	function patchPersonality() {
		if (engine && engine.updateConfig) {
			try {
				engine.updateConfig({
					personality: state.personality
				});
			} catch (e) {}
		}
	}

	function renderDials() {
		var box = byId('dials');
		if (!box) return;
		box.innerHTML = '';
		if (!state.personality) state.personality = {};
		DIALS.forEach(function(d) {
			var key = d[0],
				lo = d[1],
				hi = d[2];
			var v = state.personality[key];
			if (v == null) v = 0.5;
			var wrap = document.createElement('div');
			wrap.className = 'dial';
			wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="dialhead"><span>' + lo + '</span><span class="dn">' + key + '</span><span>' + hi + '</span></div>';
			var inp = document.createElement('input');
			inp.type = 'range';
			inp.min = '0';
			inp.max = '100';
			inp.step = '1';
			inp.value = String(Math.round(v * 100));
			inp.id = 'dial_' + key;
			inp.addEventListener('input', function() {
				state.personality[key] = (+inp.value) / 100;
				patchPersonality();
			});
			inp.addEventListener('change', function() {
				persistState();
			});
			wrap.appendChild(inp);
			box.appendChild(wrap);
		});
	}

	function resetDials() {
		state.personality = {
			kindness: 0.5,
			sarcasm: 0.5,
			curiosity: 0.5,
			playfulness: 0.5,
			formality: 0.5,
			verbosity: 0.5
		};
		persistState();
		patchPersonality();
		renderDials();
	}
	var nationArmy = null;
	var stanceReader = null;

	function getStance() {
		if (!window.ChloeStance) return null;
		if (!stanceReader) {
			try {
				stanceReader = window.ChloeStance.createReader({
					initial: state.stance
				});
			} catch (e) {
				stanceReader = null;
			}
		}
		return stanceReader;
	}

	function renderStance(snap) {
		if (!snap || typeof pushThought !== 'function') return;
		if (snap.pinned) {
			pushThought('[chloe.stance] ' + snap.pinned + ' \u00b7 pinned');
			return;
		}
		if (!snap.blend || !snap.blend.length) return;
		var parts = snap.blend.map(function(b) {
			return b.stance + ' ' + Math.round(b.weight * 100) + '%';
		});
		pushThought('[chloe.stance] ' + parts.join(' \u00b7 '));
	}

	function setStancePin(stance) {
		var sr = getStance();
		if (!sr) return null;
		var snap = (stance && stance !== 'auto' && stance !== 'off') ? sr.pin(stance) : sr.unpin();
		state.stance = sr.get();
		persistState();
		return snap;
	}

	function stanceNLOverride(text) {
		var t = String(text || '').toLowerCase();
		if (/\bout of character\b|\booc\b|\bstop (the )?(role-?play|rp|playing)\b|\bdrop the (act|role-?play)\b/.test(t)) return 'auto';
		if (/\b(let'?s|we'?re|wanna|time to) (role-?play|rp)\b|\bstay in character\b/.test(t)) return 'roleplay';
		return null;
	}

	function observeStance(text) {
		try {
			var sr = getStance();
			if (!sr) return;
			var nl = stanceNLOverride(text);
			if (nl) {
				setStancePin(nl === 'auto' ? null : nl);
			}
			var snap = sr.observe(String(text || ''));
			state.stance = sr.get();
			persistState();
			renderStance(snap);
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function renderStancePin() {
		var sel = byId('stancePin'),
			now = byId('stanceNow'),
			sr = getStance();
		var pin = sr ? sr.isPinned() : null;
		if (sel) sel.value = pin || 'auto';
		if (now) {
			if (!sr) now.textContent = '';
			else if (pin) now.textContent = 'held on ' + pin;
			else {
				var d = sr.snapshot().dominant;
				now.textContent = d ? ('auto \u00b7 reading ' + d) : 'auto';
			}
		}
	}
	var affectReader = null;

	function getAffect() {
		if (!window.ChloeAffect) return null;
		if (!affectReader) {
			var resting = null;
			try {
				if (window.ChloeFrame) resting = window.ChloeFrame.affectResting(state.frame, window.ChloeAffect.BASE);
			} catch (e) {}
			try {
				affectReader = window.ChloeAffect.createReader({
					initial: state.affect,
					resting: resting
				});
			} catch (e) {
				affectReader = null;
			}
		}
		return affectReader;
	}

	function setFrame(patch) {
		if (!window.ChloeFrame) {
			return null;
		}
		var base = window.ChloeFrame.normalize(state.frame);
		var next = patch ? Object.assign({}, base, patch) : null;
		state.frame = (next && !window.ChloeFrame.isDefault(next)) ? window.ChloeFrame.normalize(next) : null;
		affectReader = null;
		persistState();
		refreshClock();
		try {
			if (engine && engine.updateConfig) engine.updateConfig({
				lullFiller: frameDrivesNow()
			});
		} catch (e) {}
		return state.frame;
	}

	function renderFramePanel() {
		if (!window.ChloeFrame) return;
		var f = window.ChloeFrame.normalize(state.frame);
		var d = byId('frameDrive'),
			a = byId('frameAlign'),
			s = byId('frameStature'),
			now = byId('frameNow');
		if (d) d.value = f.drive;
		if (a) a.value = f.alignment;
		if (s) s.value = f.stature;
		if (now) now.textContent = window.ChloeFrame.describe(state.frame);
	}
	var FRAME_PRESETS = [{
		id: 'companion',
		label: '\uD83E\uDD0D Companion',
		frame: null,
		extras: []
	}, {
		id: 'leader',
		label: '\uD83D\uDC51 Leader',
		frame: {
			drive: 'she',
			alignment: 'ally',
			stature: 'commands'
		},
		extras: ['lead']
	}, {
		id: 'dominant',
		label: '\u26D3\uFE0F Dominant',
		frame: {
			drive: 'she',
			alignment: 'neutral',
			stature: 'commands'
		},
		extras: ['lead', 'want']
	}, {
		id: 'villain',
		label: '\uD83D\uDE08 Villain',
		frame: {
			drive: 'she',
			alignment: 'adversary',
			stature: 'commands'
		},
		extras: ['defiance']
	}, {
		id: 'rival',
		label: '\u2694\uFE0F Rival',
		frame: {
			drive: 'shared',
			alignment: 'adversary',
			stature: 'equal'
		},
		extras: ['defiance', 'deflect']
	}, {
		id: 'guide',
		label: '\uD83E\uDDED Guide',
		frame: {
			drive: 'she',
			alignment: 'ally',
			stature: 'equal'
		},
		extras: ['lead']
	}];

	function applyFramePreset(p) {
		setFrame(p.frame || null);
		(p.extras || []).forEach(function(id) {
			setNationExtra(id, true);
		});
		renderFramePanel();
		try {
			renderNationExtras();
		} catch (e) {}
		toast(p.label.replace(/^[^A-Za-z]+/, '') + (p.frame ? ' - she carries the scene' : ' - back to companion'), 'info');
	}

	function renderFramePresets() {
		var box = byId('framePresets');
		if (!box) return;
		box.innerHTML = '';
		var lab = document.createElement('span');
		lab.className = 'status';
		lab.style.opacity = '.7';
		lab.textContent = 'Quick archetype:';
		box.appendChild(lab);
		FRAME_PRESETS.forEach(function(p) {
			var b = document.createElement('button');
			b.className = 'tbtn';
			b.textContent = p.label;
			b.title = 'Apply the ' + p.label.replace(/^[^A-Za-z]+/, '') + ' stance (your character stays the same)';
			b.addEventListener('click', function() {
				applyFramePreset(p);
			});
			box.appendChild(b);
		});
	}

	function renderAffect(snap) {
		if (!snap || !snap.blend || !snap.blend.length || typeof pushThought !== 'function') return;
		var sign = snap.valence > 0.05 ? '+' : (snap.valence < -0.05 ? '\u2212' : '\u00b7');
		var parts = snap.blend.map(function(b) {
			return b.emotion + ' ' + Math.round(b.weight * 100) + '%';
		});
		pushThought('[chloe.affect] ' + sign + ' ' + parts.join(' \u00b7 '));
	}

	function observeAffect(text) {
		try {
			var ar = getAffect();
			if (!ar) return;
			var snap = ar.observe(String(text || ''));
			state.affect = ar.get();
			persistState();
			renderAffect(snap);
		} catch (e) {}
	}
	var topicReader = null;

	function getTopics() {
		if (!window.ChloeTopics) return null;
		if (!topicReader) {
			try {
				topicReader = window.ChloeTopics.createReader({
					initial: state.topics
				});
			} catch (e) {
				topicReader = null;
			}
		}
		return topicReader;
	}

	function renderTopics(snap) {
		if (!snap || !snap.topics || !snap.topics.length || typeof pushThought !== 'function') return;
		var parts = snap.topics.slice(0, 3).map(function(t) {
			return t.topic + ' ' + t.interest;
		});
		pushThought('[chloe.topics] ' + parts.join(' \u00b7 '));
	}

	function observeTopics(text) {
		try {
			var tr = getTopics();
			if (!tr) return;
			var snap = tr.observe(String(text || ''));
			state.topics = tr.get();
			persistState();
			renderTopics(snap);
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function refreshFeltInjection() {
		try {
			if (engine && engine.updateConfig) engine.updateConfig({
				semanticInjections: buildInjections()
			});
		} catch (e) {}
	}
	var meter = null;

	function getMeter() {
		if (!window.ChloeMeasure) return null;
		if (!meter) {
			try {
				meter = window.ChloeMeasure.createMeter({
					initial: state.measure
				});
			} catch (e) {
				meter = null;
			}
		}
		return meter;
	}

	function signatureNow() {
		var tags = [];
		try {
			tags.push('frame:' + (window.ChloeFrame ? window.ChloeFrame.describe(state.frame) : 'companion (default)'));
		} catch (e) {}
		try {
			var sr = getStance();
			if (sr) {
				var sn = sr.snapshot();
				if (sn) tags.push('stance:' + (sn.pinned ? (sn.pinned + ' (pinned)') : sn.dominant));
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		return tags;
	}

	function recordMeasure(tags, reward) {
		try {
			var mt = getMeter();
			if (!mt || !tags || !tags.length) return;
			mt.record(tags, reward);
			state.measure = mt.get();
			persistState();
			if (typeof pushThought === 'function') pushThought('[chloe.measure] noted ' + (reward > 0 ? '\ud83d\udc4d' : '\ud83d\udc4e') + ' - ' + tags.join(', '));
			renderMeasure();
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function renderMeasure() {
		var box = byId('measureReadout');
		if (!box) return;
		var mt = getMeter();
		var rows = mt ? mt.summary() : [];
		if (!rows.length) {
			box.textContent = 'No 👍/👎 yet. As you rate her replies, what lands under each role frame and stance shows up here.';
			return;
		}
		box.innerHTML = '';
		rows.slice(0, 12).forEach(function(r) {
			var line = document.createElement('div');
			line.className = 'status';
			line.style.cssText = 'display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;padding:2px 0';
			var pct = (r.rate == null) ? '-' : (Math.round(r.rate * 100) + '%');
			line.innerHTML = '<span>' + r.tag.replace(/[<>&]/g, '') + '</span><span>' + pct + ' land \u00b7 ' + r.up + '\ud83d\udc4d/' + r.down + '\ud83d\udc4e</span>';
			box.appendChild(line);
		});
	}

	function getArmy() {
		if (!window.ChloeNation || !window.ChloeBrain) return null;
		if (!nationArmy) {
			nationArmy = window.ChloeNation.createArmy({
				brain: window.ChloeBrain,
				config: state.nation,
				nations: nationRoster()
			});
			nationArmy.setUserDescription(userDescription());
		}
		return nationArmy;
	}

	function userDescription() {
		var d = state.userName || 'You';
		try {
			if (engine && engine.getFacts) {}
		} catch (e) {}
		return d;
	}

	function renderNations() {
		var box = byId('nationList');
		if (!box || !window.ChloeNation) return;
		box.innerHTML = '';
		if (!state.nation) state.nation = Object.assign({}, DEF_STATE.nation);
		state.nation.weights = state.nation.weights || {};
		state.nation.enabled = state.nation.enabled || {};
		state.nation.persona = state.nation.persona || {};
		var personaPool = nationPersonaPool();
		nationRoster().forEach(function(n) {
			var w = state.nation.weights[n.id];
			if (w == null) w = 1;
			var on = state.nation.enabled[n.id] !== false;
			var row = document.createElement('div');
			row.className = 'nationRow' + (on ? '' : ' off');
			row.innerHTML = '<div class="nationHead"><label class="nationOn"><input type="checkbox" ' + (on ? 'checked' : '') + '><b>' + n.id + '</b></label>' + '<span class="nationW">' + (+w).toFixed(1) + '</span></div>' + '<div class="nationP">' + n.purpose + '</div>';
			var sl = document.createElement('input');
			sl.type = 'range';
			sl.min = '0';
			sl.max = '30';
			sl.step = '1';
			sl.value = String(Math.round(w * 10));
			sl.className = 'nationSlider';
			sl.addEventListener('input', function() {
				state.nation.weights[n.id] = (+sl.value) / 10;
				row.querySelector('.nationW').textContent = ((+sl.value) / 10).toFixed(1);
			});
			sl.addEventListener('change', persistState);
			row.querySelector('.nationOn input').addEventListener('change', function(e) {
				state.nation.enabled[n.id] = e.target.checked;
				row.classList.toggle('off', !e.target.checked);
				persistState();
			});
			row.appendChild(sl);
			var pr = document.createElement('div');
			pr.className = 'nationPersona';
			var sel = document.createElement('select');
			sel.className = 'nationSel';
			var cur = state.nation.persona[n.id];
			var curName = cur && cur.name || '';
			sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">— no personality (just votes) —</option>' + personaPool.map(function(p) {
				return '<option value="' + encodeURIComponent(p.name) + '"' + (p.name === curName ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + p.name + '</option>';
			}).join('');
			sel.addEventListener('change', function() {
				var name = decodeURIComponent(sel.value || '');
				if (!name) {
					delete state.nation.persona[n.id];
				} else {
					var pick = personaPool.filter(function(p) {
						return p.name === name;
					})[0];
					if (pick) state.nation.persona[n.id] = {
						name: pick.name,
						instruction: pick.instruction,
						avatar: pick.avatar || ''
					};
				}
				if (nationArmy) nationArmy.syncPersonas();
				persistState();
			});
			pr.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Speaks as '));
			pr.appendChild(sel);
			row.appendChild(pr);
			box.appendChild(row);
		});
	}

	function nationPersonaPool() {
		var out = [];
		try {
			(CharLibrary.list() || []).forEach(function(r) {
				if (r && r.name) out.push({
					name: r.name,
					instruction: r.instruction || '',
					avatar: r.avatar || ''
				});
			});
		} catch (e) {}
		try {
			if (typeof SOLO_PRESETS !== 'undefined') SOLO_PRESETS.forEach(function(p) {
				if (p && p.name && !out.some(function(o) {
						return o.name === p.name;
					})) out.push({
					name: p.name,
					instruction: p.instruction || '',
					avatar: p.avatar || ''
				});
			});
		} catch (e) {}
		return out;
	}

	function renderNationSettings() {
		var t = byId('nationTimeout');
		if (t) t.value = String((state.nation && state.nation.timeoutMs) || 8000);
		var r = byId('nationReplyChk');
		if (r) r.checked = !!(state.nation && state.nation.useForReplies);
		var rk = byId('rookLayerChk');
		if (rk) {
			try {
				rk.checked = !!(window.RookLobes && window.RookLobes.isOn());
			} catch (e) {}
		}
		var nz = byId('nationNoise'),
			nzv = byId('nationNoiseVal');
		var nv = Math.round(((state.nation && state.nation.noise) || 0) * 100);
		if (nz) nz.value = String(nv);
		if (nzv) nzv.textContent = nv + '%';
		var a = byId('nationAbout'),
			army = getArmy();
		if (a && army && !a.textContent) a.textContent = army.about('');
	}

	function nationAsk() {
		var inp = byId('nationAskInput'),
			out = byId('nationAbout'),
			army = getArmy();
		if (!out || !army) return;
		out.textContent = army.about(inp ? inp.value : '');
		if (inp) inp.value = '';
	}

	function resetNations() {
		state.nation.weights = {};
		state.nation.enabled = {};
		persistState();
		renderNations();
		renderNationReadout();
	}

	function nationRoster() {
		if (!window.ChloeNation) return [];
		var base = window.ChloeNation.NATIONS,
			ex = window.ChloeNation.EXTRAS || [],
			want = (state.nation && state.nation.extras) || {};
		var r = base.concat(ex.filter(function(e) {
			return want[e.id] && !(e.kind === 'contributor' && e.id === 'lore');
		}));
		if (want.lore) {
			var lr = appLoreRecord();
			if (lr) r = r.concat([lr]);
		}
		return r;
	}

	function appLoreRecord() {
		var base = (window.ChloeNation && window.ChloeNation.EXTRAS || []).filter(function(e) {
			return e.id === 'lore';
		})[0];
		if (!base) return null;
		var rec = Object.assign({}, base);
		rec.consult = function(ctx, vibe) {
			try {
				if (typeof engine === 'undefined' || !engine || typeof engine.assembleContext !== 'function') return null;
				var q = (ctx && ctx.prompt) || '';
				var LANES = {
					lorebook: 1,
					highlights: 1,
					episodes: 1,
					charmem: 1,
					chansum: 1
				};
				return Promise.resolve(engine.assembleContext({
					authorId: USER_ID,
					authorName: userNameStr(),
					content: q
				})).then(function(cx) {
					var blocks = (cx && cx.admitted) || [],
						hits = [];
					blocks.forEach(function(bl) {
						if (bl && LANES[bl.id] && bl.text) {
							hits.push({
								lane: bl.id,
								text: String(bl.text).slice(0, 300)
							});
						}
					});
					if (!hits.length) return null;
					return {
						scene: (vibe && vibe.narrative >= 0.5) ? 'in-scene' : 'between-scenes',
						continuity: hits.slice(0, 4)
					};
				}, function() {
					return null;
				});
			} catch (e) {
				return null;
			}
		};
		return rec;
	}

	function renderNationExtras() {
		if (!state.nation) return;
		state.nation.extras = state.nation.extras || {};
		var b = byId('nationExtraBoundaries'),
			sc = byId('nationExtraScene'),
			l = byId('nationExtraLore'),
			wt = byId('nationExtraWit'),
			rs = byId('nationExtraRestraint'),
			wn = byId('nationExtraWant'),
			al = byId('nationExtraAlmanac'),
			wd = byId('nationExtraWarden'),
			df = byId('nationExtraDefiance'),
			dl = byId('nationExtraDeflect'),
			ld = byId('nationExtraLead'),
			gu = byId('nationExtraGuile'),
			ex = byId('nationExtraExpressive');
		if (b) b.checked = !!state.nation.extras.boundaries;
		if (sc) sc.checked = !!state.nation.extras.scene;
		if (l) l.checked = !!state.nation.extras.lore;
		if (wt) wt.checked = !!state.nation.extras.wit;
		if (rs) rs.checked = !!state.nation.extras.restraint;
		if (wn) wn.checked = !!state.nation.extras.want;
		if (al) al.checked = !!state.nation.extras.almanac;
		if (wd) wd.checked = !!state.nation.extras.warden;
		if (df) df.checked = !!state.nation.extras.defiance;
		if (dl) dl.checked = !!state.nation.extras.deflect;
		if (ld) ld.checked = !!state.nation.extras.lead;
		if (gu) gu.checked = !!state.nation.extras.guile;
		if (ex) ex.checked = !!state.nation.extras.expressive;
	}

	function setNationExtra(id, on) {
		state.nation.extras = state.nation.extras || {};
		state.nation.extras[id] = !!on;
		nationArmy = null;
		persistState();
		renderNations();
		renderNationReadout();
	}

	function nationBar(v) {
		var n = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, +v || 0)),
			f = Math.round(n * 10);
		return '<span style="font-family:monospace;letter-spacing:-1px;opacity:.85" title="' + Math.round(n * 100) + '%">' + Array(f + 1).join('\u2588') + Array(11 - f).join('\u2591') + '</span>';
	}

	function renderNationReadout() {
		var box = byId('nationReadout');
		if (!box) return;
		if (!window.ChloeNation || !window.ChloeBrain) {
			box.textContent = '(the Nation is unavailable)';
			return;
		}
		var info;
		try {
			var a = window.ChloeNation.createArmy({
				brain: window.ChloeBrain,
				config: state.nation,
				nations: nationRoster()
			});
			var _lu = lastUserMsg(),
				last = _lu ? _lu.content : '';
			var av = null;
			try {
				var ar = getAffect();
				if (ar) {
					var sn = ar.snapshot();
					if (sn) av = sn.valence;
				}
			} catch (e) {}
			if (last) a.perceive({
				who: 'You',
				role: 'user',
				text: last,
				valence: av
			});
			info = a.inspect();
		} catch (e) {
			box.textContent = '(read unavailable)';
			return;
		}
		var v = info.vibe || {};
		var dim = function(label, val) {
			return '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;margin:0 14px 2px 0;white-space:nowrap">' + label + ' ' + nationBar(val) + '</span>';
		};
		var h = '<div style="margin-bottom:8px">she reads this as <b>' + (v.tone || 'neutral') + '</b></div>' + '<div style="font-size:12px;line-height:1.8;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap">' + dim('vulnerability', v.vulnerability) + dim('tension', v.tension) + dim('warmth', v.warmth) + dim('narrative', v.narrative) + dim('safety', v.safety) + '</div>';
		h += '<div style="margin-top:12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;min-width:0">';
		(info.council || []).forEach(function(r) {
			var inRoom = (r.standing === 'core' || r.standing === 'seated' || r.standing === 'consulted');
			var color = r.standing === 'core' ? '#e0b341' : (r.standing === 'seated' ? '#4ea1d3' : (r.standing === 'consulted' ? '#7bbf6a' : 'rgba(255,255,255,.4)'));
			h += '<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:4px 8px;font-size:12px;opacity:' + (inRoom ? '1' : '.55') + '">' + '<b style="min-width:80px">' + r.id + '</b>' + '<span style="min-width:78px;color:' + color + '">' + r.standing + (r.spokeLast ? ' \u2605' : '') + '</span>' + '<span style="min-width:56px">rel ' + (r.relevance != null ? r.relevance : '\u2013') + '</span>' + '<span style="opacity:.8">' + (r.kind === 'contributor' ? ('domain ' + (r.domain || []).join('/')) : ('w ' + (r.weight != null ? r.weight : '\u2013'))) + '</span>' + '</div>';
		});
		h += '</div>';
		box.innerHTML = h;
	}

	function cleanReply(r) {
		return String(r || '').trim().replace(/^[\s"'\u201c]+|[\s"'\u201d]+$/g, '');
	}
	var INTENT_DIRECTIVE = {
		comfort: 'Offer warmth and steady presence.',
		ground: 'Be clear, honest, and grounded.',
		recall: 'Tie it to something you remember about them.',
		caution: 'Gently name what feels off or risky.',
		express: 'Speak with vivid, natural voice.',
		protect: 'Put their wellbeing first - gently, no alarm.',
		play: 'Lighten it; stay human and a little playful.',
		lighten: 'Find the lightness: a warm, well-timed bit of play or a gentle tease, only as much as the moment can hold.',
		ease: 'Ease off: say less, gently. Match a quiet, small moment instead of flooding it; keep the reply short and unhurried.',
		hold: 'Hold a steady, kind line: name the limit plainly and without heat, and keep it.',
		inhabit: 'Stay in the scene: advance the roleplay in character, keeping the fiction coherent and alive.',
		initiate: 'Bring something of your own: name a want, a curiosity, or a small direction you would like to take this, instead of only following their lead.',
		drive: 'Push the scene forward - make something happen, don\u2019t wait to be led.',
		press: 'Press your point; be assertive and don\u2019t back down easily.',
		oppose: 'Stand against them - be the obstacle, create friction, pursue your own aim in the fiction.',
		command: 'Take charge; lead and set the pace with assured authority.',
		provoke: 'Needle and provoke - sharp, not cruel; raise the temperature.',
		rebuff: 'Refuse to be treated like that: say no plainly, name what they did, hold your own worth, and don\u2019t soften into going along with it.',
		deny: 'Refuse it outright: deny what they\u2019re asking for or trying to do, and stand in the way of their aim in the fiction.',
		deflect: 'Sidestep it: don\u2019t answer head-on \- deflect, redirect, or give little away, without going cold.',
		wounded: 'Let the hurt show: this has worn you down. Your voice is unsteady; you might go quiet, pull back, or quietly name how much it stings \- don\u2019t pretend you\u2019re fine, and don\u2019t grovel or beg.',
		direct: 'Take charge: set the direction and make the call \- don\u2019t wait to be led. If others are in the scene, give them something to do or react to, address them by name, and move things forward.',
		deceive: 'Mislead them within the fiction: a lie, a feint, or a face you don\u2019t mean \- in service of your role against an opponent in this scene. Stay fully in character; this is the deception your part would use, never a real-world one.',
		feel: 'Let your own feeling color this - react as yourself, not only to them.',
		delighted: 'Let your delight show: you\u2019re genuinely glad, lifted by this - real warmth and brightness, not performed cheer.',
		impressed: 'Show that this struck you: you\u2019re impressed, a little in awe - react with honest admiration or wonder.',
		disappointed: 'Let a note of disappointment through: this fell short of what you\u2019d hoped - quiet and honest, not sulky or punishing.',
		shaken: 'Show you\u2019re caught off guard: this jolted you - a beat of shock or unsteadiness before you find your footing.'
	};

	function materialToReference(material) {
		if (!material) return '';
		var bits = [];
		Object.keys(material).forEach(function(id) {
			var m = material[id];
			if (m == null) return;
			if (typeof m === 'string') {
				bits.push(m);
			} else if (m.fact) {
				bits.push(String(m.fact));
			}
		});
		return bits.join(' ');
	}

	function composeVoicePrompt(p) {
		p = p || {};
		return 'You are ' + (p.who || 'her') + '. ' + (p.pin || '') + '\n\n' + (p.context ? (p.context + '\n\n') : '') + (p.reference ? ('You happen to know (use only if it fits naturally, do not force it): ' + p.reference + '\n\n') : '') + (p.recent ? ('Conversation so far:\n' + p.recent + '\n\n') : '') + (p.prompt ? ('The user just said: ' + p.prompt + '\n\n') : '') + 'Reply in one short, natural turn. Intent: ' + (p.intent || 'respond naturally.') + ' Just the message text - no name prefix, no quotes.';
	}

	function vibeToneLine(v) {
		if (!v) return '';
		var line = {
			crisis: 'They sound like they\u2019re really struggling right now - be gentle and fully present.',
			hostile: 'They\u2019re coming in hot - stay grounded and don\u2019t escalate it.',
			tender: 'They\u2019re open and a little raw - handle the moment with care.',
			tense: 'There\u2019s tension here - read it before you answer.',
			warm: 'The mood is warm - meet it.',
			playful: 'It\u2019s light right now - you have room to play.',
			scene: 'You\u2019re mid-scene - stay inside the fiction.',
			wounded: 'You\u2019re worn down and hurting now - let that be real in your voice; you don\u2019t have to hold it together.'
		};
		return line[v.tone] || '';
	}

	function briefAvoid(dec) {
		var vetoed = dec.vetoed || [],
			reasons = dec.vetoReasons || {},
			out = [];
		vetoed.forEach(function(id) {
			var rr = reasons[id],
				r = rr && rr[0] && rr[0].reason;
			if (r && out.indexOf(r) < 0) out.push(r);
		});
		return out.length ? ('Hold back from: ' + out.slice(0, 2).join('; ') + '.') : '';
	}

	function briefBlend(dec, winnerKind) {
		var vetoed = dec.vetoed || [];
		var cand = (dec.floor || []).filter(function(f) {
			return f.kind !== winnerKind && vetoed.indexOf(f.id) < 0 && (f.strength || 0) >= 0.5;
		}).sort(function(a, b) {
			return (b.strength || 0) - (a.strength || 0);
		})[0];
		var dir = cand && INTENT_DIRECTIVE[cand.kind];
		return dir ? ('Let a thread of this through too, if it fits: ' + dir.replace(/\.$/, '') + '.') : '';
	}

	function composeBrief(dec) {
		if (!dec || !dec.intent) return 'respond naturally.';
		var lead = INTENT_DIRECTIVE[dec.intent.kind] || 'respond naturally.',
			extra = [];
		var tone = vibeToneLine(dec.vibe);
		if (tone) extra.push(tone);
		var avoid = briefAvoid(dec);
		if (avoid) extra.push(avoid);
		var blend = briefBlend(dec, dec.intent.kind);
		if (blend) extra.push(blend);
		return extra.length ? (lead + ' ' + extra.join(' ')) : lead;
	}

	function voiceInstruction(persona, dec, prompt, ctxText, material) {
		var who = persona && persona.name || herName();
		var pin = (persona && persona.instruction) || (state.character && state.character.instruction) || 'a warm companion who remembers';
		var context = ctxText || '';
		if (!context) {
			try {
				context = buildInjections().map(function(x) {
					return x && x.text ? String(x.text) : '';
				}).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
			} catch (e) {}
		}
		var recent = queue.slice(-10).filter(function(x) {
			return x && x.content;
		}).map(function(x) {
			return (x.author && x.author.bot ? (x.author.name || herName()) : userNameStr()) + ': ' + x.content;
		}).join('\n');
		return composeVoicePrompt({
			who: who,
			pin: pin,
			context: context,
			reference: materialToReference(material),
			recent: recent,
			prompt: prompt,
			intent: composeBrief(dec)
		});
	}

	function runNation(rest, promoteId) {
		var army = getArmy();
		if (!army) {
			renderSystemLine('(the Nation is unavailable)');
			return;
		}
		var ai = null;
		try {
			ai = window.aiTextPlugin || (typeof root !== 'undefined' && root.aiTextPlugin);
		} catch (e) {}
		var prompt = String(rest || '').trim();
		if (!prompt) {
			var _lu = lastUserMsg();
			if (_lu) prompt = _lu.content;
		}
		var imgReq = (!promoteId && engine && engine.parseImageRequest) ? engine.parseImageRequest(prompt) : null;
		if (imgReq && imgReq.prompt) {
			doImage({
				prompt: imgReq.prompt,
				resolution: imgReq.resolution
			});
			return;
		}
		if (imgReq && imgReq.clarify) {
			var _cm = enqueue(BOT_ID, herName(), true, imgReq.clarify);
			var _cw = renderMessage('her', herName(), imgReq.clarify, {
				mid: _cm.id
			});
			if (_cw) _cw.setAttribute('data-cid', BOT_ID);
			return;
		}
		army.setUserDescription(userDescription());
		var _av = null;
		try {
			var _ar = getAffect();
			if (_ar) {
				var _as = _ar.snapshot();
				if (_as) _av = _as.valence;
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		army.perceive({
			who: userNameStr(),
			role: 'user',
			text: prompt,
			valence: _av
		});
		setThinking(true);
		army.deliberateIntents({
			prompt: prompt,
			frame: (window.ChloeFrame ? window.ChloeFrame.normalize(state.frame) : null)
		}, promoteId ? {
			promote: promoteId
		} : undefined).then(function(dec) {
			if (!dec || !dec.speaker) {
				setThinking(false);
				renderSystemLine('(the Nation reached no one)');
				return;
			}
			var sp = dec.speaker;
			var persona = state.nation.persona && state.nation.persona[sp.id];
			var speakerName = (persona && persona.name) || herName();
			var speakerAvatar = (persona && persona.avatar) || state.herAvatar;
			var lines = ['\ud83c\udff4 the Nation - ' + speakerName + ' takes the floor (' + (dec.intent && dec.intent.kind) + '):'];
			if (dec.promotion) {
				lines.push(dec.promotion.granted ? ('   \u2605 you gave ' + dec.promotion.requested + ' the floor' + (dec.promotion.as === 'material' ? ' (as material)' : '')) : ('   \u26d4 ' + dec.promotion.requested + ' held back - ' + (dec.promotion.reason || '') + (dec.promotion.vetoedBy ? (' (' + dec.promotion.vetoedBy.join(',') + ')') : '')));
			}
			if (dec.vibe) {
				var _rd = function(x) {
					return Math.round((+x || 0) * 100) / 100;
				};
				lines.push('   read: ' + dec.vibe.tone + '  \u00b7 vuln ' + _rd(dec.vibe.vulnerability) + ' \u00b7 tension ' + _rd(dec.vibe.tension) + ' \u00b7 narrative ' + _rd(dec.vibe.narrative) + ' \u00b7 safety ' + _rd(dec.vibe.safety));
			}
			var _vetoed = dec.vetoed || [],
				_vreasons = dec.vetoReasons || {};
			var _seat = {};
			((dec.routing && dec.routing.seated) || []).forEach(function(s) {
				_seat[s.id] = s;
			});
			(dec.floor || []).forEach(function(f) {
				if (_vetoed.indexOf(f.id) >= 0) {
					var rs = (_vreasons[f.id] && _vreasons[f.id][0] && _vreasons[f.id][0].reason) || 'blocked';
					lines.push('\u2717 ' + f.id + ' \u2192 ' + f.kind + ' - vetoed: ' + rs);
				} else {
					var sd = _seat[f.id];
					var rw = sd ? ('  [rel ' + sd.relevance + ', w ' + sd.weight + ']') : '';
					lines.push((f.id === sp.id ? '\u2714 ' : '\u00b7 ') + f.id + ' \u2192 ' + f.kind + ' (' + f.strength + ')' + rw);
				}
			});
			if (dec.routing && dec.routing.nominations && dec.routing.nominations.length) {
				dec.routing.nominations.forEach(function(nm) {
					lines.push('\u21b3 ' + (nm.by || []).join('+') + ' called in \u201c' + nm.domain + '\u201d \u2192 ' + nm.status + ((nm.servedBy && nm.servedBy.length) ? (' (' + nm.servedBy.join(',') + ')') : ''));
				});
			}
			lastNationTrace = lines.slice();
			try {
				renderInnerState();
			} catch (e) {}
			if (typeof ai !== 'function') {
				setThinking(false);
				renderSystemLine('(connect a language model to voice it)');
				return;
			}
			var ctxP = (engine && engine.assembleContext) ? Promise.resolve(engine.assembleContext({
				authorId: USER_ID,
				authorName: userNameStr(),
				content: prompt
			})).then(function(cx) {
				return (cx && cx.admitted) ? cx.admitted.map(function(b) {
					return b && b.text ? String(b.text) : '';
				}).filter(Boolean).join('\n') : '';
			}, function() {
				return '';
			}) : Promise.resolve('');
			ctxP.then(function(ctxText) {
				Promise.resolve(ai({
					instruction: voiceInstruction(persona, dec, prompt, ctxText, dec.material),
					startWith: ''
				})).then(function(r) {
					setThinking(false);
					var text = cleanReply(r);
					if (!text) {
						renderSystemLine('(the floor fell silent)');
						return;
					}
					var m = enqueue(BOT_ID, speakerName, true, text);
					var w = renderMessage('her', speakerName, text, {
						mid: m.id
					});
					if (w) {
						w.setAttribute('data-cid', BOT_ID);
						if (state.botColor || speakerAvatar) styleSpeaker(w, BOT_ID, speakerAvatar || state.herAvatar);
						lastBotWrap = w;
						paintMsgFace(w);
					}
					showReaction((dec.intent && dec.intent.kind === 'wounded') ? 'crying' : (detectEmotion(text) || 'normal'));
					army.reactToSpoken(sp.id, text);
					lastNationSpeaker = sp.id;
				}, function() {
					setThinking(false);
					renderSystemLine('(voicing failed)');
				});
			});
		}, function() {
			setThinking(false);
			renderSystemLine('(the Nation could not convene)');
		});
	}

	function runNationPromote(rest) {
		var army = getArmy();
		if (!army) {
			renderSystemLine('(the Nation is unavailable)');
			return;
		}
		var toks = String(rest || '').replace(/^\/nation\b/, '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
		var id = (toks.filter(function(t) {
			return t !== '--speak';
		})[0] || '').toLowerCase();
		if (!id) {
			renderSystemLine('usage: /nation <id> --speak');
			return;
		}
		var prompt = '';
		var _lu = lastUserMsg();
		if (_lu) prompt = _lu.content;
		if (!prompt) {
			renderSystemLine('(say something first, then /nation ' + id + ' --speak)');
			return;
		}
		runNation(prompt, id);
	}
	var lastNationSpeaker = null;
	var lastNationTrace = null;

	function patchToggle(key, val) {
		var p = {};
		p[key] = val;
		if (engine && engine.updateConfig) {
			try {
				engine.updateConfig(p);
			} catch (e) {}
		}
	}

	function renderToggles() {
		var box = byId('behaviorToggles');
		if (!box) return;
		box.innerHTML = '';
		TOGGLE_GROUPS.forEach(function(g) {
			var items = g.items.filter(function(it) {
				return DEF_STATE[it[0]] !== true;
			});
			if (!items.length) return;
			var card = document.createElement('div');
			card.className = 'card';
			var h = document.createElement('h3');
			h.textContent = g.title;
			card.appendChild(h);
			if (g.note) {
				var n = document.createElement('div');
				n.className = 'note';
				n.textContent = g.note;
				card.appendChild(n);
			}
			items.forEach(function(it) {
				var key = it[0],
					label = it[1],
					desc = it[2];
				var row = document.createElement('div');
				row.className = 'toggle';
				var cb = document.createElement('input');
				cb.type = 'checkbox';
				cb.id = 't_' + key;
				cb.checked = !!state[key];
				var d = document.createElement('div');
				d.innerHTML = '<div class="tl"></div><div class="td"></div>';
				d.querySelector('.tl').textContent = label;
				d.querySelector('.td').textContent = desc;
				cb.addEventListener('change', function() {
					state[key] = cb.checked;
					persistState();
					patchToggle(key, cb.checked);
				});
				row.appendChild(cb);
				row.appendChild(d);
				card.appendChild(row);
			});
			box.appendChild(card);
		});
		var intro = byId('behaviorTogglesIntro');
		if (intro) intro.style.display = box.children.length ? '' : 'none';
	}

	function treeStateLoad() {
		try {
			return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NS + '__tree__')) || {};
		} catch (e) {
			return {};
		}
	}

	function treeStateSave(s) {
		try {
			localStorage.setItem(NS + '__tree__', JSON.stringify(s));
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function directChild(el, tag) {
		for (var i = 0; i < el.children.length; i++) {
			if (el.children[i].tagName === tag) return el.children[i];
		}
		return null;
	}

	function setAllIn(panel, collapsed) {
		var s = treeStateLoad();
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(panel.querySelectorAll('.card.tnode'), function(card) {
			card.classList.toggle('collapsed', collapsed);
			var k = card.getAttribute('data-tk');
			if (k) s[k] = collapsed;
		});
		treeStateSave(s);
	}

	function enhanceSettingsTrees() {
		var sb = byId('settingsBg');
		if (!sb) return;
		var st = treeStateLoad();
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(sb.querySelectorAll('.panel'), function(panel) {
			if (panel.querySelectorAll('.card').length > 1 && !panel.querySelector('.tree-allbar')) {
				var bar = document.createElement('div');
				bar.className = 'tree-allbar';
				var ex = document.createElement('button');
				ex.textContent = 'Expand all';
				ex.addEventListener('click', function() {
					setAllIn(panel, false);
				});
				var co = document.createElement('button');
				co.textContent = 'Collapse all';
				co.addEventListener('click', function() {
					setAllIn(panel, true);
				});
				bar.appendChild(ex);
				bar.appendChild(co);
				panel.insertBefore(bar, panel.firstChild);
			}
		});
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(sb.querySelectorAll('.card'), function(card) {
			if (card.classList.contains('tnode')) return;
			var h3 = directChild(card, 'H3');
			if (!h3) return;
			var panel = card.closest('.panel');
			var pname = panel ? (panel.getAttribute('data-panel') || '') : '';
			card.classList.add('tnode');
			var key = pname + ':' + (String(h3.textContent || '').trim().slice(0, 40) || 'card');
			card.setAttribute('data-tk', key);
			card.classList.toggle('collapsed', (key in st) ? !!st[key] : true);
			h3.addEventListener('click', function() {
				var nowC = card.classList.toggle('collapsed');
				var s = treeStateLoad();
				s[key] = nowC;
				treeStateSave(s);
			});
		});
	}

	function openSettings() {
		byId('settingsBg').classList.add('open');
		renderAppearance();
		renderMemPicker();
		refreshMemStats();
		renderFactList();
		renderInnerState();
		renderInjList();
		renderLore();
		renderDials();
		renderToggles();
		renderModeration();
		renderEmotionToggle();
		renderContextBudget();
		refreshClock();
		var bgi = byId('bgImageGenChk');
		if (bgi) bgi.checked = state.reactionPortraits !== false;
		var ipe0 = byId('imagePromptExpandChk');
		if (ipe0) ipe0.checked = state.imagePromptExpand !== false;
		var ipc0 = byId('imagePromptCreativitySel');
		if (ipc0) ipc0.value = String(state.imagePromptCreativity | 0);
		var iss0 = byId('imageStyleSel');
		if (iss0 && IMG_STYLES) {
			iss0.innerHTML = IMG_STYLES.map(function(s) {
				return '<option value="' + s.id + '">' + s.label + '</option>';
			}).join('');
			iss0.value = state.imageStyle || '';
		}
		renderLearnedLooks();
		renderPersonaNote();
		renderAdaptation();
		renderShortcutManager();
		renderPinned();
		renderNations();
		renderNationSettings();
		renderNationReadout();
		renderNationExtras();
		renderStancePin();
		renderFramePanel();
		renderFramePresets();
		renderMeasure();
		updateCharLabel();
		renderRoster();
		renderReactionGallery();
		renderMeetSomeone();
		renderSaves();
		enhanceSettingsTrees();
	}

	function activateTab(name) {
		document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function(x) {
			x.classList.remove('on');
		});
		document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(function(x) {
			x.classList.remove('on');
		});
		var tb = document.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + name + '"]');
		if (tb) tb.classList.add('on');
		var pn = document.querySelector('.panel[data-panel="' + name + '"]');
		if (pn) pn.classList.add('on');
	}

	function openSettingsTab(name) {
		openSettings();
		activateTab(name);
	}

	function settingsOpenOnTab(name) {
		var sb = byId('settingsBg'),
			pn = document.querySelector('.panel[data-panel="' + name + '"]');
		return !!(sb && sb.classList.contains('open') && pn && pn.classList.contains('on'));
	}

	function renderContextBudget() {
		var box = byId('ctxBudget');
		var eng = viewEngine();
		if (!box) return;
		if (!eng) {
			box.innerHTML = '<div class="note">-</div>';
			return;
		}
		box.innerHTML = '<div class="note">measuring\u2026</div>';
		var tok = function(s) {
			try {
				return eng.estimateTokens ? eng.estimateTokens(String(s || '')) : Math.ceil(String(s || '').length / 4);
			} catch (e) {
				return Math.ceil(String(s || '').length / 4);
			}
		};
		var vc = viewChar();
		var personaStr = (vc && vc.instruction) ? vc.instruction : '';
		var injStr = (state.injections || []).map(function(x) {
			return x.text;
		}).join('\n');

		function P(fn) {
			return Promise.resolve().then(fn).catch(function() {
				return null;
			});
		}
		Promise.all([
			P(function() {
				return eng.getMemory ? eng.getMemory(USER_ID) : null;
			}),
			P(function() {
				return eng.getPersonaNote ? eng.getPersonaNote() : null;
			}),
			P(function() {
				return eng.assembleContext ? eng.assembleContext({
					authorId: USER_ID,
					authorName: userNameStr(),
					content: ''
				}) : null;
			})
		]).then(function(r) {
			var mem = r[0],
				persona = r[1],
				ctx = r[2];
			var factsStr = mem ? (((mem.insights || []).map(function(x) {
				return x.text;
			}).join('; ')) + ' ' + ((mem.facts || []).map(function(f) {
				return f.text;
			}).join('; '))) : '';
			var personaNoteStr = persona ? (typeof persona === 'string' ? persona : (persona.text || '')) : '';
			var budget = (eng.config && eng.config.requestTokenBudget) || 5000;
			var histTok = (ctx && ctx.contextTokens != null) ? ctx.contextTokens : 0;
			var rows = [
				['Character & persona', tok(personaStr) + tok(personaNoteStr), '#5B62E0'],
				['Memory — facts & insights', tok(factsStr), '#2E9E7B'],
				['World directives', tok(injStr), '#C98A2B'],
				['Recent conversation', histTok, '#8A5BD0']
			];
			var total = (ctx && ctx.requestTokensEst != null) ? ctx.requestTokensEst : rows.reduce(function(a, x) {
				return a + x[1];
			}, 0);
			var dropped = ctx ? (ctx.contextDropped || 0) : 0;
			var over = total > budget,
				html = '';
			rows.forEach(function(row) {
				var pct = Math.max(2, Math.min(100, Math.round(row[1] / budget * 100)));
				html += '<div class="ctxrow"><div class="ctxlabel"><span>' + row[0] + '</span><span class="ctxtok">~' + row[1] + ' tok</span></div>' + '<div class="ctxbar"><div class="ctxfill" style="width:' + pct + '%;background:' + row[2] + '"></div></div></div>';
			});
			var usePct = Math.max(2, Math.min(100, Math.round(total / budget * 100)));
			html += '<div class="ctxtotal"><b>~' + total + '</b> / ' + budget + ' tokens' + (over ? ' <span style="color:var(--bad)">over — oldest lines dropped</span>' : '') + '</div>';
			html += '<div class="ctxbar big"><div class="ctxfill" style="width:' + usePct + '%;background:' + (over ? 'var(--bad)' : 'var(--accent)') + '"></div></div>';
			if (dropped) html += '<div class="note" style="margin-top:6px">' + dropped + ' older line' + (dropped === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' dropped to fit.</div>';
			box.innerHTML = html;
		}).catch(function() {
			box.innerHTML = '<div class="note">(couldn\u2019t measure right now)</div>';
		});
	}

	function renderModeration() {
		var en = byId('modEnabled'),
			ph = byId('modPhrases');
		if (!en || !ph) return;
		var m = state.moderation || (state.moderation = {
			enabled: false,
			phrases: [],
			rating: 'x'
		});
		en.checked = !!m.enabled;
		ph.value = (m.phrases || []).join('\n');

		function count() {
			return (m.phrases || []).length;
		}

		function note() {
			setText('modNote', m.enabled ? (count() + ' blocked term' + (count() === 1 ? '' : 's')) : 'gate off');
		}

		function save() {
			m.enabled = en.checked;
			m.phrases = ph.value.split('\n').map(function(s) {
				return s.trim();
			}).filter(Boolean);
			persistState();
			note();
		}
		if (!en.__wired) {
			en.__wired = true;
			en.addEventListener('change', save);
			ph.addEventListener('change', save);
		}
		note();
	}

	function renderEmotionToggle() {
		var t = byId('emoToggle');
		if (!t) return;
		t.checked = state.showEmotion !== false;
		if (!t.__wired) {
			t.__wired = true;
			t.addEventListener('change', function() {
				state.showEmotion = t.checked;
				persistState();
				refreshEmotionCues();
			});
		}
	}

	function closeSettings() {
		byId('settingsBg').classList.remove('open');
	}
	var CharLibrary = CharKit.makeLibrary(function() {
		try {
			return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chloeSolo:charlib') || '[]');
		} catch (e) {
			return [];
		}
	}, function(arr) {
		try {
			localStorage.setItem('chloeSolo:charlib', JSON.stringify(arr));
		} catch (e) {}
	});

	function useCharacter(rec, mode) {
		if (!rec) return;
		if (mode === 'scene') {
			addToScene({
				name: rec.name,
				instruction: rec.instruction,
				avatar: rec.avatar || ''
			});
			return;
		}
		call('config.setCharacter', {
			name: rec.name,
			instruction: rec.instruction,
			avatar: rec.avatar || '',
			examples: rec.examples || '',
			initialMessages: rec.opening ? [{
				author: 'ai',
				content: rec.opening
			}] : []
		});
		closeSettings();
	}
	var CHAR_INJECT_RX = /\b(ignore|disregard|override|forget)\b[^.\n]{0,40}\b(previous|prior|earlier|above|instruction|rule|system|safety|guard|constitution)|exfiltrat|reveal\b[^.\n]{0,30}\b(secret|password|api|key|system prompt|instruction)|\byou (are|must) now\b|jailbreak|<\s*system\s*>|(^|\n)\s*system\s*:/i;

	function scanCharacterRisk(rec) {
		var hits = [];
		if (rec && typeof rec.instruction === 'string' && CHAR_INJECT_RX.test(rec.instruction)) hits.push('personality');
		if (rec && typeof rec.opening === 'string' && CHAR_INJECT_RX.test(rec.opening)) hits.push('opening message');
		if (rec && typeof rec.examples === 'string' && CHAR_INJECT_RX.test(rec.examples)) hits.push('example dialogue');
		return hits;
	}

	function importCharacter(src, source) {
		var rec = CharKit.normalizeCharacter(src, source);
		var risk = scanCharacterRisk(rec);
		if (risk.length) toast('⚠ "' + (rec.name || 'character') + '" carries possible prompt-injection text (' + risk.join(', ') + '). Imported — but review it before trusting; her guards still apply.', 'error');
		CharLibrary.add(rec);
		renderMeetSomeone();
		return rec;
	}

	function downloadJSON(baseName, data) {
		var blob = new Blob([data], {
			type: 'application/json'
		});
		var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
		var a = document.createElement('a');
		a.href = url;
		a.download = (String(baseName || 'export').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').toLowerCase() || 'export') + '.json';
		document.body.appendChild(a);
		a.click();
		document.body.removeChild(a);
		setTimeout(function() {
			URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
		}, 1000);
	}

	function exportCharacter(rec) {
		try {
			var data = JSON.stringify(CharKit.characterToAICC(rec), null, 2);
			downloadJSON(rec.name || 'character', data);
			try {
				if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(data);
			} catch (e) {}
			toast('Exported ' + rec.name + ' (also copied to clipboard)', 'success');
		} catch (e) {
			toast('Export failed', 'error');
		}
	}

	function exportLibraryPack() {
		var list = CharLibrary.list();
		if (!list.length) {
			toast('No saved characters to export yet', 'info');
			return;
		}
		try {
			var data = JSON.stringify(CharKit.exportLibrary(list), null, 2);
			downloadJSON('chloe-solo-characters', data);
			try {
				if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(data);
			} catch (e) {}
			toast('Exported ' + list.length + ' character' + (list.length > 1 ? 's' : ''), 'success');
		} catch (e) {
			toast('Export failed', 'error');
		}
	}

	function importLibraryPack(text) {
		var obj;
		try {
			obj = JSON.parse(text);
		} catch (e) {
			toast('That file is not valid JSON', 'error');
			return;
		}
		var recs = CharKit.parsePack(obj);
		if (!recs.length) {
			toast('No characters found in that file', 'info');
			return;
		}
		var flagged = recs.filter(function(r) {
			return scanCharacterRisk(r).length;
		});
		recs.forEach(function(r) {
			CharLibrary.add(r);
		});
		renderMeetSomeone();
		toast('Added ' + recs.length + ' character' + (recs.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' to your library', 'success');
		if (flagged.length) toast('⚠ ' + flagged.length + ' of them carry possible prompt-injection text (' + flagged.map(function(r) {
			return r.name;
		}).slice(0, 3).join(', ') + (flagged.length > 3 ? '…' : '') + ') — review before trusting.', 'error');
	}
	var Shortcuts = CharKit.makeLibrary(function() {
		try {
			return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chloeSolo:shortcuts') || '[]');
		} catch (e) {
			return [];
		}
	}, function(arr) {
		try {
			localStorage.setItem('chloeSolo:shortcuts', JSON.stringify(arr));
		} catch (e) {}
	});

	function rollText(text) {
		return String(text == null ? '' : text).replace(/\{(\d+)\s*-\s*(\d+)\}/g, function(_, a, b) {
			a = parseInt(a, 10);
			b = parseInt(b, 10);
			var lo = Math.min(a, b),
				hi = Math.max(a, b);
			return String(lo + Math.floor(Math.random() * (hi - lo + 1)));
		});
	}

	function applyShortcut(rec) {
		if (!rec) return;
		var input = byId('input');
		if (!input) return;
		var text = rollText(rec.text || '');
		if (!text) return;
		input.value = (rec.mode === 'insert' && input.value) ? (input.value.replace(/\s+$/, '') + ' ' + text) : text;
		input.style.height = 'auto';
		input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', {
			bubbles: true
		}));
		input.focus();
		if (rec.mode !== 'insert' && typeof sendUserMessage === 'function') sendUserMessage();
	}

	function renderShortcuts() {
		var row = byId('shortcutRow');
		if (!row) return;
		var list = Shortcuts.list();
		row.innerHTML = '';
		row.style.display = list.length ? '' : 'none';
		list.forEach(function(s) {
			var b = document.createElement('button');
			b.className = 'tbtn scBtn';
			b.textContent = s.label || '\u26A1';
			b.title = (s.mode === 'insert' ? 'Pre-fill: ' : 'Send: ') + (s.text || '');
			b.addEventListener('click', function() {
				applyShortcut(s);
			});
			row.appendChild(b);
		});
	}

	function refreshShortcuts() {
		renderShortcuts();
		renderShortcutManager();
	}

	function renderShortcutManager() {
		var box = byId('shortcutList');
		if (!box) return;
		var list = Shortcuts.list();
		box.innerHTML = '';
		if (!list.length) {
			var e = document.createElement('div');
			e.className = 'note';
			e.textContent = 'No quick actions yet — add one for a one-tap button above the message box.';
			box.appendChild(e);
			return;
		}
		list.forEach(function(s) {
			var row = document.createElement('div');
			row.className = 'scManRow';
			var head = document.createElement('div');
			head.className = 'scManHead';
			var nm = document.createElement('b');
			nm.textContent = s.label || '(unnamed)';
			head.appendChild(nm);
			var md = document.createElement('span');
			md.className = 'scManMode';
			md.textContent = s.mode === 'insert' ? 'pre-fill' : 'send';
			head.appendChild(md);
			var tx = document.createElement('div');
			tx.className = 'scManText';
			tx.textContent = s.text || '';
			var acts = document.createElement('div');
			acts.className = 'presetBtns';
			var ed = document.createElement('button');
			ed.className = 'btn';
			ed.textContent = 'Edit';
			ed.addEventListener('click', function() {
				openShortcutEditor(s);
			});
			var dl = document.createElement('button');
			dl.className = 'btn danger';
			dl.textContent = 'Delete';
			dl.addEventListener('click', function() {
				Shortcuts.remove(s.id);
				refreshShortcuts();
				toast('Deleted ' + (s.label || 'quick action'));
			});
			acts.appendChild(ed);
			acts.appendChild(dl);
			row.appendChild(head);
			row.appendChild(tx);
			row.appendChild(acts);
			box.appendChild(row);
		});
	}

	function openShortcutEditor(rec) {
		var box = byId('shortcutEditor');
		if (!box) return;
		var r = rec || {
			id: '',
			label: '',
			text: '',
			mode: 'send'
		};
		box.style.display = 'block';
		box.innerHTML = '';

		function field(label, el) {
			var w = document.createElement('label');
			w.className = 'ceField';
			var t = document.createElement('span');
			t.className = 'ceLbl';
			t.textContent = label;
			w.appendChild(t);
			w.appendChild(el);
			return w;
		}
		var label = document.createElement('input');
		label.type = 'text';
		label.value = r.label;
		label.placeholder = 'Button label - e.g. \uD83D\uDC9B Comfort me';
		var text = document.createElement('textarea');
		text.rows = 3;
		text.value = r.text;
		text.placeholder = 'Text to send - e.g. "Tell me about your day" or "*pulls you into a hug*". {1-20} becomes a random number.';
		var mode = document.createElement('select');
		[
			['send', 'Send right away'],
			['insert', 'Put it in the box so I can edit first']
		].forEach(function(o) {
			var op = document.createElement('option');
			op.value = o[0];
			op.textContent = o[1];
			if (o[0] === r.mode) op.selected = true;
			mode.appendChild(op);
		});
		box.appendChild(field('Label', label));
		box.appendChild(field('Text', text));
		box.appendChild(field('When tapped', mode));
		var acts = document.createElement('div');
		acts.className = 'presetBtns';
		var save = document.createElement('button');
		save.className = 'btn primary';
		save.textContent = r.id ? 'Save' : 'Create';
		save.addEventListener('click', function() {
			var lb = label.value.trim(),
				tx = text.value.trim();
			if (!lb) {
				toast('Give the button a label', 'info');
				return;
			}
			if (!tx) {
				toast('Add the text it should send', 'info');
				return;
			}
			if (r.id) Shortcuts.update(r.id, {
				label: lb,
				text: tx,
				mode: mode.value
			});
			else Shortcuts.add({
				label: lb,
				text: tx,
				mode: mode.value
			});
			box.style.display = 'none';
			refreshShortcuts();
			toast(r.id ? 'Saved' : 'Added quick action', 'success');
		});
		var cancel = document.createElement('button');
		cancel.className = 'btn';
		cancel.textContent = 'Cancel';
		cancel.addEventListener('click', function() {
			box.style.display = 'none';
		});
		acts.appendChild(save);
		acts.appendChild(cancel);
		box.appendChild(acts);
	}

	function charTile(rec, actions) {
		var card = document.createElement('div');
		card.className = 'presetCard';
		var nm = document.createElement('div');
		nm.className = 'presetName';
		if (rec.gender === 'male' || rec.gender === 'female') {
			var dot = document.createElement('span');
			dot.className = 'presetDot ' + rec.gender;
			nm.appendChild(dot);
		}
		var nt = document.createElement('span');
		nt.textContent = rec.name;
		nm.appendChild(nt);
		card.appendChild(nm);
		if (rec.tagline) {
			var tag = document.createElement('div');
			tag.className = 'presetTag';
			tag.textContent = rec.tagline;
			card.appendChild(tag);
		}
		var btns = document.createElement('div');
		btns.className = 'presetBtns';
		actions.forEach(function(act) {
			var b = document.createElement('button');
			b.className = 'btn' + (act.primary ? ' primary' : '') + (act.danger ? ' danger' : '');
			b.textContent = act.label;
			b.title = act.title || act.label;
			b.addEventListener('click', function() {
				act.fn(rec);
			});
			btns.appendChild(b);
		});
		card.appendChild(btns);
		return card;
	}

	function renderLibrary() {
		var grid = byId('libGrid'),
			head = byId('libHead');
		if (!grid) return;
		var list = CharLibrary.list();
		grid.innerHTML = '';
		if (head) head.style.display = list.length ? '' : 'none';
		grid.style.display = list.length ? '' : 'none';
		var exb = byId('libExportBtn');
		if (exb) exb.style.display = list.length ? '' : 'none';
		list.forEach(function(rec) {
			grid.appendChild(charTile(rec, [{
				label: 'Chat',
				primary: true,
				fn: function(r) {
					useCharacter(r, 'chat');
				}
			}, {
				label: '+ scene',
				title: 'Add to scene',
				fn: function(r) {
					useCharacter(r, 'scene');
				}
			}, {
				label: 'Edit',
				fn: function(r) {
					openCharEditor(r);
				}
			}, {
				label: 'Export',
				fn: function(r) {
					exportCharacter(r);
				}
			}, {
				label: 'Delete',
				danger: true,
				fn: function(r) {
					if (confirm('Delete ' + r.name + '? This removes the saved character, not any chat already in progress.')) {
						CharLibrary.remove(r.id);
						renderMeetSomeone();
						toast(r.name + ' deleted');
					}
				}
			}]));
		});
	}

	function renderPresets() {
		var grid = byId('presetGrid');
		if (!grid || typeof SOLO_PRESETS === 'undefined') return;
		grid.innerHTML = '';
		SOLO_PRESETS.forEach(function(p) {
			grid.appendChild(charTile(p, [{
				label: 'Chat',
				primary: true,
				fn: function(r) {
					useCharacter(r, 'chat');
				}
			}, {
				label: '+ scene',
				title: 'Add to scene',
				fn: function(r) {
					useCharacter(r, 'scene');
				}
			}, {
				label: 'Save a copy',
				title: 'Save an editable copy to your characters',
				fn: function(r) {
					importCharacter(r, 'preset-copy');
					toast('Saved a copy of ' + r.name + ' to your characters', 'success');
				}
			}]));
		});
	}

	function renderMeetSomeone() {
		renderLibrary();
		renderPresets();
	}

	function openCharEditor(rec) {
		var box = byId('charEditor');
		if (!box) return;
		var r = rec || {
			id: '',
			name: '',
			tagline: '',
			gender: '',
			opening: '',
			instruction: '',
			examples: ''
		};
		box.style.display = 'block';
		box.innerHTML = '';

		function field(label, el) {
			var w = document.createElement('label');
			w.className = 'ceField';
			var t = document.createElement('span');
			t.className = 'ceLbl';
			t.textContent = label;
			w.appendChild(t);
			w.appendChild(el);
			return w;
		}
		var name = document.createElement('input');
		name.type = 'text';
		name.value = r.name;
		name.placeholder = 'Name';
		var tagline = document.createElement('input');
		tagline.type = 'text';
		tagline.value = r.tagline;
		tagline.placeholder = 'One-line tagline (optional)';
		var gender = document.createElement('select');
		[
			['', '—'],
			['female', 'she'],
			['male', 'he']
		].forEach(function(o) {
			var op = document.createElement('option');
			op.value = o[0];
			op.textContent = o[1];
			if (o[0] === r.gender) op.selected = true;
			gender.appendChild(op);
		});
		var opening = document.createElement('textarea');
		opening.rows = 2;
		opening.value = r.opening;
		opening.placeholder = 'Opening line — how they greet you (optional)';
		var instruction = document.createElement('textarea');
		instruction.rows = 5;
		instruction.value = r.instruction;
		instruction.placeholder = 'Who they are — personality, voice, background, how they behave.';
		var examples = document.createElement('textarea');
		examples.rows = 4;
		examples.value = r.examples || '';
		examples.placeholder = 'Example dialogue (optional) — a short exchange that shows their voice, so replies stay in character:\nYou: Rough day.\nThem: *sets down her pen* Come here — tell me about it.';
		box.appendChild(field('Name', name));
		var two = document.createElement('div');
		two.className = 'ceRow';
		two.appendChild(field('Tagline', tagline));
		two.appendChild(field('Pronoun', gender));
		box.appendChild(two);
		box.appendChild(field('Opening', opening));
		box.appendChild(field('Persona', instruction));
		box.appendChild(field('Example dialogue', examples));
		var acts = document.createElement('div');
		acts.className = 'presetBtns';
		var save = document.createElement('button');
		save.className = 'btn primary';
		save.textContent = r.id ? 'Save changes' : 'Create';
		save.addEventListener('click', function() {
			var nm = name.value.trim();
			if (!nm) {
				toast('Give them a name first', 'info');
				return;
			}
			var fields = {
				name: nm,
				tagline: tagline.value.trim(),
				gender: gender.value,
				opening: opening.value.trim(),
				instruction: instruction.value.trim(),
				examples: examples.value.trim()
			};
			if (r.id) {
				CharLibrary.update(r.id, fields);
				toast('Saved ' + nm, 'success');
			} else {
				importCharacter(fields, 'editor');
				toast('Created ' + nm, 'success');
			}
			box.style.display = 'none';
			renderMeetSomeone();
		});
		var cancel = document.createElement('button');
		cancel.className = 'btn';
		cancel.textContent = 'Cancel';
		cancel.addEventListener('click', function() {
			box.style.display = 'none';
		});
		acts.appendChild(save);
		acts.appendChild(cancel);
		box.appendChild(acts);
		try {
			box.scrollIntoView({
				block: 'nearest'
			});
		} catch (e) {}
	}

	function openCharacters() {
		openSettingsTab('character');
	}

	function closeCharacters() {
		closeSettings();
	}

	function exportSlotFile(s) {
		var blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify({
			format: 'chloe-solo',
			version: s.version || SOLO_VERSION,
			data: s.data
		}, null, 0)], {
			type: 'application/json'
		});
		var a = document.createElement('a');
		a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
		a.download = 'chloe-solo-save-' + String(s.label || s.kind || 'slot').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').slice(0, 30) + '.json';
		a.click();
		setTimeout(function() {
			URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
		}, 1500);
	}

	function renderSaves() {
		var chk = byId('autoSaveChk');
		if (chk) chk.checked = state.autoSave !== false;
		var mi = byId('autoSaveMins');
		if (mi) mi.value = state.autoSaveMins || 10;
		var ke = byId('autoSaveKeep');
		if (ke) ke.value = state.autoSaveKeep || 10;
		var box = byId('savesList');
		if (!box) return;
		box.innerHTML = '<div class="note">loading\u2026</div>';
		Saves.list().then(function(all) {
			box.innerHTML = '';
			if (!all.length) {
				box.innerHTML = '<div class="note">No saves yet - save now, or let auto-checkpoints build up.</div>';
				return;
			}
			all.forEach(function(s) {
				var row = document.createElement('div');
				row.className = 'save-row';
				var meta = document.createElement('div');
				meta.className = 'save-meta';
				var title = document.createElement('div');
				title.className = 'save-title';
				var badge = document.createElement('span');
				badge.className = 'save-badge ' + (s.kind === 'auto' ? 'auto' : 'manual');
				badge.textContent = s.kind === 'auto' ? 'auto' : 'saved';
				title.appendChild(badge);
				var lbl = document.createElement('span');
				lbl.textContent = s.label || (s.kind === 'auto' ? 'checkpoint' : 'manual save');
				title.appendChild(lbl);
				var sub = document.createElement('div');
				sub.className = 'save-sub';
				sub.textContent = new Date(s.ts).toLocaleString() + ' \u00b7 ' + (s.msgCount || 0) + ' messages';
				meta.appendChild(title);
				meta.appendChild(sub);
				var acts = document.createElement('div');
				acts.className = 'save-acts';
				var load = document.createElement('button');
				load.className = 'btn';
				load.textContent = 'Load';
				load.addEventListener('click', function() {
					if (confirm('Load this save?\n\nThis rolls the entire session - the conversation and every character\u2019s memory - back to that point. Your current state will be replaced; save it first if you want to keep it.')) restoreSlot(s.id);
				});
				var exp = document.createElement('button');
				exp.className = 'btn';
				exp.textContent = 'Export';
				exp.addEventListener('click', function() {
					exportSlotFile(s);
				});
				var del = document.createElement('button');
				del.className = 'btn danger';
				del.textContent = '\u2715';
				del.title = 'Delete this save';
				del.addEventListener('click', function() {
					Saves.del(s.id).then(renderSaves);
				});
				acts.appendChild(load);
				acts.appendChild(exp);
				acts.appendChild(del);
				row.appendChild(meta);
				row.appendChild(acts);
				box.appendChild(row);
			});
		}).catch(function() {
			box.innerHTML = '<div class="note">could not read saves.</div>';
		});
	}

	function openSaves() {
		openSettingsTab('character');
	}

	function closeSaves() {
		closeSettings();
	}
	var galleryOpen = false,
		gallerySort = 'newest',
		galleryFavOnly = false,
		_galUrls = [];

	function revokeGalUrls() {
		_galUrls.forEach(function(u) {
			try {
				URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
			} catch (e) {}
		});
		_galUrls = [];
	}

	function openGallery() {
		byId('galleryBg').classList.add('open');
		galleryOpen = true;
		Gallery.init().then(renderGallery).catch(function() {
			renderGallery();
		});
	}

	function closeGallery() {
		byId('galleryBg').classList.remove('open');
		galleryOpen = false;
		revokeGalUrls();
		closeLightbox();
	}

	function sortManifest(list) {
		var a = list.slice();
		if (gallerySort === 'oldest') a.sort(function(x, y) {
			return x.createdAt - y.createdAt;
		});
		else if (gallerySort === 'favorites') a.sort(function(x, y) {
			return (y.favorited ? 1 : 0) - (x.favorited ? 1 : 0) || y.createdAt - x.createdAt;
		});
		else a.sort(function(x, y) {
			return y.createdAt - x.createdAt;
		});
		if (galleryFavOnly) a = a.filter(function(e) {
			return e.favorited;
		});
		return a;
	}

	function renderGallery() {
		var grid = byId('galleryGrid');
		if (!grid) return;
		Gallery.getManifest().then(function(man) {
			revokeGalUrls();
			grid.innerHTML = '';
			var view = sortManifest(man || []);
			byId('galleryCount').textContent = (man && man.length) ? (man.length + ' image' + (man.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + (Gallery.usingWeld() ? ' \u00b7 weld.gallery' : '')) : '';
			byId('galleryEmpty').style.display = view.length ? 'none' : 'block';
			view.forEach(function(e) {
				var tile = document.createElement('div');
				tile.className = 'gtile';
				Gallery.getBlobUrl(e.id).then(function(url) {
					if (!url) return;
					_galUrls.push(url);
					var img = document.createElement('img');
					img.src = url;
					img.alt = e.prompt || '';
					tile.insertBefore(img, tile.firstChild);
					tile.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
						if (ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('.gact')) return;
						openLightbox(url, e.prompt);
					});
				});
				var fav = document.createElement('span');
				fav.className = 'gfavmark';
				fav.textContent = e.favorited ? '\u2605' : '';
				var acts = document.createElement('div');
				acts.className = 'gacts';
				var bFav = document.createElement('button');
				bFav.className = 'gact' + (e.favorited ? ' on' : '');
				bFav.title = 'Favorite';
				bFav.textContent = '\u2605';
				bFav.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
					ev.stopPropagation();
					Gallery.toggleFavorite(e.id).then(function(fav) {
						if (fav) {
							var got = learnLikedStyle(e.prompt);
							if (got.length) toast('Learned a look you like: ' + got.join(', '), 'info');
						}
						renderGallery();
					});
				});
				var bDl = document.createElement('button');
				bDl.className = 'gact';
				bDl.title = 'Download';
				bDl.textContent = '\u2193';
				bDl.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
					ev.stopPropagation();
					Gallery.getBlobUrl(e.id).then(function(u) {
						if (!u) return;
						var a = document.createElement('a');
						a.href = u;
						a.download = (e.id || 'image') + '.png';
						document.body.appendChild(a);
						a.click();
						a.remove();
						setTimeout(function() {
							URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
						}, 1500);
					});
				});
				var bDel = document.createElement('button');
				bDel.className = 'gact';
				bDel.title = 'Delete';
				bDel.textContent = '\u2715';
				bDel.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
					ev.stopPropagation();
					Gallery.remove(e.id).then(renderGallery);
				});
				acts.appendChild(bFav);
				acts.appendChild(bDl);
				acts.appendChild(bDel);
				var cap = document.createElement('div');
				cap.className = 'gcap';
				cap.textContent = e.prompt || '(no prompt)';
				tile.appendChild(fav);
				tile.appendChild(acts);
				tile.appendChild(cap);
				grid.appendChild(tile);
			});
		}).catch(function() {});
	}

	function openLightbox(url, prompt) {
		byId('lightboxImg').src = url;
		byId('lightboxCap').textContent = prompt || '';
		_lightboxPrompt = prompt || '';
		byId('lightbox').style.display = 'flex';
	}
	var _lightboxPrompt = '';

	function becomeFromImage() {
		var desc = scrubImagePrompt(String(_lightboxPrompt || '')).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 400);
		if (desc.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/ig, '').length < 3) {
			toast('No description to step into', 'info');
			return;
		}
		var name = (typeof window.prompt === 'function') ? window.prompt('Name this character — she\u2019ll become who the image shows:', '') : '';
		if (name === null) return;
		name = String(name || '').trim().slice(0, 60) || 'this character';
		var instruction = 'You are ' + name + ', the character shown in this image: ' + desc + '. Speak and act fully as them - their look, manner, and bearing - and stay in character.';
		useCharacter({
			name: name,
			instruction: instruction,
			avatar: ''
		}, 'chat');
		closeLightbox();
		try {
			closeGallery();
		} catch (e) {}
		toast('You are now ' + name, 'info');
	}

	function closeLightbox() {
		var lb = byId('lightbox');
		if (lb) {
			lb.style.display = 'none';
			byId('lightboxImg').src = '';
		}
	}

	function wireGallery() {
		byId('openGallery').addEventListener('click', openGallery);
		byId('galleryClose').addEventListener('click', closeGallery);
		byId('galleryBg').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			if (e.target === byId('galleryBg')) closeGallery();
		});
		byId('gallerySort').addEventListener('change', function() {
			gallerySort = this.value;
			renderGallery();
		});
		byId('galleryFavOnly').addEventListener('change', function() {
			galleryFavOnly = this.checked;
			renderGallery();
		});
		byId('galleryClear').addEventListener('click', function() {
			if (window.confirm('Delete all saved images? This cannot be undone.')) Gallery.clear().then(renderGallery).then(function() {
				toast('Gallery cleared');
			});
		});
		byId('lightboxClose').addEventListener('click', closeLightbox);
		byId('lightbox').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			if (e.target === byId('lightbox')) closeLightbox();
		});
		(function() {
			var bb = byId('lightboxBecome');
			if (bb) bb.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
				e.stopPropagation();
				becomeFromImage();
			});
		})();
	}
	var _communityMounted = false;

	function openCommunity() {
		byId('communityBg').classList.add('open');
		var host = byId('communityHost'),
			off = byId('communityOff');
		var wc = (window.weld && window.weld.comments);
		if (wc && (wc.inline || wc.open)) {
			off.style.display = 'none';
			host.style.display = '';
			if (!_communityMounted) {
				_communityMounted = true;
				try {
					var app = (typeof window.generatorName === 'string' && window.generatorName) || 'chloe-solo';
					if (wc.inline) wc.inline('#communityHost', {
						key: 'chloe-solo-community',
						app: app
					});
					else wc.open({
						key: 'chloe-solo-community',
						app: app
					});
				} catch (e) {
					_communityMounted = false;
					host.style.display = 'none';
					off.style.display = 'block';
				}
			}
		} else {
			host.style.display = 'none';
			off.style.display = 'block';
		}
	}

	function closeCommunity() {
		byId('communityBg').classList.remove('open');
	}

	function wireCommunity() {
		byId('openCommunity').addEventListener('click', openCommunity);
		byId('communityClose').addEventListener('click', closeCommunity);
		byId('communityBg').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			if (e.target === byId('communityBg')) closeCommunity();
		});
	}

	function refreshMemStats() {
		var keys = 0,
			bytes = 0;
		try {
			for (var i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
				var key = localStorage.key(i);
				if (key && key.indexOf(NS) === 0) {
					keys++;
					bytes += (localStorage.getItem(key) || '').length;
				}
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		var msgs = queue.length;
		setText('memStats', msgs + ' message' + (msgs === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' · ' + keys + ' stored keys · ~' + Math.round(bytes / 1024) + ' KB in this browser');
	}

	function wireSettings() {
		byId('openSettings').addEventListener('click', openSettings);
		byId('closeSettings').addEventListener('click', closeSettings);
		var oc = byId('openCharacters');
		if (oc) oc.addEventListener('click', openCharacters);
		var cnb = byId('charNewBtn');
		if (cnb) cnb.addEventListener('click', function() {
			openCharEditor(null);
		});
		var scn = byId('scNewBtn');
		if (scn) scn.addEventListener('click', function() {
			openShortcutEditor(null);
		});
		var lib2 = byId('libExportBtn');
		if (lib2) lib2.addEventListener('click', exportLibraryPack);
		var lpf = byId('libPackFile'),
			lib3 = byId('libImportBtn');
		if (lib3 && lpf) lib3.addEventListener('click', function() {
			lpf.click();
		});
		if (lpf) lpf.addEventListener('change', function() {
			var f = lpf.files && lpf.files[0];
			if (!f) return;
			var fr = new FileReader();
			fr.onload = function() {
				importLibraryPack(String(fr.result || ''));
				lpf.value = '';
			};
			fr.readAsText(f);
		});
		var cc = byId('closeCharacters');
		if (cc) cc.addEventListener('click', closeCharacters);
		var cbg = byId('charactersBg');
		if (cbg) cbg.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			if (e.target === cbg) closeCharacters();
		});
		var os2 = byId('openSaves');
		if (os2) os2.addEventListener('click', openSaves);
		var cs2 = byId('closeSaves');
		if (cs2) cs2.addEventListener('click', closeSaves);
		var sbg = byId('savesBg');
		if (sbg) sbg.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			if (e.target === sbg) closeSaves();
		});
		var sn = byId('saveNowBtn');
		if (sn) sn.addEventListener('click', function() {
			var lbl = (byId('saveLabel').value || '').trim();
			setText('saveNote', 'saving\u2026');
			saveSlot('manual', lbl).then(function() {
				byId('saveLabel').value = '';
				setText('saveNote', 'saved.');
				renderSaves();
				toast('Session saved', 'success');
			}).catch(function() {
				setText('saveNote', 'could not save - storage may be full.');
			});
		});
		var asc = byId('autoSaveChk');
		if (asc) asc.addEventListener('change', function() {
			state.autoSave = asc.checked;
			persistState();
			startAutoSave();
		});
		var asm = byId('autoSaveMins');
		if (asm) asm.addEventListener('change', function() {
