					var key = localStorage.key(i);
					if (key && key.indexOf(NS) === 0) dump[key] = localStorage.getItem(key);
				}
			} catch (e) {}
			Promise.all(BACKUP_DBS.map(dumpDb)).then(function(dbs) {
				var idb = dbs.filter(Boolean);
				var blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify({
					format: 'chloe-solo',
					version: SOLO_VERSION,
					data: dump,
					idb: idb
				})], {
					type: 'application/json'
				});
				var a = document.createElement('a');
				a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
				a.download = 'chloe-solo-backup.json';
				a.click();
				setText('dataNote', 'full backup exported - memory, images, saves & characters.');
				toast('Full backup exported', 'success');
			}, function() {
				setText('dataNote', 'export hit a snag - try again.');
			});
		});
		byId('importBtn').addEventListener('click', function() {

		});
		byId('importFile').addEventListener('change', function() {
			var f = this.files && this.files[0];
			if (!f) return;
			setText('dataNote', 'importing\u2026');
			var r = new FileReader();
			r.onload = function() {
				try {
					var obj = JSON.parse(r.result);
					var d = obj && obj.data;
					if (!d) throw 0;
					Object.keys(d).forEach(function(key) {
						try {
							localStorage.setItem(key, d[key]);
						} catch (e) {}
					});
					var idb = (obj && obj.idb) || [];
					Promise.all(idb.map(restoreDb)).then(function() {
						state = loadState();
						queue = loadQueue();
						location.reload();
					}, function() {
						state = loadState();
						queue = loadQueue();
						location.reload();
					});
				} catch (e) {
					setText('dataNote', 'that did not look like a Chloe Solo export.');
				}
			};
			r.readAsText(f);
		});
	}

	function emptyTemplate() {
		var d = document.createElement('div');
		d.className = 'empty';
		d.id = 'emptyState';
		d.innerHTML = '<h2>Say hello.</h2><div>Characters remember what you tell them and think before they speak - open <b>Thoughts</b> to watch.</div>';
		return d;
	}

	function wireMind() {
		streamEl = byId('stream');

			byId('mind').classList.add('open');
			state.mindOpen = true;
			persistState();
		}

			byId('mind').classList.remove('open');
			state.mindOpen = false;
			persistState();
		}
		byId('openMind').addEventListener('click', openMind);
		byId('mindTab').addEventListener('click', openMind);
		byId('closeMind').addEventListener('click', closeMindFn);
	}

	function wireComposer() {
		var inp = byId('input'),
			btn = byId('sendBtn');

		function grow() {
			inp.style.height = 'auto';
			inp.style.height = Math.min(160, inp.scrollHeight) + 'px';
			btn.disabled = !inp.value.trim();
		}
		inp.addEventListener('input', grow);
		inp.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
			if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
				e.preventDefault();
				sendUserMessage();
			}
		});
		btn.addEventListener('click', sendUserMessage);
	}
	var ACCENT_PRESETS = [{
		name: 'Indigo',
		hex: '#5B62E0'
	}, {

		hex: '#8A5BD0'
	}, {

		hex: '#C2417A'
	}, {
		name: 'Coral',
		hex: '#C0562B'
	}, {
		name: 'Amber',
		hex: '#B8902A'
	}, {
		name: 'Teal',
		hex: '#2E9E7B'
	}, {
		name: 'Ocean',
		hex: '#2E7DD1'
	}, {
		name: 'Forest',
		hex: '#5C8A3A'
	}, {
		name: 'Slate',
		hex: '#5A6478'
	}];

	function clampN(n, a, b) {
		return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
	}

	function hexToHsl(hex) {
		hex = String(hex || '').replace('#', '');
		if (hex.length === 3) hex = hex.replace(/(.)/g, '$1$1');
		var r = parseInt(hex.slice(0, 2), 16) / 255,
			g = parseInt(hex.slice(2, 4), 16) / 255,
			b = parseInt(hex.slice(4, 6), 16) / 255;
		var mx = Math.max(r, g, b),
			mn = Math.min(r, g, b),
			hh = 0,
			s = 0,
			l = (mx + mn) / 2;
		if (mx !== mn) {
			var d = mx - mn;
			s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - mx - mn) : d / (mx + mn);
			hh = mx === r ? (g - b) / d + (g < b ? 6 : 0) : mx === g ? (b - r) / d + 2 : (r - g) / d + 4;
			hh /= 6;
		}
		return {
			h: hh * 360,
			s: s * 100,
			l: l * 100
		};
	}

	function hslToHex(h, s, l) {
		h = (h % 360 + 360) % 360;
		s = clampN(s, 0, 100) / 100;
		l = clampN(l, 0, 100) / 100;
		var c = (1 - Math.abs(2 * l - 1)) * s,
			x = c * (1 - Math.abs((h / 60) % 2 - 1)),
			m = l - c / 2,
			r = 0,
			g = 0,
			b = 0;
		if (h < 60) {
			r = c;
			g = x;
		} else if (h < 120) {
			r = x;
			g = c;
		} else if (h < 180) {
			g = c;
			b = x;
		} else if (h < 240) {
			g = x;
			b = c;
		} else if (h < 300) {
			r = x;
			b = c;
		} else {
			r = c;
			b = x;
		}

		function hx(v) {
			return ('0' + Math.round((v + m) * 255).toString(16)).slice(-2);
		}
		return '#' + hx(r) + hx(g) + hx(b);
	}

	function effectiveDark() {
		if (state.theme === 'dark') return true;
		if (state.theme === 'light') return false;
		try {
			return !!(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);
		} catch (e) {
			return false;
		}
	}

	function applyThemeMode() {
		var root = document.documentElement;
		if (state.theme === 'light' || state.theme === 'dark') root.setAttribute('data-theme', state.theme);
		else root.removeAttribute('data-theme');
	}

	function applyAccent() {
		var root = document.documentElement,
			props = ['--accent', '--accent-ink', '--accent-soft', '--accent-line'];
		if (!state.accent) {
			props.forEach(function(p) {
				root.style.removeProperty(p);
			});
			return;
		}
		var c = hexToHsl(state.accent),
			dark = effectiveDark();
		if (dark) {
			root.style.setProperty('--accent', hslToHex(c.h, c.s, clampN(c.l, 58, 70)));
			root.style.setProperty('--accent-ink', hslToHex(c.h, c.s, 77));
			root.style.setProperty('--accent-soft', hslToHex(c.h, clampN(c.s, 18, 45), 16));
			root.style.setProperty('--accent-line', hslToHex(c.h, clampN(c.s, 18, 50), 34));
		} else {
			root.style.setProperty('--accent', hslToHex(c.h, c.s, clampN(c.l, 46, 60)));
			root.style.setProperty('--accent-ink', hslToHex(c.h, c.s, clampN(c.l - 14, 26, 46)));
			root.style.setProperty('--accent-soft', hslToHex(c.h, clampN(c.s, 40, 82), 94));
			root.style.setProperty('--accent-line', hslToHex(c.h, clampN(c.s, 30, 72), 84));
		}
	}

	function applyAppearance() {
		applyThemeMode();
		applyAccent();
	}

	function renderAppearance() {
		var seg = byId('themeSeg');
		if (seg) Array.prototype.forEach.call(seg.querySelectorAll('.segbtn'), function(b) {
			b.classList.toggle('on', (state.theme || 'system') === b.getAttribute('data-mode'));
			if (!b.__w) {
				b.__w = 1;
				b.addEventListener('click', function() {
					state.theme = b.getAttribute('data-mode');
					persistState();
					applyAppearance();
					renderAppearance();
					recolorMessages();
				});
			}
		});
		var sw = byId('accentSwatches');
		if (sw) {
			sw.innerHTML = '';
			ACCENT_PRESETS.forEach(function(p) {
				var b = document.createElement('button');
				b.className = 'swatch' + (((state.accent || '#5B62E0').toLowerCase() === p.hex.toLowerCase()) ? ' on' : '');
				b.style.background = p.hex;
				b.title = p.name;
				b.addEventListener('click', function() {
					state.accent = p.hex;
					persistState();
					applyAppearance();
					renderAppearance();
				});
				sw.appendChild(b);
			});
		}
		var cc = byId('accentCustom');
		if (cc) {
			cc.value = state.accent || '#5B62E0';
			if (!cc.__w) {
				cc.__w = 1;
				cc.addEventListener('input', function() {
					state.accent = cc.value;
					persistState();
					applyAppearance();
				});
				cc.addEventListener('change', function() {
					renderAppearance();
				});
			}
		}
		var note = byId('accentNote');
		if (note) note.textContent = state.accent ? ('using ' + state.accent.toUpperCase()) : 'default';
		var rb = byId('appearanceReset');
		if (rb && !rb.__w) {
			rb.__w = 1;
			rb.addEventListener('click', function() {
				state.theme = 'system';
				state.accent = null;
				persistState();
				applyAppearance();
				renderAppearance();
				recolorMessages();
				toast('Appearance reset', 'success');
			});
		}
	}

	function colorFor(id) {
		if (id === BOT_ID) return state.botColor || null;
		var c = (state.cast || []).filter(function(x) {
			return x.id === id;
		})[0];
		return (c && c.color) || castColor(id);
	}

	function recolorMessages() {
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#thread .msg.her[data-cid]'), function(w) {
			var color = colorFor(w.getAttribute('data-cid'));
			if (color || isGroup()) {
				w.classList.add('hascolor');
				w.style.setProperty('--speaker', color || 'var(--accent-ink)');
				var who = w.querySelector('.who');
				if (who) who.style.color = color || '';
			} else {
				w.classList.remove('hascolor');
				w.style.removeProperty('--speaker');
				var who2 = w.querySelector('.who');
				if (who2) who2.style.color = '';
			}
		});
	}

	function boot() {
		applyAppearance();
		try {
			if (window.matchMedia) {
				var __mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
				var __onScheme = function() {
					if ((state.theme || 'system') === 'system') {
						applyAccent();
						recolorMessages();
					}
				};
				__mq.addEventListener ? __mq.addEventListener('change', __onScheme) : (__mq.addListener && __mq.addListener(__onScheme));
			}
		} catch (e) {}
		threadCol = byId('threadCol');
		emptyState = byId('emptyState');
		streamEl = byId('stream');
		threadScroller = makeSticky(byId('thread'), byId('jumpThread'));
		mindScroller = makeSticky(byId('stream'), byId('jumpMind'));
		var tr = document.createElement('div');
		tr.className = 'typing';
		tr.id = 'typingRow';
		tr.innerHTML = '<i></i><i></i><i></i>';
		threadCol.appendChild(tr);
		installMockIfNeeded();
		installPainter();
		wrapAiForStreaming();
		wireTokenizer();
		requestPersistentStorage();
		clearStaleLocks();
		updateCharLabel();
		wireComposer();
		wireMind();
		wireSettings();
		wireToolbar();
		wireGallery();
		wireCommunity();
		if (state.mindOpen == null ? window.innerWidth > 880 : state.mindOpen) byId('mind').classList.add('open');
		if (queue.length) {
			if (emptyState && emptyState.parentNode) {
				emptyState.parentNode.removeChild(emptyState);
				emptyState = null;
			}
			queue.forEach(function(m) {
				var isBot = !!(m.author && m.author.bot);
				var w = renderMessage(isBot ? 'her' : 'you', speakerNameFor(m), m.content, {
					mid: m.id
				});
				if (w && isBot) {
					var cid = m.author.id;
					w.setAttribute('data-cid', cid);
					if (cid !== BOT_ID || isGroup() || state.botColor) styleSpeaker(w, cid, avatarForCid(cid));
				}
			});
			try {
				Gallery.init().then(function() {
					queue.forEach(function(m) {
						if (!m.image) return;
						Gallery.getBlobUrl(m.image).then(function(url) {
							if (!url) return;
							var wrap = wrapFor(m);
							if (wrap && !wrap.querySelector('img.avatar')) {
								var im = document.createElement('img');
								im.className = 'avatar';
								im.src = url;
								wrap.appendChild(im);
							}
						}, function() {});
					});
				}, function() {});
			} catch (e) {}
		}
		ensureUserPartition().then(buildEngine).then(function() {
			wrapAiForStreaming();
			refreshClock();
			renderRoster();
			startAutoSave();
			autoMatchLang();
			renderShortcuts();
		});
		setInterval(refreshClock, 60000);
		setInterval(reactionIdleTick, 12000);
	}
	if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
	else boot();
	window.__solo = {
		call: call,
		byId: byId,
		setText: setText,
		log: log,
		refresh: refresh,
		selChannel: selChannel,
		addToScene: addToScene,
		importCharacter: importCharacter
	};
})(); < /script>
